# Reading Tracker Backend Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the Go backend from the agent spec (tasks T8–T23): the `api` Lambda (Echo behind a Function URL), the `notion-writer` worker Lambda (SQS FIFO), and a `cmd/local` runner, with tests against DynamoDB Local and a fake Notion.

**Architecture:** One Go module in `backend/`. `internal/` packages each own one concern: `crypto` (sealing, HMAC, PKCE), `config` (env + `/v1/config` defaults), `store` (DynamoDB single table), `notion` (thin HTTP client + `notiontest` fake), `queue` (FIFO messages, dedup IDs, SQS/local/recorder senders), `setup` (template resolution, DB creation, resync), `syncsvc` (push validation + aggregation), `httpapi` (Echo app), `worker` (SQS handler), and `app` (production wiring). Integration tests run against DynamoDB Local in Podman. All Notion traffic in tests goes to an in-memory fake.

**Tech Stack:** Go toolchain 1.26 (module targets ≥ 1.23), Echo v4.16, aws-lambda-go 1.55, aws-lambda-go-api-proxy 0.16 (`echoadapter.NewV2`), aws-sdk-go-v2 (dynamodb 1.70, sqs 1.52, attributevalue), oklog/ulid/v2, DynamoDB Local (`docker.io/amazon/dynamodb-local`) under Podman.

**Spec:** [`docs/reading-tracker-agent-spec.md`](../../reading-tracker-agent-spec.md) (contracts, keys, schema, constraints). Rationale is in [`docs/reading-tracker-onboarding.md`](../../reading-tracker-onboarding.md).

**Scope:** This is plan 1 of 3. The spec covers three independent subsystems. This plan covers the backend. The extension (T25–T31) and Terraform (T1–T7) each get their own plan after this one lands. T24 (Notion template) and T32 (cloud smoke test) are manual. Task 17 here adds a local end-to-end test that stands in for T32 until the infrastructure exists.

## Global Constraints

- Go module at `backend/`, module path `reading-tracker/backend`. The real path is still open (🔶 spec §7); rename later with `go mod edit -module` plus an import rewrite.
- Libraries: Echo **v4** (`github.com/labstack/echo/v4`); `github.com/awslabs/aws-lambda-go-api-proxy/echo` `NewV2(e).ProxyWithContext`; `aws-sdk-go-v2`; `github.com/oklog/ulid/v2`.
- `Notion-Version: 2025-09-03` on every Notion request. Use `/v1/data_sources/*` and `parent.type = data_source_id` (C10). Config refuses any other version.
- DynamoDB: one table, keys `pk`/`sk` (S), no GSIs, PROVISIONED 25/25 (C11). Every read of `OAUTHSTATE`/`AUTHCODE` checks `ttl > now` (C12).
- Push: SEG put and ART/DAY increments go in **one** `TransactWriteItems` conditioned on `attribute_not_exists(pk)` (C2). At most 50 segments per push (C25).
- Never log tokens, auth codes, query strings, request bodies, or Notion token responses (C13). Request logs record method, **path only**, status and latency.
- Server never writes `Tags`, `Status`, `Rating`, `Notes`, not even on create (C1). Notion properties are addressed by **stored property ID** (C9).
- Only the worker writes pages and refreshes tokens (C3, C8). A refresh persists **both** new tokens, guarded by `token_version` (C7). The worker writes absolute values read from DynamoDB (C6).
- Every queue message has `MessageGroupId = installation_id` (purge without installation: `user#<uid>`) (C4). On the first failure, the worker fails that message and every later one in the batch (C21). Notion calls are spaced ≥ 350 ms apart and honour `Retry-After` (C22).
- AES-256-GCM with a fresh random 12-byte nonce per seal and AAD = `bot_id` (C18). Device tokens are stored only as `hex(HMAC-SHA256(pepper, token))` (C20).
- Binaries: `CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux GOARCH=arm64 go build -tags lambda.norpc`, output named `bootstrap` (C16). `import _ "time/tzdata"` (C15, done once in `internal/config`).
- 🔶 §7 values are never constants in code. Tracking thresholds come from `config.PublicConfig` (embedded defaults + `TRACKING_CONFIG_JSON`). API base, extension IDs and debounce bucket come from env.
- Containers use **Podman** (`podman run …`), never Docker. JS tooling (later plans) uses **pnpm**.

## Interpretations of the spec (deliberate; flag in review if you disagree)

1. **Template validation checks server-owned properties only.** These are the 12 + 4 logical keys in §3.3. User-owned columns (Tags/Status/Rating/Notes) are never written, so a user deleting `Tags` must not break sync. `POST /v1/setup` still creates them.
2. **Over-long `title`/`author`/`site_name` are truncated** (rune-safe), not rejected. Rejecting would permanently drop that reading time, because the client deletes rejected segments. An invalid `canonical_url`, `source` or `word_count` still invalidates the article, and its segments are rejected as `invalid_article`.
3. **`duplicate` segments also enqueue their article/day messages.** Without this, a push whose enqueue failed after commit would never reach Notion: the client's retry only sees duplicates. Extra messages are harmless because of bucket dedup and absolute writes (C6).
4. **An adopted page (found by URL or date query) also gets a metrics PATCH**, so a page created by a timed-out earlier attempt doesn't keep stale numbers.
5. **Purge is defensive.** It ignores users not marked `deleted_at`. It skips an `INST#` partition whose `META.user_id` now belongs to someone else. It deletes `NUSER#` only if that item still points at the purged user.
6. **Queue sends use `SendMessageBatch`** (≤ 10 per call). Semantics match `SendMessage`, with fewer requests (resync can enqueue hundreds).
7. **A Notion 401 inside the API** (search/setup) returns `409 conflict` ("reconnect"). The API never refreshes (C8).
8. **Reconnecting without a template keeps the existing databases.** If the installation already has DB IDs and props, it goes back to `active` and resyncs (scenario 11).
9. Segment rejection reasons are `invalid_id`, `invalid_session_id`, `invalid_seq`, `invalid_timestamp`, `active_seconds_out_of_range`, `ended_before_started`, `active_seconds_exceed_window`, `max_progress_out_of_range`, `started_at_out_of_range`, `unknown_article` and `invalid_article`.

## Review Focus

These are inputs and failure modes the spec implies but no spec AC tests. Each line names the task whose tests pin it.

1. **Two devices push the same article at the same moment** (scenario 4). DynamoDB cancels one transaction with `TransactionConflict`. The server should retry it internally and report `accepted`, not `retry` or 500. → Task 4 `TestPushRetriesTransactionConflict`.
2. **Enqueue fails after the segments committed.** The client retries and gets `duplicate`, and the article/day messages must still be sent. → Task 12 `TestPushRetryAfterEnqueueFailureStillEnqueues`.
3. **The worker re-enqueues inside the same debounce bucket** (404/archived page). SQS dedup must not swallow the re-enqueue. → Task 6 `TestRequeueDedupIDNeverCollides`, Task 14 `TestArticleArchivedPageIsRecreated`.
4. **Notion field limits:** canonical URL > 2000 chars, and a site name containing commas or over 100 chars. The page must still be created rather than acked as a poison message. → Task 14 `TestArticleLongURLAndCommaSiteStillCreate`.
5. **Reconnect after `revoked` when the user skips the template.** Existing databases are reused, status returns to `active` and resync runs. → Task 9 `TestOAuthReconnectWithoutTemplateKeepsDatabases`.

`backend/internal/crypto/keyring_test.go`:

```go
package crypto

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func testKey(b byte) string { return base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bytes.Repeat([]byte{b}, 32)) }

func mustKeyring(t *testing.T, spec string) *Keyring {
	t.Helper()
	k, err := ParseKeyring(spec)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ParseKeyring: %v", err)
	}
	return k
}

func TestSealOpenRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	k := mustKeyring(t, "v1:"+testKey(1))
	sealed, err := k.Seal([]byte("secret_abc"), []byte("bot-1"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(sealed, "v1:") || strings.Contains(sealed, "secret_abc") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected sealed form %q", sealed)
	}
	got, err := k.Open(sealed, []byte("bot-1"))
	if err != nil || string(got) != "secret_abc" {
		t.Fatalf("Open = %q, %v", got, err)
	}
}

func TestOpenWithDifferentAADFails(t *testing.T) {
	k := mustKeyring(t, "v1:"+testKey(1))
	sealed, _ := k.Seal([]byte("secret"), []byte("bot-1"))
	if _, err := k.Open(sealed, []byte("bot-2")); !errors.Is(err, ErrOpen) {
		t.Fatalf("want ErrOpen, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestOldKeyOpensAfterRotation(t *testing.T) {
	old := mustKeyring(t, "v0:"+testKey(0))
	sealed, _ := old.Seal([]byte("secret"), []byte("bot-1"))

	rotated := mustKeyring(t, "v1:"+testKey(1)+",v0:"+testKey(0))
	got, err := rotated.Open(sealed, []byte("bot-1"))
	if err != nil || string(got) != "secret" {
		t.Fatalf("Open after rotation = %q, %v", got, err)
	}
	resealed, _ := rotated.Seal([]byte("secret"), []byte("bot-1"))
	if !strings.HasPrefix(resealed, "v1:") || rotated.CurrentVersion() != "v1" {
		t.Fatalf("new seals must use the first key, got %q", resealed)
	}
}

func TestTamperedCiphertextFails(t *testing.T) {
	k := mustKeyring(t, "v1:"+testKey(1))
	sealed, _ := k.Seal([]byte("secret"), []byte("bot-1"))
	raw, _ := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(strings.TrimPrefix(sealed, "v1:"))
	raw[len(raw)-1] ^= 0x01
	tampered := "v1:" + base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(raw)
	if _, err := k.Open(tampered, []byte("bot-1")); !errors.Is(err, ErrOpen) {
		t.Fatalf("want ErrOpen, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestSealUsesFreshNonce(t *testing.T) {
	k := mustKeyring(t, "v1:"+testKey(1))
	a, _ := k.Seal([]byte("same"), []byte("bot-1"))
	b, _ := k.Seal([]byte("same"), []byte("bot-1"))
	if a == b {
		t.Fatal("two seals of the same plaintext must differ (C18 nonce reuse)")
	}
}

func TestParseKeyringRejectsBadInput(t *testing.T) {
	short := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte("too-short"))
	for _, spec := range []string{"", "v1", "v1:not*base64", "v1:" + short, "v1:" + testKey(1) + ",v1:" + testKey(2)} {
		if _, err := ParseKeyring(spec); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("ParseKeyring(%q) succeeded, want error", spec)
		}
	}
}

func TestOpenUnknownVersionFails(t *testing.T) {
	k := mustKeyring(t, "v1:"+testKey(1))
	if _, err := k.Open("v9:AAAA", []byte("bot-1")); !errors.Is(err, ErrOpen) {
		t.Fatalf("want ErrOpen, got %v", err)
	}
}
```

```go
package crypto

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// RFC 7636 Appendix B test vector.
const (
	rfcVerifier  = "dBjftJeZ4CVP-mB92K27uhbUJU1p1r_wW1gFWFOEjXk"
	rfcChallenge = "E9Melhoa2OwvFrEMTJguCHaoeK1t8URWbuGJSstw-cM"
)

func TestS256ChallengeMatchesRFC(t *testing.T) {
	if got := S256Challenge(rfcVerifier); got != rfcChallenge {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", got)
	}
	if !ValidChallenge(rfcChallenge) {
		t.Fatal("RFC challenge should be valid")
	}
}

func TestVerifyPKCE(t *testing.T) {
	if !VerifyPKCE(rfcVerifier, rfcChallenge) {
		t.Fatal("RFC pair must verify")
	}
	if VerifyPKCE(strings.Repeat("a", 64), rfcChallenge) {
		t.Fatal("wrong verifier must fail")
	}
	if VerifyPKCE("short", S256Challenge("short")) {
		t.Fatal("verifier shorter than 43 chars must fail")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the crypto package**

`backend/internal/crypto/random.go`:

```go
// Package crypto holds the app-level primitives from spec D7/C18/C20:
// versioned AES-256-GCM sealing, device-token HMAC and PKCE (RFC 7636).
package crypto

import (
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/base64"
)

// RandomB64URL returns n cryptographically random bytes as unpadded base64url.
func RandomB64URL(n int) (string, error) {
	b := make([]byte, n)
	if _, err := rand.Read(b); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b), nil
}

func isB64URLByte(c byte) bool {
	return c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z' || c >= 'a' && c <= 'z' || c >= '0' && c <= '9' || c == '-' || c == '_'
}

func isB64URL(s string) bool {
	for i := 0; i < len(s); i++ {
		if !isB64URLByte(s[i]) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}
```

`backend/internal/crypto/keyring.go`:

```go
package crypto

import (
	"crypto/aes"
	"crypto/cipher"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

// ErrOpen hides why a sealed value failed to open (unknown key, bad AAD, tampering).
var ErrOpen = errors.New("crypto: cannot open sealed value")

// Keyring seals with the first (current) key and opens with any listed key, so
// keys rotate by prepending a new version (spec D7, scenario 19).
type Keyring struct {
	current string
	aeads   map[string]cipher.AEAD
}

// ParseKeyring parses TOKEN_ENC_KEYS: "v1:<b64 32B>[,v0:<b64 32B>…]", first = current.
func ParseKeyring(spec string) (*Keyring, error) {
	k := &Keyring{aeads: map[string]cipher.AEAD{}}
	for i, part := range strings.Split(spec, ",") {
		ver, b64, ok := strings.Cut(strings.TrimSpace(part), ":")
		if !ok || ver == "" {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("TOKEN_ENC_KEYS entry %d: want <version>:<base64 key>", i)
		}
		if _, dup := k.aeads[ver]; dup {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("TOKEN_ENC_KEYS: duplicate version %q", ver)
		}
		key, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(b64)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("TOKEN_ENC_KEYS %s: %w", ver, err)
		}
		if len(key) != 32 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("TOKEN_ENC_KEYS %s: key is %d bytes, want 32", ver, len(key))
		}
		block, err := aes.NewCipher(key)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		aead, err := cipher.NewGCM(block)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		k.aeads[ver] = aead
		if i == 0 {
			k.current = ver
		}
	}
	return k, nil
}

// CurrentVersion is the key version new seals use.
func (k *Keyring) CurrentVersion() string { return k.current }

// Seal encrypts plain with a fresh 12-byte nonce (C18) and returns "<ver>:<b64(nonce‖ct)>".
func (k *Keyring) Seal(plain, aad []byte) (string, error) {
	aead := k.aeads[k.current]
	nonce := make([]byte, aead.NonceSize())
	if _, err := rand.Read(nonce); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	out := aead.Seal(nonce, nonce, plain, aad)
	return k.current + ":" + base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(out), nil
}

// Open reverses Seal. aad must match the value used when sealing (bot_id).
func (k *Keyring) Open(sealed string, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	ver, b64, ok := strings.Cut(sealed, ":")
	aead, known := k.aeads[ver]
	if !ok || !known {
		return nil, ErrOpen
	}
	raw, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(b64)
	if err != nil || len(raw) < aead.NonceSize()+aead.Overhead() {
		return nil, ErrOpen
	}
	plain, err := aead.Open(nil, raw[:aead.NonceSize()], raw[aead.NonceSize():], aad)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, ErrOpen
	}
	return plain, nil
}
```

`backend/internal/crypto/devicetoken.go`:

```go
package crypto

import (
	"crypto/hmac"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"strings"
)

// DeviceTokenPrefix marks extension bearer tokens: "rt_" + 43 base64url chars.
const DeviceTokenPrefix = "rt_"

// NewDeviceToken returns "rt_" + base64url(32 random bytes) (spec §2.3).
func NewDeviceToken() (string, error) {
	s, err := RandomB64URL(32)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return DeviceTokenPrefix + s, nil
}

// ValidDeviceTokenFormat rejects malformed bearer tokens before any DynamoDB read.
func ValidDeviceTokenFormat(tok string) bool {
	rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(tok, DeviceTokenPrefix)
	return ok && len(rest) == 43 && isB64URL(rest)
}

// TokenHMAC is the only form a device token is stored in (C20).
func TokenHMAC(pepper []byte, token string) string {
	m := hmac.New(sha256.New, pepper)
	m.Write([]byte(token))
	return hex.EncodeToString(m.Sum(nil))
}
```

`backend/internal/crypto/pkce.go`:

```go
package crypto

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/subtle"
	"encoding/base64"
)

// S256Challenge returns base64url(SHA-256(verifier)) without padding (RFC 7636 §4.2).
func S256Challenge(verifier string) string {
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(verifier))
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// ValidVerifier: 43–128 chars of [A-Za-z0-9-._~] (RFC 7636 §4.1).
func ValidVerifier(v string) bool {
	if len(v) < 43 || len(v) > 128 {
		return false
	}
	for i := 0; i < len(v); i++ {
		if c := v[i]; !isB64URLByte(c) && c != '.' && c != '~' {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// ValidChallenge: an S256 challenge is exactly 43 base64url chars.
func ValidChallenge(c string) bool { return len(c) == 43 && isB64URL(c) }

// VerifyPKCE checks the verifier against the stored challenge in constant time.
func VerifyPKCE(verifier, challenge string) bool {
	if !ValidVerifier(verifier) {
		return false
	}
	return subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(S256Challenge(verifier)), []byte(challenge)) == 1
}
```

```json
    "complete_time_ratio": 0.3,

  },
```

`backend/internal/config/public.go`:

```go
package config

import (
	_ "embed"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

//go:embed default_config.json
var defaultPublicJSON []byte

// Tracking holds the 🔶 thresholds shared by extension and worker (spec §2.10, §7).
type Tracking struct {
	IdleSeconds           int     `json:"idle_seconds"`
	SegmentSeconds        int     `json:"segment_seconds"`
	MinCreateSeconds      int     `json:"min_create_seconds"`
	CompleteProgress      float64 `json:"complete_progress"`
	CompleteTimeRatio     float64 `json:"complete_time_ratio"`
	WordsPerMinute        int     `json:"words_per_minute"`
	SyncIntervalMinutes   int     `json:"sync_interval_minutes"`
	SyncThresholdSegments int     `json:"sync_threshold_segments"`
}

// PublicConfig is the GET /v1/config body.
type PublicConfig struct {
	Version          int      `json:"version"`
	Tracking         Tracking `json:"tracking"`
	FreediumHosts    []string `json:"freedium_hosts"`
	StripQueryParams []string `json:"strip_query_params"`
}

// LoadPublic returns the embedded defaults with TRACKING_CONFIG_JSON merged on top.
// Unknown keys are rejected so a typo can't silently fall back to a default.
func LoadPublic(override string) (PublicConfig, error) {
	var pc PublicConfig
	if err := json.Unmarshal(defaultPublicJSON, &pc); err != nil {
		return pc, fmt.Errorf("config: embedded defaults: %w", err)
	}
	if strings.TrimSpace(override) != "" {
		dec := json.NewDecoder(strings.NewReader(override))
		dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
		if err := dec.Decode(&pc); err != nil {
			return pc, fmt.Errorf("config: TRACKING_CONFIG_JSON: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return pc, pc.validate()
}

func (pc PublicConfig) validate() error {
	t := pc.Tracking
	if t.IdleSeconds < 1 || t.SegmentSeconds < 1 || t.MinCreateSeconds < 0 || t.WordsPerMinute < 1 ||
		t.SyncIntervalMinutes < 1 || t.SyncThresholdSegments < 1 {
		return fmt.Errorf("config: tracking values must be positive: %+v", t)
	}
	if t.CompleteProgress <= 0 || t.CompleteProgress > 1 || t.CompleteTimeRatio < 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("config: completion thresholds out of range: %+v", t)
	}
	return nil
}
```

`backend/internal/config/config.go`:

```go
// Package config loads and validates the Lambda environment (spec §3.5).
package config

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
	_ "time/tzdata" // C15: provided.al2023 ships no zoneinfo.

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
)

// NotionVersion is pinned (C10); any other NOTION_VERSION fails fast.
const NotionVersion = "2025-09-03"

// Role selects which variables are required.
type Role int

const (
	RoleAPI Role = iota
	RoleWorker
)

// Config is the parsed environment shared by cmd/api, cmd/worker and cmd/local.
type Config struct {
	AppEnv              string
	TableName           string
	QueueURL            string
	NotionClientID      string
	NotionClientSecret  string
	NotionVersion       string
	NotionAPIBase       string // NOTION_API_BASE, default https://api.notion.com (tests/local point it at a fake)
	PublicBaseURL       string // PUBLIC_BASE_URL, may be empty (C14)
	Keys                *crypto.Keyring
	DeviceTokenPepper   []byte
	AllowedExtensionIDs map[string]bool
	DebounceBucket      time.Duration
	Public              PublicConfig
}

// Load reads the environment through getenv and fails on the first invalid value,
// listing every missing variable at once.
func Load(role Role, getenv func(string) string) (*Config, error) {
	var missing []string
	req := func(name string) string {
		v := strings.TrimSpace(getenv(name))
		if v == "" {
			missing = append(missing, name)
		}
		return v
	}
	c := &Config{
		AppEnv:             req("APP_ENV"),
		TableName:          req("TABLE_NAME"),
		QueueURL:           req("QUEUE_URL"),
		NotionClientID:     req("NOTION_CLIENT_ID"),
		NotionClientSecret: req("NOTION_CLIENT_SECRET"),
		NotionVersion:      req("NOTION_VERSION"),
		NotionAPIBase:      strings.TrimRight(strings.TrimSpace(getenv("NOTION_API_BASE")), "/"),
		PublicBaseURL:      strings.TrimRight(strings.TrimSpace(getenv("PUBLIC_BASE_URL")), "/"),
	}
	keys := req("TOKEN_ENC_KEYS")
	var pepper, extIDs, bucket string
	if role == RoleAPI {
		pepper = req("DEVICE_TOKEN_PEPPER")
		extIDs = req("ALLOWED_EXTENSION_IDS")
		bucket = req("DEBOUNCE_BUCKET_SECONDS")
	}
	if len(missing) > 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("config: missing env %s", strings.Join(missing, ", "))
	}
	if c.NotionVersion != NotionVersion {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("config: NOTION_VERSION must be %s, got %q", NotionVersion, c.NotionVersion)
	}
	if c.NotionAPIBase == "" {
		c.NotionAPIBase = "https://api.notion.com"
	}
	var err error
	if c.Keys, err = crypto.ParseKeyring(keys); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("config: %w", err)
	}
	if role == RoleAPI {
		c.DeviceTokenPepper, err = base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(pepper)
		if err != nil || len(c.DeviceTokenPepper) < 32 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("config: DEVICE_TOKEN_PEPPER must be base64 of >= 32 bytes")
		}
		c.AllowedExtensionIDs = map[string]bool{}
		for _, id := range strings.Split(extIDs, ",") {
			if id = strings.TrimSpace(id); id != "" {
				c.AllowedExtensionIDs[id] = true
			}
		}
		if len(c.AllowedExtensionIDs) == 0 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("config: ALLOWED_EXTENSION_IDS has no ids")
		}
		secs, err := strconv.Atoi(bucket)
		if err != nil || secs < 1 || secs > 300 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("config: DEBOUNCE_BUCKET_SECONDS must be 1..300 (C5), got %q", bucket)
		}
		c.DebounceBucket = time.Duration(secs) * time.Second
	}
	if c.Public, err = LoadPublic(getenv("TRACKING_CONFIG_JSON")); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return c, nil
}
```

`backend/internal/store/store.go`:

```go
// Package store is the DynamoDB single-table repository (spec §3.1–§3.2).
package store

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/feature/dynamodb/attributevalue"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
	"github.com/aws/smithy-go"
)

// API is the subset of *dynamodb.Client the store uses; tests wrap it to inject errors.
type API interface {
	GetItem(context.Context, *dynamodb.GetItemInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.GetItemOutput, error)
	PutItem(context.Context, *dynamodb.PutItemInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.PutItemOutput, error)
	UpdateItem(context.Context, *dynamodb.UpdateItemInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.UpdateItemOutput, error)
	DeleteItem(context.Context, *dynamodb.DeleteItemInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.DeleteItemOutput, error)
	Query(context.Context, *dynamodb.QueryInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.QueryOutput, error)
	BatchGetItem(context.Context, *dynamodb.BatchGetItemInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.BatchGetItemOutput, error)
	BatchWriteItem(context.Context, *dynamodb.BatchWriteItemInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.BatchWriteItemOutput, error)
	TransactWriteItems(context.Context, *dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsOutput, error)
}

var (
	ErrNotFound        = errors.New("store: not found")
	ErrConditionFailed = errors.New("store: condition failed")
)

// Store reads and writes the reading table.
type Store struct {
	db    API
	table string
	sleep func(time.Duration)
}

// Option configures a Store.
type Option func(*Store)

// WithSleep replaces time.Sleep in retry back-offs (tests).
func WithSleep(f func(time.Duration)) Option { return func(s *Store) { s.sleep = f } }

func New(db API, table string, opts ...Option) *Store {
	s := &Store{db: db, table: table, sleep: time.Sleep}
	for _, o := range opts {
		o(s)
	}
	return s
}

// Timestamp formats t as RFC 3339 UTC with second precision. Every stored time uses
// it so string comparisons in condition expressions order chronologically.
func Timestamp(t time.Time) string { return t.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339) }

// IsThrottle reports DynamoDB capacity errors that the client should retry later.
func IsThrottle(err error) bool {
	var ae smithy.APIError
	if !errors.As(err, &ae) {
		return false
	}
	switch ae.ErrorCode() {
	case "ProvisionedThroughputExceededException", "ThrottlingException", "RequestLimitExceeded":
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func isConditionFailed(err error) bool {
	var c *types.ConditionalCheckFailedException
	return errors.As(err, &c)
}

func key(pk, sk string) map[string]types.AttributeValue {
	return map[string]types.AttributeValue{"pk": str(pk), "sk": str(sk)}
}

func str(v string) types.AttributeValue { return &types.AttributeValueMemberS{Value: v} }

func numI(v int64) types.AttributeValue {
	return &types.AttributeValueMemberN{Value: strconv.FormatInt(v, 10)}
}

func numF(v float64) types.AttributeValue {
	return &types.AttributeValueMemberN{Value: strconv.FormatFloat(v, 'f', -1, 64)}
}

func boolV(v bool) types.AttributeValue { return &types.AttributeValueMemberBOOL{Value: v} }

func (s *Store) get(ctx context.Context, pk, sk string, out any) error {
	res, err := s.db.GetItem(ctx, &dynamodb.GetItemInput{TableName: &s.table, Key: key(pk, sk)})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(res.Item) == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return attributevalue.UnmarshalMap(res.Item, out)
}

func (s *Store) putItem(ctx context.Context, item any) error {
	av, err := attributevalue.MarshalMap(item)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err = s.db.PutItem(ctx, &dynamodb.PutItemInput{TableName: &s.table, Item: av})
	return err
}

// update runs one UpdateItem; a failed condition becomes ErrConditionFailed.
func (s *Store) update(ctx context.Context, pk, sk, expr, cond string, names map[string]string, values map[string]types.AttributeValue) error {
	in := &dynamodb.UpdateItemInput{TableName: &s.table, Key: key(pk, sk), UpdateExpression: aws.String(expr)}
	if len(names) > 0 {
		in.ExpressionAttributeNames = names
	}
	if len(values) > 0 {
		in.ExpressionAttributeValues = values
	}
	if cond != "" {
		in.ConditionExpression = aws.String(cond)
	}
	_, err := s.db.UpdateItem(ctx, in)
	if isConditionFailed(err) {
		return ErrConditionFailed
	}
	return err
}

// queryAll pages through a query and returns every item.
func (s *Store) queryAll(ctx context.Context, in *dynamodb.QueryInput) ([]map[string]types.AttributeValue, error) {
	in.TableName = &s.table
	var items []map[string]types.AttributeValue
	for {
		res, err := s.db.Query(ctx, in)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		items = append(items, res.Items...)
		if len(res.LastEvaluatedKey) == 0 {
			return items, nil
		}
		in.ExclusiveStartKey = res.LastEvaluatedKey
	}
}

// queryPrefix returns every item in partition pk whose sk starts with prefix.
func (s *Store) queryPrefix(ctx context.Context, pk, prefix string) ([]map[string]types.AttributeValue, error) {
	return s.queryAll(ctx, &dynamodb.QueryInput{
		KeyConditionExpression:    aws.String("pk = :pk AND begins_with(sk, :prefix)"),
		ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{":pk": str(pk), ":prefix": str(prefix)},
	})
}

const maxBatchAttempts = 8

func backoff(attempt int) time.Duration {
	return time.Duration(min(1<<attempt, 64)) * 25 * time.Millisecond
}
```

`backend/internal/store/keys.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
)

// Sort keys and key builders for every item in spec §3.1.
const (
	SKProfile  = "PROFILE"
	SKNUser    = "USER"
	SKDevToken = "DEVTOKEN"
	SKMeta     = "META"
	SKState    = "STATE"
	SKCode     = "CODE"
)

func UserPK(uid string) string          { return "USER#" + uid }
func NUserPK(notionUID string) string   { return "NUSER#" + notionUID }
func DeviceSK(deviceID string) string   { return "DEVICE#" + deviceID }
func DevTokenPK(tokenHMAC string) string { return "DEVTOKEN#" + tokenHMAC }

// InstPK is both the installation partition key and, under USER#<uid>, the pointer sort key.
func InstPK(botID string) string          { return "INST#" + botID }
func ArticleSK(urlHash string) string     { return "ART#" + urlHash }
func SegmentSK(segmentID string) string   { return "SEG#" + segmentID }
func DaySK(date string) string            { return "DAY#" + date }
func OAuthStatePK(state string) string    { return "OAUTHSTATE#" + state }
func AuthCodePK(code string) string       { return "AUTHCODE#" + code }

// URLHash is hex(SHA-256(canonical_url))[:32] (spec §2.8 step 1).
func URLHash(canonicalURL string) string {
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(canonicalURL))
	return hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])[:32]
}
```

`backend/internal/store/models.go`:

```go
package store

import "strings"

// Installation statuses (spec §3.1).
const (
	StatusActive     = "active"
	StatusNeedsSetup = "needs_setup"
	StatusRevoked    = "revoked"
)

type Profile struct {
	PK                   string `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK                   string `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	NotionUserID         string `dynamodbav:"notion_user_id"`
	Name                 string `dynamodbav:"name"`
	Email                string `dynamodbav:"email,omitempty"`
	ActiveInstallationID string `dynamodbav:"active_installation_id,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt            string `dynamodbav:"created_at"`
	DeletedAt            string `dynamodbav:"deleted_at,omitempty"`
}

func (p Profile) UserID() string { return strings.TrimPrefix(p.PK, "USER#") }

type Device struct {
	PK         string `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK         string `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	Name       string `dynamodbav:"name"`
	TokenHMAC  string `dynamodbav:"token_hmac"`
	CreatedAt  string `dynamodbav:"created_at"`
	LastSeenAt string `dynamodbav:"last_seen_at"`
	RevokedAt  string `dynamodbav:"revoked_at,omitempty"`
}

func (d Device) ID() string { return strings.TrimPrefix(d.SK, "DEVICE#") }

type DeviceToken struct {
	PK       string `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK       string `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	UserID   string `dynamodbav:"user_id"`
	DeviceID string `dynamodbav:"device_id"`
}

type OAuthState struct {
	PK            string `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK            string `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	ExtRedirect   string `dynamodbav:"ext_redirect"`
	CodeChallenge string `dynamodbav:"code_challenge"`
	TTL           int64  `dynamodbav:"ttl"`
}

type AuthCode struct {
	PK             string `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK             string `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	UserID         string `dynamodbav:"user_id"`
	InstallationID string `dynamodbav:"installation_id"`
	CodeChallenge  string `dynamodbav:"code_challenge"`
	ExtRedirect    string `dynamodbav:"ext_redirect"`
	TTL            int64  `dynamodbav:"ttl"`
}

// DBConfig is the resolved Notion target of an installation (spec §2.11).
type DBConfig struct {
	LibraryDBID string
	LibraryDSID string
	LibraryURL  string
	DailyDBID   string
	DailyDSID   string
	Props       map[string]string // logical key → Notion property id (C9)
}

type Installation struct {
	PK                   string            `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK                   string            `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	UserID               string            `dynamodbav:"user_id"`
	WorkspaceID          string            `dynamodbav:"workspace_id"`
	WorkspaceName        string            `dynamodbav:"workspace_name"`
	WorkspaceIcon        string            `dynamodbav:"workspace_icon,omitempty"`
	Status               string            `dynamodbav:"status"`
	SetupError           string            `dynamodbav:"setup_error,omitempty"`
	AccessTokenEnc       string            `dynamodbav:"access_token_enc"`
	RefreshTokenEnc      string            `dynamodbav:"refresh_token_enc"`
	TokenExpiresAt       string            `dynamodbav:"token_expires_at,omitempty"`
	TokenVersion         int               `dynamodbav:"token_version"`
	DuplicatedTemplateID string            `dynamodbav:"duplicated_template_id,omitempty"`
	LibraryDBID          string            `dynamodbav:"library_db_id,omitempty"`
	LibraryDSID          string            `dynamodbav:"library_ds_id,omitempty"`
	LibraryURL           string            `dynamodbav:"library_url,omitempty"`
	DailyDBID            string            `dynamodbav:"daily_db_id,omitempty"`
	DailyDSID            string            `dynamodbav:"daily_ds_id,omitempty"`
	Props                map[string]string `dynamodbav:"props,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt            string            `dynamodbav:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt            string            `dynamodbav:"updated_at"`
}

func (i Installation) ID() string { return strings.TrimPrefix(i.PK, "INST#") }

// HasDatabases reports whether both data sources and their property ids are known.
func (i Installation) HasDatabases() bool {
	return i.LibraryDSID != "" && i.DailyDSID != "" && len(i.Props) > 0
}

func (i *Installation) ApplyDatabases(db DBConfig) {
	i.LibraryDBID, i.LibraryDSID, i.LibraryURL = db.LibraryDBID, db.LibraryDSID, db.LibraryURL
	i.DailyDBID, i.DailyDSID, i.Props = db.DailyDBID, db.DailyDSID, db.Props
}

type Article struct {
	PK                  string  `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK                  string  `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	CanonicalURL        string  `dynamodbav:"canonical_url"`
	Source              string  `dynamodbav:"source"`
	Title               string  `dynamodbav:"title"`
	Author              string  `dynamodbav:"author"`
	SiteName            string  `dynamodbav:"site_name"`
	WordCount           int     `dynamodbav:"word_count"`
	PublishedAt         string  `dynamodbav:"published_at"`
	FirstReadAt         string  `dynamodbav:"first_read_at"`
	LastReadAt          string  `dynamodbav:"last_read_at"`
	TotalSeconds        int64   `dynamodbav:"total_seconds"`
	Sessions            int64   `dynamodbav:"sessions"`
	MaxProgress         float64 `dynamodbav:"max_progress"`
	Completed           bool    `dynamodbav:"completed"`
	Version             int64   `dynamodbav:"version"`
	NotionPageID        string  `dynamodbav:"notion_page_id,omitempty"`
	NotionURL           string  `dynamodbav:"notion_url,omitempty"`
	NotionSyncedVersion int64   `dynamodbav:"notion_synced_version,omitempty"`
	NotionSyncedAt      string  `dynamodbav:"notion_synced_at,omitempty"`
}

func (a Article) Hash() string { return strings.TrimPrefix(a.SK, "ART#") }

type Day struct {
	PK                  string   `dynamodbav:"pk"`
	SK                  string   `dynamodbav:"sk"`
	Date                string   `dynamodbav:"date"`
	Seconds             int64    `dynamodbav:"seconds"`
	ArticleHashes       []string `dynamodbav:"article_hashes,stringset"`
	Version             int64    `dynamodbav:"version"`
	NotionPageID        string   `dynamodbav:"notion_page_id,omitempty"`
	NotionSyncedVersion int64    `dynamodbav:"notion_synced_version,omitempty"`
}
```

`backend/internal/store/table.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
)

// CreateTable creates the §3.1 table if it doesn't exist. Terraform owns the real
// table (D8); this exists for tests and cmd/local.
func CreateTable(ctx context.Context, db *dynamodb.Client, name string) error {
	_, err := db.CreateTable(ctx, &dynamodb.CreateTableInput{
		TableName: aws.String(name),
		AttributeDefinitions: []types.AttributeDefinition{
			{AttributeName: aws.String("pk"), AttributeType: types.ScalarAttributeTypeS},
			{AttributeName: aws.String("sk"), AttributeType: types.ScalarAttributeTypeS},
		},
		KeySchema: []types.KeySchemaElement{
			{AttributeName: aws.String("pk"), KeyType: types.KeyTypeHash},
			{AttributeName: aws.String("sk"), KeyType: types.KeyTypeRange},
		},
		BillingMode:           types.BillingModeProvisioned,
		ProvisionedThroughput: &types.ProvisionedThroughput{ReadCapacityUnits: aws.Int64(25), WriteCapacityUnits: aws.Int64(25)},
	})
	var inUse *types.ResourceInUseException
	if errors.As(err, &inUse) {
		return nil
	}
	return err
}
```

`backend/internal/store/storetest/storetest.go`:

```go
// Package storetest provisions throwaway tables on DynamoDB Local (`make ddb-up`).
package storetest

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"math/rand/v2"
	"os"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// Env is one fresh table plus a Store bound to it.
type Env struct {
	Store *store.Store
	DB    *dynamodb.Client
	Table string
}

// Endpoint is DYNAMODB_ENDPOINT or http://localhost:8000.
func Endpoint() string {
	if v := os.Getenv("DYNAMODB_ENDPOINT"); v != "" {
		return v
	}
	return "http://localhost:8000"
}

// Client returns a DynamoDB Local client with static dummy credentials.
func Client() *dynamodb.Client {
	return dynamodb.New(dynamodb.Options{
		Region:       "us-east-1",
		Credentials:  credentials.NewStaticCredentialsProvider("local", "local", ""),
		BaseEndpoint: aws.String(Endpoint()),
	})
}

// New creates a uniquely named table, dropped when the test ends. It fails the
// test (never skips) when DynamoDB Local is unreachable.
func New(t testing.TB) *Env {
	t.Helper()
	db := Client()
	name := fmt.Sprintf("reading-test-%d", rand.Int64())
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if err := store.CreateTable(ctx, db, name); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("DynamoDB Local not reachable at %s (run `make ddb-up`): %v", Endpoint(), err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		_, _ = db.DeleteTable(context.Background(), &dynamodb.DeleteTableInput{TableName: aws.String(name)})
	})
	return &Env{Store: store.New(db, name), DB: db, Table: name}
}

// Scan returns every item in the table.
func (e *Env) Scan(t testing.TB) []map[string]types.AttributeValue {
	t.Helper()
	var items []map[string]types.AttributeValue
	in := &dynamodb.ScanInput{TableName: aws.String(e.Table)}
	for {
		res, err := e.DB.Scan(context.Background(), in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("scan: %v", err)
		}
		items = append(items, res.Items...)
		if len(res.LastEvaluatedKey) == 0 {
			return items
		}
		in.ExclusiveStartKey = res.LastEvaluatedKey
	}
}
```

```go
package store_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store/storetest"
)

var (
	ctx     = context.Background()
	testNow = time.Date(2026, 10, 6, 3, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
)

func must(t *testing.T, err error) {
	t.Helper()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestCreateUserAndResolveByNotionUser(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.CreateUser(ctx, "u1", store.Profile{NotionUserID: "n1", Name: "Tuan Do", CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(testNow)}))

	uid, err := s.UserIDForNotionUser(ctx, "n1")
	must(t, err)
	p, err := s.GetProfile(ctx, uid)
	must(t, err)
	if uid != "u1" || p.Name != "Tuan Do" || p.UserID() != "u1" {
		t.Fatalf("got uid=%q profile=%+v", uid, p)
	}
	must(t, s.SetActiveInstallation(ctx, "u1", "bot-1"))
	p, _ = s.GetProfile(ctx, "u1")
	if p.ActiveInstallationID != "bot-1" {
		t.Fatalf("active installation not set: %+v", p)
	}
	if _, err := s.UserIDForNotionUser(ctx, "nobody"); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("want ErrNotFound, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestDeviceLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.CreateDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1", store.Device{Name: "Mac", TokenHMAC: "h1", CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(testNow), LastSeenAt: store.Timestamp(testNow)}))

	ref, err := s.GetDeviceToken(ctx, "h1")
	must(t, err)
	if ref.UserID != "u1" || ref.DeviceID != "d1" {
		t.Fatalf("bad token ref %+v", ref)
	}
	devs, err := s.ListDevices(ctx, "u1")
	must(t, err)
	if len(devs) != 1 || devs[0].ID() != "d1" {
		t.Fatalf("ListDevices = %+v", devs)
	}

	must(t, s.RevokeDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1", "h1", testNow))
	if _, err := s.GetDeviceToken(ctx, "h1"); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("token lookup must be gone after revoke, got %v", err)
	}
	d, err := s.GetDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1")
	must(t, err)
	if d.RevokedAt == "" {
		t.Fatal("revoked_at not set")
	}
}

func TestTouchDeviceWritesAtMostHourly(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.CreateDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1", store.Device{Name: "Mac", TokenHMAC: "h1", CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(testNow), LastSeenAt: store.Timestamp(testNow)}))

	must(t, s.TouchDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1", testNow.Add(10*time.Minute)))
	d, _ := s.GetDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1")
	if d.LastSeenAt != store.Timestamp(testNow) {
		t.Fatalf("touched within the hour: %s", d.LastSeenAt)
	}
	must(t, s.TouchDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1", testNow.Add(61*time.Minute)))
	d, _ = s.GetDevice(ctx, "u1", "d1")
	if d.LastSeenAt != store.Timestamp(testNow.Add(61*time.Minute)) {
		t.Fatalf("not touched after an hour: %s", d.LastSeenAt)
	}
}
```

```go
func TestOAuthStateConsumedOnce(t *testing.T) {

	must(t, s.PutOAuthState(ctx, "st1", store.OAuthState{ExtRedirect: "https://x.chromiumapp.org/", CodeChallenge: "c"}, testNow.Add(10*time.Minute)))

	if _, err := s.ConsumeOAuthState(ctx, "st1", testNow); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("second consume must fail, got %v", err)

// Scenario 18: an expired item that DynamoDB has not reaped yet is still rejected (C12).
func TestExpiredAuthCodeRejected(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.PutAuthCode(ctx, "code1", store.AuthCode{UserID: "u1", InstallationID: "bot-1", CodeChallenge: "c"}, testNow.Add(-time.Second)))
	if _, err := s.ConsumeAuthCode(ctx, "code1", testNow); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("expired code must be rejected, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestAuthCodeRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.PutAuthCode(ctx, "code1", store.AuthCode{UserID: "u1", InstallationID: "bot-1", CodeChallenge: "c", ExtRedirect: "e"}, testNow.Add(2*time.Minute)))
	ac, err := s.ConsumeAuthCode(ctx, "code1", testNow)
	must(t, err)
	if ac.UserID != "u1" || ac.InstallationID != "bot-1" || ac.ExtRedirect != "e" {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v", ac)
	}
}
```

```go
package store_test

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store/storetest"
)

func newInstallation() *store.Installation {
	return &store.Installation{UserID: "u1", WorkspaceID: "ws", WorkspaceName: "WS", Status: store.StatusNeedsSetup,
		SetupError: "template_not_found", AccessTokenEnc: "v1:a", RefreshTokenEnc: "v1:r", TokenVersion: 1,
		CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(testNow), UpdatedAt: store.Timestamp(testNow)}
}

func TestInstallationTokensVersionGuard(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.PutInstallation(ctx, "bot-1", newInstallation()))

	must(t, s.UpdateInstallationTokens(ctx, "bot-1", "v1:a2", "v1:r2", 1, testNow))
	inst, err := s.GetInstallation(ctx, "bot-1")
	must(t, err)
	if inst.TokenVersion != 2 || inst.AccessTokenEnc != "v1:a2" || inst.RefreshTokenEnc != "v1:r2" || inst.ID() != "bot-1" {
		t.Fatalf("tokens not rotated: %+v", inst)
	}
	if err := s.UpdateInstallationTokens(ctx, "bot-1", "v1:a3", "v1:r3", 1, testNow); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrConditionFailed) {
		t.Fatalf("stale token_version must fail (C7), got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestSaveInstallationDatabasesActivates(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.PutInstallation(ctx, "bot-1", newInstallation()))
	db := store.DBConfig{LibraryDBID: "ldb", LibraryDSID: "lds", LibraryURL: "https://notion.so/l", DailyDBID: "ddb", DailyDSID: "dds",
		Props: map[string]string{"title": "p-title", "day_date": "p-date"}}

	must(t, s.SaveInstallationDatabases(ctx, "bot-1", db, testNow))
	inst, _ := s.GetInstallation(ctx, "bot-1")
	if inst.Status != store.StatusActive || inst.SetupError != "" || inst.Props["title"] != "p-title" || !inst.HasDatabases() {
		t.Fatalf("not activated: %+v", inst)
	}

	must(t, s.SetInstallationStatus(ctx, "bot-1", store.StatusNeedsSetup, "database_missing:Reading Library", testNow))
	inst, _ = s.GetInstallation(ctx, "bot-1")
	if inst.Status != store.StatusNeedsSetup || inst.SetupError != "database_missing:Reading Library" {
		t.Fatalf("status not updated: %+v", inst)
	}
}

func TestUserInstallationPointers(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.PutUserInstallation(ctx, "u1", "bot-1", testNow))
	must(t, s.PutUserInstallation(ctx, "u1", "bot-1", testNow)) // idempotent
	must(t, s.PutUserInstallation(ctx, "u1", "bot-2", testNow))
	ids, err := s.ListUserInstallationIDs(ctx, "u1")
	must(t, err)
	if len(ids) != 2 || ids[0] != "bot-1" || ids[1] != "bot-2" {
		t.Fatalf("got %v", ids)
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/store/identity.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/feature/dynamodb/attributevalue"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
)

func (s *Store) GetProfile(ctx context.Context, uid string) (*Profile, error) {
	var p Profile
	if err := s.get(ctx, UserPK(uid), SKProfile, &p); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &p, nil
}

// UserIDForNotionUser resolves NUSER#<notion_user_id> → user_id (spec §2.2 step 4).
func (s *Store) UserIDForNotionUser(ctx context.Context, notionUserID string) (string, error) {
	var link struct {
		UserID string `dynamodbav:"user_id"`
	}
	if err := s.get(ctx, NUserPK(notionUserID), SKNUser, &link); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return link.UserID, nil
}

// CreateUser writes PROFILE and the NUSER pointer in one transaction.
func (s *Store) CreateUser(ctx context.Context, uid string, p Profile) error {
	p.PK, p.SK = UserPK(uid), SKProfile
	prof, err := attributevalue.MarshalMap(p)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	link := map[string]types.AttributeValue{"pk": str(NUserPK(p.NotionUserID)), "sk": str(SKNUser), "user_id": str(uid)}
	_, err = s.db.TransactWriteItems(ctx, &dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput{TransactItems: []types.TransactWriteItem{
		{Put: &types.Put{TableName: &s.table, Item: prof}},
		{Put: &types.Put{TableName: &s.table, Item: link}},
	}})
	return err
}

func (s *Store) SetActiveInstallation(ctx context.Context, uid, botID string) error {
	return s.update(ctx, UserPK(uid), SKProfile, "SET #a = :a", "attribute_exists(pk)",
		map[string]string{"#a": "active_installation_id"}, map[string]types.AttributeValue{":a": str(botID)})
}

func (s *Store) MarkProfileDeleted(ctx context.Context, uid string, now time.Time) error {
	return s.update(ctx, UserPK(uid), SKProfile, "SET #d = :d", "attribute_exists(pk)",
		map[string]string{"#d": "deleted_at"}, map[string]types.AttributeValue{":d": str(Timestamp(now))})
}

// CreateDevice writes DEVICE and its DEVTOKEN lookup together (spec §2.3 step 4).
func (s *Store) CreateDevice(ctx context.Context, uid, deviceID string, d Device) error {
	d.PK, d.SK = UserPK(uid), DeviceSK(deviceID)
	dev, err := attributevalue.MarshalMap(d)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tok := map[string]types.AttributeValue{
		"pk": str(DevTokenPK(d.TokenHMAC)), "sk": str(SKDevToken), "user_id": str(uid), "device_id": str(deviceID),
	}
	_, err = s.db.TransactWriteItems(ctx, &dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput{TransactItems: []types.TransactWriteItem{
		{Put: &types.Put{TableName: &s.table, Item: dev}},
		{Put: &types.Put{TableName: &s.table, Item: tok, ConditionExpression: aws.String("attribute_not_exists(pk)")}},
	}})
	return err
}

func (s *Store) GetDeviceToken(ctx context.Context, tokenHMAC string) (*DeviceToken, error) {
	var t DeviceToken
	if err := s.get(ctx, DevTokenPK(tokenHMAC), SKDevToken, &t); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &t, nil
}

func (s *Store) GetDevice(ctx context.Context, uid, deviceID string) (*Device, error) {
	var d Device
	if err := s.get(ctx, UserPK(uid), DeviceSK(deviceID), &d); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &d, nil
}

// TouchDevice bumps last_seen_at only when it is over an hour old (spec §2.4 step 3).
func (s *Store) TouchDevice(ctx context.Context, uid, deviceID string, now time.Time) error {
	err := s.update(ctx, UserPK(uid), DeviceSK(deviceID), "SET #ls = :now",
		"attribute_exists(pk) AND (attribute_not_exists(#ls) OR #ls < :cutoff)",
		map[string]string{"#ls": "last_seen_at"},
		map[string]types.AttributeValue{":now": str(Timestamp(now)), ":cutoff": str(Timestamp(now.Add(-time.Hour)))})
	if errors.Is(err, ErrConditionFailed) {
		return nil
	}
	return err
}

// RevokeDevice sets revoked_at and deletes the token lookup atomically (spec §2.17).
func (s *Store) RevokeDevice(ctx context.Context, uid, deviceID, tokenHMAC string, now time.Time) error {
	_, err := s.db.TransactWriteItems(ctx, &dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput{TransactItems: []types.TransactWriteItem{
		{Update: &types.Update{
			TableName:                 &s.table,
			Key:                       key(UserPK(uid), DeviceSK(deviceID)),
			UpdateExpression:          aws.String("SET #r = :r"),
			ExpressionAttributeNames:  map[string]string{"#r": "revoked_at"},
			ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{":r": str(Timestamp(now))},
		}},
		{Delete: &types.Delete{TableName: &s.table, Key: key(DevTokenPK(tokenHMAC), SKDevToken)}},
	}})
	return err
}

func (s *Store) ListDevices(ctx context.Context, uid string) ([]Device, error) {
	items, err := s.queryPrefix(ctx, UserPK(uid), "DEVICE#")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []Device
	return out, attributevalue.UnmarshalListOfMaps(items, &out)
}

func (s *Store) DeleteDeviceToken(ctx context.Context, tokenHMAC string) error {
	_, err := s.db.DeleteItem(ctx, &dynamodb.DeleteItemInput{TableName: &s.table, Key: key(DevTokenPK(tokenHMAC), SKDevToken)})
	return err
}
```

`backend/internal/store/oauth.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/feature/dynamodb/attributevalue"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
)

func (s *Store) PutOAuthState(ctx context.Context, state string, st OAuthState, expires time.Time) error {
	st.PK, st.SK, st.TTL = OAuthStatePK(state), SKState, expires.Unix()
	return s.putItem(ctx, st)
}

func (s *Store) ConsumeOAuthState(ctx context.Context, state string, now time.Time) (*OAuthState, error) {
	var st OAuthState
	if err := s.consume(ctx, OAuthStatePK(state), SKState, now, &st); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &st, nil
}

func (s *Store) PutAuthCode(ctx context.Context, code string, ac AuthCode, expires time.Time) error {
	ac.PK, ac.SK, ac.TTL = AuthCodePK(code), SKCode, expires.Unix()
	return s.putItem(ctx, ac)
}

func (s *Store) ConsumeAuthCode(ctx context.Context, code string, now time.Time) (*AuthCode, error) {
	var ac AuthCode
	if err := s.consume(ctx, AuthCodePK(code), SKCode, now, &ac); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &ac, nil
}

// consume deletes a single-use TTL item and returns it. Expired items that DynamoDB
// hasn't reaped yet fail the condition, because TTL deletion is lazy (C12).
func (s *Store) consume(ctx context.Context, pk, sk string, now time.Time, out any) error {
	res, err := s.db.DeleteItem(ctx, &dynamodb.DeleteItemInput{
		TableName:                 &s.table,
		Key:                       key(pk, sk),
		ConditionExpression:       aws.String("attribute_exists(pk) AND #ttl > :now"),
		ExpressionAttributeNames:  map[string]string{"#ttl": "ttl"},
		ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{":now": numI(now.Unix())},
		ReturnValues:              types.ReturnValueAllOld,
	})
	if isConditionFailed(err) {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return attributevalue.UnmarshalMap(res.Attributes, out)
}
```

`backend/internal/store/installations.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"context"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/feature/dynamodb/attributevalue"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
)

func (s *Store) GetInstallation(ctx context.Context, botID string) (*Installation, error) {
	var inst Installation
	if err := s.get(ctx, InstPK(botID), SKMeta, &inst); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &inst, nil
}

// PutInstallation writes the whole META item (used by the OAuth callback upsert).
func (s *Store) PutInstallation(ctx context.Context, botID string, inst *Installation) error {
	inst.PK, inst.SK = InstPK(botID), SKMeta
	return s.putItem(ctx, inst)
}

// PutUserInstallation records USER#<uid>/INST#<bot_id>, keeping the first created_at.
func (s *Store) PutUserInstallation(ctx context.Context, uid, botID string, now time.Time) error {
	item := map[string]types.AttributeValue{"pk": str(UserPK(uid)), "sk": str(InstPK(botID)), "created_at": str(Timestamp(now))}
	_, err := s.db.PutItem(ctx, &dynamodb.PutItemInput{TableName: &s.table, Item: item, ConditionExpression: aws.String("attribute_not_exists(pk)")})
	if isConditionFailed(err) {
		return nil
	}
	return err
}

func (s *Store) ListUserInstallationIDs(ctx context.Context, uid string) ([]string, error) {
	items, err := s.queryPrefix(ctx, UserPK(uid), "INST#")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	ids := make([]string, 0, len(items))
	for _, it := range items {
		var k struct {
			SK string `dynamodbav:"sk"`
		}
		if err := attributevalue.UnmarshalMap(it, &k); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		ids = append(ids, strings.TrimPrefix(k.SK, "INST#"))
	}
	return ids, nil
}

// SetInstallationStatus sets status; an empty setupError removes setup_error.
func (s *Store) SetInstallationStatus(ctx context.Context, botID, status, setupError string, now time.Time) error {
	names := map[string]string{"#s": "status", "#u": "updated_at", "#e": "setup_error"}
	values := map[string]types.AttributeValue{":s": str(status), ":u": str(Timestamp(now))}
	expr := "SET #s = :s, #u = :u REMOVE #e"
	if setupError != "" {
		expr = "SET #s = :s, #u = :u, #e = :e"
		values[":e"] = str(setupError)
	}
	return s.update(ctx, InstPK(botID), SKMeta, expr, "attribute_exists(pk)", names, values)
}

// SaveInstallationDatabases stores the resolved Notion IDs and marks the installation active.
func (s *Store) SaveInstallationDatabases(ctx context.Context, botID string, db DBConfig, now time.Time) error {
	props, err := attributevalue.Marshal(db.Props)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return s.update(ctx, InstPK(botID), SKMeta,
		"SET #ldb = :ldb, #lds = :lds, #lurl = :lurl, #ddb = :ddb, #dds = :dds, #p = :p, #s = :active, #u = :u REMOVE #e",
		"attribute_exists(pk)",
		map[string]string{"#ldb": "library_db_id", "#lds": "library_ds_id", "#lurl": "library_url", "#ddb": "daily_db_id",
			"#dds": "daily_ds_id", "#p": "props", "#s": "status", "#u": "updated_at", "#e": "setup_error"},
		map[string]types.AttributeValue{":ldb": str(db.LibraryDBID), ":lds": str(db.LibraryDSID), ":lurl": str(db.LibraryURL),
			":ddb": str(db.DailyDBID), ":dds": str(db.DailyDSID), ":p": props, ":active": str(StatusActive), ":u": str(Timestamp(now))})
}

// UpdateInstallationTokens persists both rotated tokens, guarded by token_version (C7).
func (s *Store) UpdateInstallationTokens(ctx context.Context, botID, accessEnc, refreshEnc string, oldVersion int, now time.Time) error {
	return s.update(ctx, InstPK(botID), SKMeta,
		"SET #a = :a, #r = :r, #v = :new, #u = :u", "#v = :old",
		map[string]string{"#a": "access_token_enc", "#r": "refresh_token_enc", "#v": "token_version", "#u": "updated_at"},
		map[string]types.AttributeValue{":a": str(accessEnc), ":r": str(refreshEnc), ":new": numI(int64(oldVersion + 1)),
			":old": numI(int64(oldVersion)), ":u": str(Timestamp(now))})
}
```

```go
package store_test

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store/storetest"
)

const articleURL = "https://medium.com/p/8f3c2a1b9d4e"

func segment(id, url string, seq, secs int, progress float64, started time.Time) store.SegmentWrite {
	return store.SegmentWrite{
		InstallationID: "bot-1", DeviceID: "dev-1", SegmentID: id, SessionID: "sess-1", Seq: seq,
		URLHash: store.URLHash(url), Date: started.UTC().Format("2006-01-02"),
		StartedAt: started, EndedAt: started.Add(time.Duration(secs) * time.Second),
		ActiveSeconds: secs, MaxProgress: progress,
		Article: store.ArticleMeta{CanonicalURL: url, Source: "medium", Title: "Designing Idempotent APIs", WordCount: 1820},
	}
}

func push(t *testing.T, s *store.Store, w store.SegmentWrite) store.PushOutcome {
	t.Helper()
	out, err := s.PushSegment(ctx, w)
	must(t, err)
	return out
}

// T10 AC + scenario 2: the same segment twice increments aggregates once (C2).
func TestPushSegmentTwiceCountsOnce(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	w := segment("6f1d2c9e-6a43-4b1e-9a0e-2f7c1d5b8e11", articleURL, 0, 58, 0.42, testNow)

	if out := push(t, s, w); out != store.PushAccepted {
		t.Fatalf("first push = %v", out)
	}
	if out := push(t, s, w); out != store.PushDuplicate {
		t.Fatalf("second push = %v, want duplicate", out)
	}
	a, err := s.GetArticle(ctx, "bot-1", store.URLHash(articleURL))
	must(t, err)
	if a.TotalSeconds != 58 || a.Sessions != 1 || a.Version != 1 || a.MaxProgress != 0.42 || a.CanonicalURL != articleURL || a.Title != "Designing Idempotent APIs" {
		t.Fatalf("aggregate wrong: %+v", a)
	}
	if a.FirstReadAt != store.Timestamp(testNow) || a.LastReadAt != store.Timestamp(testNow.Add(58*time.Second)) {
		t.Fatalf("read times wrong: first=%s last=%s", a.FirstReadAt, a.LastReadAt)
	}
	d, err := s.GetDay(ctx, "bot-1", "2026-10-06")
	must(t, err)
	if d.Seconds != 58 || d.Version != 1 || len(d.ArticleHashes) != 1 || d.ArticleHashes[0] != store.URLHash(articleURL) {
		t.Fatalf("day wrong: %+v", d)
	}
}

// T10 AC: max_progress (and last_read_at) never decrease.
func TestMaxProgressNeverDecreases(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.6, testNow.Add(time.Hour)))
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002", articleURL, 1, 60, 0.3, testNow))
	a, _ := s.GetArticle(ctx, "bot-1", store.URLHash(articleURL))
	if a.MaxProgress != 0.6 {
		t.Fatalf("max_progress = %v, want 0.6", a.MaxProgress)
	}
	if a.LastReadAt != store.Timestamp(testNow.Add(time.Hour+60*time.Second)) {
		t.Fatalf("last_read_at went backwards: %s", a.LastReadAt)
	}
	if a.TotalSeconds != 120 || a.Version != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("totals wrong: %+v", a)
	}
}

// Scenario 3: seq 1 before seq 0 still counts one session.
func TestOutOfOrderSessionCountedOnce(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002", articleURL, 1, 60, 0.5, testNow.Add(time.Minute)))
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.2, testNow))
	a, _ := s.GetArticle(ctx, "bot-1", store.URLHash(articleURL))
	if a.Sessions != 1 || a.TotalSeconds != 120 {
		t.Fatalf("sessions=%d total=%d", a.Sessions, a.TotalSeconds)
	}
}

type throttlingDB struct{ store.API }

func (throttlingDB) TransactWriteItems(context.Context, *dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput, ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsOutput, error) {
	return nil, &types.ProvisionedThroughputExceededException{Message: aws.String("slow down")}
}

// Scenario 14: throttling → retry, nothing written.
func TestPushThrottleIsRetry(t *testing.T) {
	env := storetest.New(t)
	s := store.New(throttlingDB{env.DB}, env.Table)
	if out := push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.5, testNow)); out != store.PushRetry {
		t.Fatalf("got %v, want retry", out)
	}
	if items := env.Scan(t); len(items) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("throttled push wrote %d items", len(items))
	}
}

type conflictOnceDB struct {
	store.API
	conflicts int
}

func (c *conflictOnceDB) TransactWriteItems(ctx context.Context, in *dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput, opts ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsOutput, error) {
	if c.conflicts == 0 {
		c.conflicts++
		return nil, &types.TransactionCanceledException{Message: aws.String("conflict"), CancellationReasons: []types.CancellationReason{
			{Code: aws.String("None")}, {Code: aws.String("TransactionConflict")}, {Code: aws.String("None")},
		}}
	}
	return c.API.TransactWriteItems(ctx, in, opts...)
}

// Review focus 1 / scenario 4: concurrent pushes to the same ART item conflict; retry in-server.
func TestPushRetriesTransactionConflict(t *testing.T) {
	env := storetest.New(t)
	s := store.New(&conflictOnceDB{API: env.DB}, env.Table, store.WithSleep(func(time.Duration) {}))
	if out := push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.5, testNow)); out != store.PushAccepted {
		t.Fatalf("got %v, want accepted after conflict retry", out)
	}
}

type countingDB struct {
	store.API
	batchGets int
}

func (c *countingDB) BatchGetItem(ctx context.Context, in *dynamodb.BatchGetItemInput, opts ...func(*dynamodb.Options)) (*dynamodb.BatchGetItemOutput, error) {
	c.batchGets++
	return c.API.BatchGetItem(ctx, in, opts...)
}

// T17 AC: status for 100 URLs uses one BatchGet round trip (plus unprocessed retries).
func TestBatchGetArticlesOneRoundTripFor100(t *testing.T) {
	env := storetest.New(t)
	db := &countingDB{API: env.DB}
	s := store.New(db, env.Table)
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.5, testNow))

	hashes := []string{store.URLHash(articleURL)}
	for i := len(hashes); i < 100; i++ {
		hashes = append(hashes, store.URLHash(articleURL+"?n="+string(rune('a'+i%26))+string(rune('a'+i/26))))
	}
	got, err := s.BatchGetArticles(ctx, "bot-1", hashes)
	must(t, err)
	if db.batchGets != 1 || len(got) != 1 || got[store.URLHash(articleURL)].TotalSeconds != 60 {
		t.Fatalf("batchGets=%d got=%d", db.batchGets, len(got))
	}
}

func TestMarkArticleSyncedIsMonotonic(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	h := store.URLHash(articleURL)
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.5, testNow))
	must(t, s.MarkArticleSynced(ctx, "bot-1", h, 2, true, testNow))
	must(t, s.MarkArticleSynced(ctx, "bot-1", h, 1, false, testNow))
	a, _ := s.GetArticle(ctx, "bot-1", h)
	if a.NotionSyncedVersion != 2 || !a.Completed {
		t.Fatalf("older version overwrote newer: %+v", a)
	}
}

func TestArticleAndDayPageLinks(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	h := store.URLHash(articleURL)
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.5, testNow))
	must(t, s.SetArticlePage(ctx, "bot-1", h, "page-1", "https://notion.so/page-1"))
	must(t, s.SetDayPage(ctx, "bot-1", "2026-10-06", "day-page-1"))
	a, _ := s.GetArticle(ctx, "bot-1", h)
	d, _ := s.GetDay(ctx, "bot-1", "2026-10-06")
	if a.NotionPageID != "page-1" || a.NotionURL != "https://notion.so/page-1" || d.NotionPageID != "day-page-1" {
		t.Fatalf("links not saved: %+v %+v", a, d)
	}
	must(t, s.ClearArticlePage(ctx, "bot-1", h))
	must(t, s.ClearDayPage(ctx, "bot-1", "2026-10-06"))
	a, _ = s.GetArticle(ctx, "bot-1", h)
	d, _ = s.GetDay(ctx, "bot-1", "2026-10-06")
	if a.NotionPageID != "" || a.NotionURL != "" || d.NotionPageID != "" {
		t.Fatalf("links not cleared: %+v %+v", a, d)
	}
}
```

```go
package store_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"testing"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store/storetest"
)

// T22: only items whose version is ahead of notion_synced_version are pending.
func TestPendingSync(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	other := "https://example.com/post"
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", articleURL, 0, 60, 0.5, testNow))
	push(t, s, segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002", other, 0, 60, 0.5, testNow))
	must(t, s.MarkArticleSynced(ctx, "bot-1", store.URLHash(articleURL), 1, false, testNow))

	hashes, dates, err := s.PendingSync(ctx, "bot-1")
	must(t, err)
	if len(hashes) != 1 || hashes[0] != store.URLHash(other) {
		t.Fatalf("hashes = %v", hashes)
	}
	if len(dates) != 1 || dates[0] != "2026-10-06" {
		t.Fatalf("dates = %v", dates)
	}
	must(t, s.MarkDaySynced(ctx, "bot-1", "2026-10-06", 2))
	if _, dates, _ = s.PendingSync(ctx, "bot-1"); len(dates) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("synced day still pending: %v", dates)
	}
}

func TestBatchDeleteRemovesWholePartition(t *testing.T) {
	env := storetest.New(t)
	for i := 0; i < 30; i++ { // > 25 forces two BatchWriteItem chunks
		_, err := env.DB.PutItem(ctx, &dynamodb.PutItemInput{TableName: aws.String(env.Table), Item: map[string]types.AttributeValue{
			"pk": &types.AttributeValueMemberS{Value: "INST#bot-1"}, "sk": &types.AttributeValueMemberS{Value: fmt.Sprintf("SEG#%02d", i)},
		}})
		must(t, err)
	}
	keys, err := env.Store.PartitionKeys(ctx, "INST#bot-1")
	must(t, err)
	if len(keys) != 30 {
		t.Fatalf("PartitionKeys = %d", len(keys))
	}
	must(t, env.Store.BatchDelete(ctx, keys))
	if keys, _ = env.Store.PartitionKeys(ctx, "INST#bot-1"); len(keys) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("%d items left", len(keys))
	}
}

func TestDeleteNotionUserLinkOnlyForOwner(t *testing.T) {
	s := storetest.New(t).Store
	must(t, s.CreateUser(ctx, "u2", store.Profile{NotionUserID: "n1", Name: "new owner", CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(testNow)}))
	must(t, s.DeleteNotionUserLink(ctx, "n1", "u1")) // stale owner: no-op
	if uid, err := s.UserIDForNotionUser(ctx, "n1"); err != nil || uid != "u2" {
		t.Fatalf("link of another user deleted: %q %v", uid, err)
	}
	must(t, s.DeleteNotionUserLink(ctx, "n1", "u2"))
	if _, err := s.UserIDForNotionUser(ctx, "n1"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("link not deleted")
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/store/push.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
)

// ArticleMeta is the descriptive part of an article, written once (if_not_exists).
type ArticleMeta struct {
	CanonicalURL string
	Source       string
	Title        string
	Author       string
	SiteName     string
	WordCount    int
	PublishedAt  string
}

// SegmentWrite is one validated segment ready for the push transaction.
type SegmentWrite struct {
	InstallationID string
	DeviceID       string
	SegmentID      string
	SessionID      string
	Seq            int
	URLHash        string
	Date           string // YYYY-MM-DD of StartedAt in the request tz
	StartedAt      time.Time
	EndedAt        time.Time
	ActiveSeconds  int
	MaxProgress    float64
	Article        ArticleMeta
}

type PushOutcome int

const (
	PushAccepted PushOutcome = iota + 1
	PushDuplicate
	PushRetry
)

const (
	segmentTTL       = 90 * 24 * time.Hour
	conflictAttempts = 3
)

// PushSegment applies one segment exactly once (C2): the SEG put plus the ART and
// DAY increments commit together or not at all.
func (s *Store) PushSegment(ctx context.Context, w SegmentWrite) (PushOutcome, error) {
	in := s.pushTx(w)
	for attempt := 1; ; attempt++ {
		_, err := s.db.TransactWriteItems(ctx, in)
		if err == nil {
			break
		}
		switch classifyTx(err) {
		case txDuplicate:
			return PushDuplicate, nil
		case txThrottled:
			return PushRetry, nil
		case txConflict:
			// Another push is updating the same ART/DAY item right now (scenario 4).
			if attempt >= conflictAttempts {
				return PushRetry, nil
			}
			s.sleep(time.Duration(attempt*50) * time.Millisecond)
		default:
			return 0, err
		}
	}
	s.raiseArticleMax(ctx, w)
	return PushAccepted, nil
}

func (s *Store) pushTx(w SegmentWrite) *dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput {
	pk := InstPK(w.InstallationID)
	started := Timestamp(w.StartedAt)
	first := int64(0)
	if w.Seq == 0 {
		first = 1
	}
	seg := map[string]types.AttributeValue{
		"pk": str(pk), "sk": str(SegmentSK(w.SegmentID)),
		"session_id": str(w.SessionID), "seq": numI(int64(w.Seq)), "url_hash": str(w.URLHash),
		"device_id": str(w.DeviceID), "started_at": str(started), "ended_at": str(Timestamp(w.EndedAt)),
		"active_seconds": numI(int64(w.ActiveSeconds)), "max_progress": numF(w.MaxProgress),
		"ttl": numI(w.StartedAt.Add(segmentTTL).Unix()),
	}
	a := w.Article
	return &dynamodb.TransactWriteItemsInput{TransactItems: []types.TransactWriteItem{
		{Put: &types.Put{TableName: &s.table, Item: seg, ConditionExpression: aws.String("attribute_not_exists(pk)")}},
		{Update: &types.Update{
			TableName: &s.table,
			Key:       key(pk, ArticleSK(w.URLHash)),
			UpdateExpression: aws.String("SET #u = if_not_exists(#u, :u), #src = if_not_exists(#src, :src), " +
				"#t = if_not_exists(#t, :t), #a = if_not_exists(#a, :a), #sn = if_not_exists(#sn, :sn), " +
				"#wc = if_not_exists(#wc, :wc), #pa = if_not_exists(#pa, :pa), #fr = if_not_exists(#fr, :st) " +
				"ADD #ts :as, #ss :first, #v :one"),
			ExpressionAttributeNames: map[string]string{
				"#u": "canonical_url", "#src": "source", "#t": "title", "#a": "author", "#sn": "site_name",
				"#wc": "word_count", "#pa": "published_at", "#fr": "first_read_at",
				"#ts": "total_seconds", "#ss": "sessions", "#v": "version",
			},
			ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{
				":u": str(a.CanonicalURL), ":src": str(a.Source), ":t": str(a.Title), ":a": str(a.Author),
				":sn": str(a.SiteName), ":wc": numI(int64(a.WordCount)), ":pa": str(a.PublishedAt), ":st": str(started),
				":as": numI(int64(w.ActiveSeconds)), ":first": numI(first), ":one": numI(1),
			},
		}},
		{Update: &types.Update{
			TableName:                &s.table,
			Key:                      key(pk, DaySK(w.Date)),
			UpdateExpression:         aws.String("SET #d = :date ADD #s :as, #v :one, #h :h"),
			ExpressionAttributeNames: map[string]string{"#d": "date", "#s": "seconds", "#v": "version", "#h": "article_hashes"},
			ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{
				":date": str(w.Date), ":as": numI(int64(w.ActiveSeconds)), ":one": numI(1),
				":h": &types.AttributeValueMemberSS{Value: []string{w.URLHash}},
			},
		}},
	}}
}

// raiseArticleMax applies the post-commit monotonic updates (spec §3.2). They are
// outside the transaction; a lost update is healed by the article's next segment.
func (s *Store) raiseArticleMax(ctx context.Context, w SegmentWrite) {
	k := key(InstPK(w.InstallationID), ArticleSK(w.URLHash))
	if w.MaxProgress > 0 {
		s.setIfGreater(ctx, k, "max_progress", numF(w.MaxProgress))
	}
	s.setIfGreater(ctx, k, "last_read_at", str(Timestamp(w.EndedAt)))
}

func (s *Store) setIfGreater(ctx context.Context, k map[string]types.AttributeValue, attr string, v types.AttributeValue) {
	_, err := s.db.UpdateItem(ctx, &dynamodb.UpdateItemInput{
		TableName:                 &s.table,
		Key:                       k,
		UpdateExpression:          aws.String("SET #f = :v"),
		ConditionExpression:       aws.String("attribute_not_exists(#f) OR #f < :v"),
		ExpressionAttributeNames:  map[string]string{"#f": attr},
		ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{":v": v},
	})
	if err != nil && !isConditionFailed(err) {
		slog.WarnContext(ctx, "store: post-commit update failed", "attr", attr, "err", err)
	}
}

type txClass int

const (
	txOther txClass = iota
	txDuplicate
	txThrottled
	txConflict
)

func classifyTx(err error) txClass {
	var tce *types.TransactionCanceledException
	if errors.As(err, &tce) {
		codes := cancellationCodes(tce)
		if len(codes) > 0 && codes[0] == "ConditionalCheckFailed" { // item 0 is the SEG put
			return txDuplicate
		}
		for _, c := range codes {
			switch c {
			case "ProvisionedThroughputExceeded", "ThrottlingError":
				return txThrottled
			case "TransactionConflict":
				return txConflict
			}
		}
		return txOther
	}
	if IsThrottle(err) {
		return txThrottled
	}
	return txOther
}

// cancellationCodes reads the per-item reasons, falling back to the
// "[ConditionalCheckFailed, None, None]" list in the message when they're absent.
func cancellationCodes(tce *types.TransactionCanceledException) []string {
	codes := make([]string, 0, len(tce.CancellationReasons))
	for _, r := range tce.CancellationReasons {
		codes = append(codes, aws.ToString(r.Code))
	}
	if len(codes) == 0 {
		msg := aws.ToString(tce.Message)
		if i, j := strings.LastIndex(msg, "["), strings.LastIndex(msg, "]"); i >= 0 && j > i {
			for _, c := range strings.Split(msg[i+1:j], ",") {
				codes = append(codes, strings.TrimSpace(c))
			}
		}
	}
	return codes
}
```

`backend/internal/store/articles.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/feature/dynamodb/attributevalue"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
)

const batchGetLimit = 100

func (s *Store) GetArticle(ctx context.Context, inst, hash string) (*Article, error) {
	var a Article
	if err := s.get(ctx, InstPK(inst), ArticleSK(hash), &a); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &a, nil
}

// BatchGetArticles loads ART items keyed by hash, ≤100 keys per call, retrying
// UnprocessedKeys with back-off. Missing articles are absent from the map.
func (s *Store) BatchGetArticles(ctx context.Context, inst string, hashes []string) (map[string]*Article, error) {
	out := map[string]*Article{}
	for start := 0; start < len(hashes); start += batchGetLimit {
		chunk := hashes[start:min(start+batchGetLimit, len(hashes))]
		keys := make([]map[string]types.AttributeValue, 0, len(chunk))
		seen := map[string]bool{}
		for _, h := range chunk {
			if !seen[h] {
				seen[h] = true
				keys = append(keys, key(InstPK(inst), ArticleSK(h)))
			}
		}
		req := map[string]types.KeysAndAttributes{s.table: {Keys: keys}}
		for attempt := 0; len(req) > 0; attempt++ {
			if attempt > 0 {
				if attempt > maxBatchAttempts {
					return nil, fmt.Errorf("store: batch get: keys still unprocessed after %d attempts", maxBatchAttempts)
				}
				s.sleep(backoff(attempt))
			}
			res, err := s.db.BatchGetItem(ctx, &dynamodb.BatchGetItemInput{RequestItems: req})
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			for _, item := range res.Responses[s.table] {
				var a Article
				if err := attributevalue.UnmarshalMap(item, &a); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				out[a.Hash()] = &a
			}
			req = res.UnprocessedKeys
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *Store) SetArticlePage(ctx context.Context, inst, hash, pageID, url string) error {
	return s.update(ctx, InstPK(inst), ArticleSK(hash), "SET #p = :p, #u = :u", "attribute_exists(pk)",
		map[string]string{"#p": "notion_page_id", "#u": "notion_url"},
		map[string]types.AttributeValue{":p": str(pageID), ":u": str(url)})
}

func (s *Store) ClearArticlePage(ctx context.Context, inst, hash string) error {
	return s.update(ctx, InstPK(inst), ArticleSK(hash), "REMOVE #p, #u", "",
		map[string]string{"#p": "notion_page_id", "#u": "notion_url"}, nil)
}

// MarkArticleSynced records the version written to Notion (spec §2.13 step 5).
// An older version never overwrites a newer one.
func (s *Store) MarkArticleSynced(ctx context.Context, inst, hash string, version int64, completed bool, now time.Time) error {
	err := s.update(ctx, InstPK(inst), ArticleSK(hash), "SET #sv = :v, #sa = :now, #c = :c",
		"attribute_exists(pk) AND (attribute_not_exists(#sv) OR #sv < :v)",
		map[string]string{"#sv": "notion_synced_version", "#sa": "notion_synced_at", "#c": "completed"},
		map[string]types.AttributeValue{":v": numI(version), ":now": str(Timestamp(now)), ":c": boolV(completed)})
	if errors.Is(err, ErrConditionFailed) {
		return nil
	}
	return err
}
```

```go
		map[string]string{"#sv": "notion_synced_version"}, map[string]types.AttributeValue{":v": numI(version)})
	if errors.Is(err, ErrConditionFailed) {
```
