`backend/internal/store/maintenance.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/feature/dynamodb/attributevalue"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types"
)

// PendingSync lists ART hashes and DAY dates whose version is ahead of
// notion_synced_version, i.e. never synced or changed since (T22).
func (s *Store) PendingSync(ctx context.Context, inst string) (hashes, dates []string, err error) {
	if hashes, err = s.pending(ctx, inst, "ART#"); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	if dates, err = s.pending(ctx, inst, "DAY#"); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return hashes, dates, nil
}

func (s *Store) pending(ctx context.Context, inst, prefix string) ([]string, error) {
	items, err := s.queryAll(ctx, &dynamodb.QueryInput{
		KeyConditionExpression:    aws.String("pk = :pk AND begins_with(sk, :prefix)"),
		ProjectionExpression:      aws.String("sk, #v, #sv"),
		ExpressionAttributeNames:  map[string]string{"#v": "version", "#sv": "notion_synced_version"},
		ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{":pk": str(InstPK(inst)), ":prefix": str(prefix)},
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []string
	for _, it := range items {
		var row struct {
			SK     string `dynamodbav:"sk"`
			Ver    int64  `dynamodbav:"version"`
			Synced int64  `dynamodbav:"notion_synced_version"`
		}
		if err := attributevalue.UnmarshalMap(it, &row); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if row.Ver > row.Synced {
			out = append(out, strings.TrimPrefix(row.SK, prefix))
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// Key identifies one item.
type Key struct{ PK, SK string }

// PartitionKeys lists the keys of every item in partition pk (purge, T23).
func (s *Store) PartitionKeys(ctx context.Context, pk string) ([]Key, error) {
	items, err := s.queryAll(ctx, &dynamodb.QueryInput{
		KeyConditionExpression:    aws.String("pk = :pk"),
		ProjectionExpression:      aws.String("pk, sk"),
		ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{":pk": str(pk)},
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	keys := make([]Key, len(items))
	for i, it := range items {
		keys[i] = Key{PK: it["pk"].(*types.AttributeValueMemberS).Value, SK: it["sk"].(*types.AttributeValueMemberS).Value}
	}
	return keys, nil
}

const batchWriteLimit = 25

// BatchDelete deletes keys 25 at a time, retrying UnprocessedItems with back-off.
func (s *Store) BatchDelete(ctx context.Context, keys []Key) error {
	for start := 0; start < len(keys); start += batchWriteLimit {
		chunk := keys[start:min(start+batchWriteLimit, len(keys))]
		reqs := make([]types.WriteRequest, len(chunk))
		for i, k := range chunk {
			reqs[i] = types.WriteRequest{DeleteRequest: &types.DeleteRequest{Key: key(k.PK, k.SK)}}
		}
		pending := map[string][]types.WriteRequest{s.table: reqs}
		for attempt := 0; len(pending) > 0; attempt++ {
			if attempt > 0 {
				if attempt > maxBatchAttempts {
					return fmt.Errorf("store: batch delete: items still unprocessed after %d attempts", maxBatchAttempts)
				}
				s.sleep(backoff(attempt))
			}
			res, err := s.db.BatchWriteItem(ctx, &dynamodb.BatchWriteItemInput{RequestItems: pending})
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			pending = res.UnprocessedItems
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// DeleteNotionUserLink deletes NUSER#<id> only while it still points at uid.
func (s *Store) DeleteNotionUserLink(ctx context.Context, notionUserID, uid string) error {
	_, err := s.db.DeleteItem(ctx, &dynamodb.DeleteItemInput{
		TableName:                 &s.table,
		Key:                       key(NUserPK(notionUserID), SKNUser),
		ConditionExpression:       aws.String("#u = :u"),
		ExpressionAttributeNames:  map[string]string{"#u": "user_id"},
		ExpressionAttributeValues: map[string]types.AttributeValue{":u": str(uid)},
	})
	if isConditionFailed(err) {
		return nil
	}
	return err
}
```

`backend/internal/notion/client_test.go`:

```go
package notion

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

func newClient(t *testing.T, h http.HandlerFunc) *Client {
	t.Helper()
	srv := httptest.NewServer(h)
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
	return &Client{BaseURL: srv.URL, Version: "2025-09-03", ClientID: "cid", ClientSecret: "csecret"}
}

// T11 AC: Notion-Version is on every request.
func TestEveryRequestSendsNotionVersion(t *testing.T) {
	var mu sync.Mutex
	var versions []string
	c := newClient(t, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		mu.Lock()
		versions = append(versions, r.Header.Get("Notion-Version"))
		mu.Unlock()
		w.Write([]byte(`{"results":[],"has_more":false,"properties":{},"data_sources":[]}`))
	})
	c.SearchPages(ctx, "tok", "q")
	c.ListBlockChildren(ctx, "tok", "b")
	c.GetDatabase(ctx, "tok", "d")
	c.CreateDatabase(ctx, "tok", "parent", "T", map[string]any{})
	c.GetDataSource(ctx, "tok", "ds")
	c.QueryDataSource(ctx, "tok", "ds", map[string]any{})
	c.CreatePage(ctx, "tok", map[string]any{})
	c.UpdatePage(ctx, "tok", "p", map[string]any{})
	c.ExchangeCode(ctx, "code", "https://x/cb")
	c.RefreshToken(ctx, "rt")
	if len(versions) != 10 {
		t.Fatalf("made %d requests, want 10", len(versions))
	}
	for i, v := range versions {
		if v != "2025-09-03" {
			t.Errorf("request %d Notion-Version = %q", i, v)
		}
	}
}

// T11 AC: 429 with Retry-After: 2 → APIError.RetryAfter = 2s.
func TestRateLimitedErrorCarriesRetryAfter(t *testing.T) {
	c := newClient(t, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.Header().Set("Retry-After", "2")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusTooManyRequests)
		w.Write([]byte(`{"object":"error","status":429,"code":"rate_limited","message":"slow down"}`))
	})
	_, err := c.UpdatePage(ctx, "tok", "p", map[string]any{})
	e, ok := AsAPIError(err)
	if !ok || e.Status != 429 || e.Code != "rate_limited" || e.RetryAfter != 2*time.Second {
		t.Fatalf("got %#v", err)
	}
}

func TestExchangeCodeUsesBasicAuthAndDecodesOwner(t *testing.T) {
	c := newClient(t, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		id, secret, ok := r.BasicAuth()
		var body map[string]string
		json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&body)
		if !ok || id != "cid" || secret != "csecret" || body["grant_type"] != "authorization_code" || body["redirect_uri"] != "https://x/cb" {
			t.Errorf("bad token request: auth=%v %q %q body=%v", ok, id, secret, body)
		}
		w.Write([]byte(`{"access_token":"at","refresh_token":"rt","bot_id":"bot-1","workspace_id":"ws","workspace_name":"WS",
			"workspace_icon":null,"duplicated_template_id":"tpl","owner":{"type":"user","user":{"id":"nu","name":"Tuan","person":{"email":"t@x"}}}}`))
	})
	res, err := c.ExchangeCode(ctx, "code", "https://x/cb")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if res.AccessToken != "at" || res.BotID != "bot-1" || res.DuplicatedTemplateID != "tpl" || res.Owner.User.ID != "nu" || res.Owner.User.Person.Email != "t@x" {
		t.Fatalf("decoded %+v", res)
	}
}

func TestOAuthErrorBodyParsed(t *testing.T) {
	c := newClient(t, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
		w.Write([]byte(`{"error":"invalid_grant","error_description":"refresh token is invalid"}`))
	})
	_, err := c.RefreshToken(ctx, "rt")
	if e, ok := AsAPIError(err); !ok || e.Status != 400 || e.Code != "invalid_grant" {
		t.Fatalf("got %#v", err)
	}
}

func TestListBlockChildrenPaginates(t *testing.T) {
	c := newClient(t, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.URL.Query().Get("start_cursor") == "" {
			w.Write([]byte(`{"results":[{"id":"a","type":"paragraph"},{"id":"b","type":"child_database","child_database":{"title":"Reading Library"}}],"has_more":true,"next_cursor":"c2"}`))
			return
		}
		w.Write([]byte(`{"results":[{"id":"c","type":"child_database","child_database":{"title":"Daily Log"}}],"has_more":false,"next_cursor":null}`))
	})
	blocks, err := c.ListBlockChildren(ctx, "tok", "tpl")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(blocks) != 3 || blocks[1].ChildDatabase.Title != "Reading Library" || blocks[2].ID != "c" {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v", blocks)
	}
}

func TestSearchPagesExtractsTitle(t *testing.T) {
	c := newClient(t, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		var body map[string]any
		json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&body)
		if f := body["filter"].(map[string]any); f["value"] != "page" || body["page_size"].(float64) != 25 {
			t.Errorf("bad search body %v", body)
		}
		w.Write([]byte(`{"results":[{"id":"p1","url":"https://notion.so/p1","properties":{"Name":{"id":"title","type":"title","title":[{"plain_text":"Read"},{"plain_text":"ing"}]}}}]}`))
	})
	pages, err := c.SearchPages(ctx, "tok", "Read")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(pages) != 1 || pages[0].Title != "Reading" || pages[0].URL != "https://notion.so/p1" {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v", pages)
	}
}

func TestBearerTokenSent(t *testing.T) {
	c := newClient(t, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.Header.Get("Authorization") != "Bearer secret-token" {
			t.Errorf("Authorization = %q", r.Header.Get("Authorization"))
		}
		w.Write([]byte(`{"id":"p","url":"u"}`))
	})
	if _, err := c.UpdatePage(ctx, "secret-token", "p", map[string]any{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestMinIntervalPacer(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	var slept []time.Duration
	p := &MinInterval{Interval: 350 * time.Millisecond, now: func() time.Time { return now },
		sleep: func(_ context.Context, d time.Duration) error { slept = append(slept, d); now = now.Add(d); return nil }}
	p.Wait(ctx)
	now = now.Add(100 * time.Millisecond)
	p.Wait(ctx)
	now = now.Add(time.Second)
	p.Wait(ctx)
	if len(slept) != 1 || slept[0] != 250*time.Millisecond {
		t.Fatalf("slept %v, want [250ms]", slept)
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/notion/client.go`:

```go
// Package notion is a thin client for the Notion endpoints the backend uses
// (spec §8), pinned to Notion-Version 2025-09-03 (C10).
package notion

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

var defaultHTTP = &http.Client{Timeout: 15 * time.Second}

// Client calls Notion. Pacer, when set, spaces requests (worker only, C22).
type Client struct {
	BaseURL      string
	Version      string
	ClientID     string
	ClientSecret string
	HTTP         *http.Client
	Pacer        Pacer
}

// Pacer blocks until the next request may be sent.
type Pacer interface {
	Wait(ctx context.Context) error
}

// APIError is any non-2xx Notion response. Code is Notion's "code" or OAuth's "error".
type APIError struct {
	Status     int
	Code       string
	Message    string
	RetryAfter time.Duration
}

func (e *APIError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("notion: %d %s: %s", e.Status, e.Code, e.Message)
}

// AsAPIError unwraps err into *APIError.
func AsAPIError(err error) (*APIError, bool) {
	var e *APIError
	ok := errors.As(err, &e)
	return e, ok
}

type credentials struct {
	bearer string
	basic  bool
}

func bearer(token string) credentials { return credentials{bearer: token} }

var basicAuth = credentials{basic: true}

func (c *Client) do(ctx context.Context, method, path string, cred credentials, body, out any) error {
	if c.Pacer != nil {
		if err := c.Pacer.Wait(ctx); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	var rdr io.Reader
	if body != nil {
		b, err := json.Marshal(body)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rdr = bytes.NewReader(b)
	}
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, method, c.BaseURL+path, rdr)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	req.Header.Set("Notion-Version", c.Version)
	if body != nil {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	}
	if cred.basic {
		req.SetBasicAuth(c.ClientID, c.ClientSecret)
	} else {
		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+cred.bearer)
	}
	hc := c.HTTP
	if hc == nil {
		hc = defaultHTTP
	}
	resp, err := hc.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("notion: %s %s: %w", method, path, err)
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 4<<20))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if resp.StatusCode >= 300 {
		return parseError(resp, data)
	}
	if out == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return json.Unmarshal(data, out)
}

func parseError(resp *http.Response, data []byte) error {
	e := &APIError{Status: resp.StatusCode}
	var body struct {
		Code             string `json:"code"`
		Message          string `json:"message"`
		OAuthError       string `json:"error"`
		ErrorDescription string `json:"error_description"`
	}
	if json.Unmarshal(data, &body) == nil {
		e.Code, e.Message = body.Code, body.Message
		if e.Code == "" {
			e.Code, e.Message = body.OAuthError, body.ErrorDescription
		}
	}
	if secs, err := strconv.Atoi(resp.Header.Get("Retry-After")); err == nil && secs >= 0 {
		e.RetryAfter = time.Duration(secs) * time.Second
	}
	return e
}
```

`backend/internal/notion/oauth.go`:

```go
package notion

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
)

// TokenResponse is the /v1/oauth/token body. ExpiresIn is kept if Notion ever sends it (🔶 §7).
type TokenResponse struct {
	AccessToken          string     `json:"access_token"`
	RefreshToken         string     `json:"refresh_token"`
	BotID                string     `json:"bot_id"`
	WorkspaceID          string     `json:"workspace_id"`
	WorkspaceName        string     `json:"workspace_name"`
	WorkspaceIcon        string     `json:"workspace_icon"`
	DuplicatedTemplateID string     `json:"duplicated_template_id"`
	ExpiresIn            int        `json:"expires_in"`
	Owner                TokenOwner `json:"owner"`
}

type TokenOwner struct {
	User struct {
		ID     string `json:"id"`
		Name   string `json:"name"`
		Person struct {
			Email string `json:"email"`
		} `json:"person"`
	} `json:"user"`
}

// AuthorizeURL is where /v1/oauth/notion/start redirects (spec §2.1 step 3).
func (c *Client) AuthorizeURL(redirectURI, state string) string {
	q := url.Values{
		"client_id": {c.ClientID}, "response_type": {"code"}, "owner": {"user"},
		"redirect_uri": {redirectURI}, "state": {state},
	}
	return c.BaseURL + "/v1/oauth/authorize?" + q.Encode()
}

func (c *Client) ExchangeCode(ctx context.Context, code, redirectURI string) (*TokenResponse, error) {
	return c.token(ctx, map[string]string{"grant_type": "authorization_code", "code": code, "redirect_uri": redirectURI})
}

// RefreshToken exchanges a refresh token. Notion rotates refresh tokens, so callers
// must persist the returned one (C7).
func (c *Client) RefreshToken(ctx context.Context, refreshToken string) (*TokenResponse, error) {
	return c.token(ctx, map[string]string{"grant_type": "refresh_token", "refresh_token": refreshToken})
}

func (c *Client) token(ctx context.Context, body map[string]string) (*TokenResponse, error) {
	var out TokenResponse
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodPost, "/v1/oauth/token", basicAuth, body, &out); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &out, nil
}
```

`backend/internal/notion/api.go`:

```go
package notion

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
)

type PageRef struct {
	ID    string `json:"id"`
	Title string `json:"title"`
	URL   string `json:"url"`
}

// SearchPages lists pages the integration can see (spec §2.6).
func (c *Client) SearchPages(ctx context.Context, token, query string) ([]PageRef, error) {
	body := map[string]any{
		"query":     query,
		"filter":    map[string]string{"property": "object", "value": "page"},
		"page_size": 25,
	}
	var res struct {
		Results []struct {
			ID         string `json:"id"`
			URL        string `json:"url"`
			Properties map[string]struct {
				Type  string `json:"type"`
				Title []struct {
					PlainText string `json:"plain_text"`
				} `json:"title"`
			} `json:"properties"`
		} `json:"results"`
	}
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodPost, "/v1/search", bearer(token), body, &res); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	pages := make([]PageRef, 0, len(res.Results))
	for _, r := range res.Results {
		var title strings.Builder
		for _, p := range r.Properties {
			if p.Type == "title" {
				for _, t := range p.Title {
					title.WriteString(t.PlainText)
				}
			}
		}
		pages = append(pages, PageRef{ID: r.ID, Title: title.String(), URL: r.URL})
	}
	return pages, nil
}

type Block struct {
	ID            string         `json:"id"`
	Type          string         `json:"type"`
	ChildDatabase *ChildDatabase `json:"child_database,omitempty"`
}

type ChildDatabase struct {
	Title string `json:"title"`
}

// ListBlockChildren returns every child block, following start_cursor pagination.
func (c *Client) ListBlockChildren(ctx context.Context, token, blockID string) ([]Block, error) {
	var all []Block
	cursor := ""
	for {
		path := "/v1/blocks/" + url.PathEscape(blockID) + "/children?page_size=100"
		if cursor != "" {
			path += "&start_cursor=" + url.QueryEscape(cursor)
		}
		var res struct {
			Results    []Block `json:"results"`
			HasMore    bool    `json:"has_more"`
			NextCursor string  `json:"next_cursor"`
		}
		if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodGet, path, bearer(token), nil, &res); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		all = append(all, res.Results...)
		if !res.HasMore || res.NextCursor == "" {
			return all, nil
		}
		cursor = res.NextCursor
	}
}

type DataSourceRef struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

type Database struct {
	ID          string          `json:"id"`
	URL         string          `json:"url"`
	DataSources []DataSourceRef `json:"data_sources"`
}

func (c *Client) GetDatabase(ctx context.Context, token, id string) (*Database, error) {
	var db Database
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodGet, "/v1/databases/"+url.PathEscape(id), bearer(token), nil, &db); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &db, nil
}

// CreateDatabase creates a database with one initial data source (2025-09-03 shape).
func (c *Client) CreateDatabase(ctx context.Context, token, parentPageID, title string, properties map[string]any) (*Database, error) {
	body := map[string]any{
		"parent":              map[string]any{"type": "page_id", "page_id": parentPageID},
		"title":               []any{map[string]any{"type": "text", "text": map[string]any{"content": title}}},
		"initial_data_source": map[string]any{"properties": properties},
	}
	var db Database
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodPost, "/v1/databases", bearer(token), body, &db); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &db, nil
}

type Property struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
	Type string `json:"type"`
}

// DataSource.Properties is keyed by property name.
type DataSource struct {
	ID         string              `json:"id"`
	Properties map[string]Property `json:"properties"`
}

func (c *Client) GetDataSource(ctx context.Context, token, id string) (*DataSource, error) {
	var ds DataSource
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodGet, "/v1/data_sources/"+url.PathEscape(id), bearer(token), nil, &ds); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &ds, nil
}

type Page struct {
	ID       string `json:"id"`
	URL      string `json:"url"`
	Archived bool   `json:"archived"`
	InTrash  bool   `json:"in_trash"`
}

type QueryResult struct {
	Results []Page `json:"results"`
}

func (c *Client) QueryDataSource(ctx context.Context, token, id string, body map[string]any) (*QueryResult, error) {
	var res QueryResult
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodPost, "/v1/data_sources/"+url.PathEscape(id)+"/query", bearer(token), body, &res); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &res, nil
}

func (c *Client) CreatePage(ctx context.Context, token string, body map[string]any) (*Page, error) {
	var p Page
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodPost, "/v1/pages", bearer(token), body, &p); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &p, nil
}

// UpdatePage PATCHes only the given properties (keyed by property id).
func (c *Client) UpdatePage(ctx context.Context, token, pageID string, properties map[string]any) (*Page, error) {
	var p Page
	body := map[string]any{"properties": properties}
	if err := c.do(ctx, http.MethodPatch, "/v1/pages/"+url.PathEscape(pageID), bearer(token), body, &p); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &p, nil
}
```

`backend/internal/notion/pacer.go`:

```go
package notion

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// MinInterval spaces calls at least Interval apart (C22: ≥ 350 ms between Notion calls).
type MinInterval struct {
	Interval time.Duration
	mu       sync.Mutex
	last     time.Time
	now      func() time.Time
	sleep    func(context.Context, time.Duration) error
}

func NewMinInterval(d time.Duration) *MinInterval {
	return &MinInterval{Interval: d, now: time.Now, sleep: sleepCtx}
}

func (p *MinInterval) Wait(ctx context.Context) error {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()
	if !p.last.IsZero() {
		if d := p.Interval - p.now().Sub(p.last); d > 0 {
			if err := p.sleep(ctx, d); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
	p.last = p.now()
	return nil
}

func sleepCtx(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error {
	t := time.NewTimer(d)
	defer t.Stop()
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	case <-t.C:
		return nil
	}
}
```

### Task 6: Queue messages, dedup IDs and senders (spec T18)

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/internal/queue/message.go`
- Create: `backend/internal/queue/sqs.go`
- Create: `backend/internal/queue/recorder.go`
- Create: `backend/internal/queue/local.go`
- Test: `backend/internal/queue/queue_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces:
  - `queue.Type` with values `TypeArticle`, `TypeDay` and `TypePurge`; `queue.Message{Type; InstallationID, URLHash, Date, UserID string}` (JSON per §2.12)
  - Constructors `queue.Article(inst, hash)`, `queue.Day(inst, date)` and `queue.Purge(userID, inst)`
  - `(Message).GroupID() string`, `(Message).DedupID(now time.Time, bucket time.Duration) string`, `(Message).RequeueDedupID(now time.Time) string`
  - `queue.Outgoing{Message Message; DedupID string}`; `queue.Bucketed(now, bucket, msgs ...Message) []Outgoing`
  - `queue.Sender` interface `Send(ctx, msgs ...Outgoing) error`
  - `queue.SQS{API SQSAPI; QueueURL string}` implements `Sender` and `ChangeVisibility(ctx, receiptHandle string, d time.Duration) error`
  - `queue.Recorder` (test sender) with `Send`, `Drain() []Outgoing`, `Messages() []Message`, and an `Err` field
  - `queue.Local{Delay time.Duration; Handle func(ctx, events.SQSEvent) (events.SQSEventResponse, error); Log *slog.Logger}` implements `Sender` and `ChangeVisibility`

`backend/internal/queue/queue_test.go`:

```go
package queue

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/events"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/sqs"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/sqs/types"
)

const (
	bot  = "b7c1e0f2-1111-2222-3333-444455556666"
	hash = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef"
)

// T18 AC: same bucket → identical IDs; different bucket → different.
func TestDedupIDBuckets(t *testing.T) {
	bucket := 120 * time.Second
	start := time.Unix(1000*120, 0)
	m := Article(bot, hash)
	a := m.DedupID(start.Add(5*time.Second), bucket)
	b := m.DedupID(start.Add(119*time.Second), bucket)
	c := m.DedupID(start.Add(120*time.Second), bucket)
	if a != b {
		t.Fatalf("same bucket differs: %q %q", a, b)
	}
	if a == c {
		t.Fatal("next bucket must differ")
	}
	if a != "article#"+bot+"#"+hash+"#1000" {
		t.Fatalf("format: %q", a)
	}
	if d := Day(bot, "2026-10-06").DedupID(start, bucket); d != "day#"+bot+"#2026-10-06#1000" {
		t.Fatalf("day format: %q", d)
	}
}

// Review focus 3: a worker re-enqueue inside the same bucket must not be deduplicated away.
func TestRequeueDedupIDNeverCollides(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Unix(1000*120+5, 0)
	m := Article(bot, hash)
	r := m.RequeueDedupID(now)
	if r == m.DedupID(now, 120*time.Second) || len(r) > 128 || r == m.RequeueDedupID(now.Add(time.Nanosecond)) {
		t.Fatalf("requeue id %q collides or is too long", r)
	}
}

func TestGroupIDs(t *testing.T) {
	if g := Article(bot, hash).GroupID(); g != bot {
		t.Fatalf("article group %q", g)
	}
	if g := Purge("u1", "").GroupID(); g != "user#u1" {
		t.Fatalf("purge group without installation %q", g)
	}
	if g := Purge("u1", bot).GroupID(); g != bot {
		t.Fatalf("purge group with installation %q", g)
	}
	if id := Purge("u1", bot).DedupID(time.Unix(42, 0), time.Minute); id != "purge#u1#42" {
		t.Fatalf("purge dedup %q", id)
	}
}

func TestMessageJSONShape(t *testing.T) {
	b, _ := json.Marshal(Article(bot, hash))
	if string(b) != `{"type":"article","installation_id":"`+bot+`","url_hash":"`+hash+`"}` {
		t.Fatalf("article json %s", b)
	}
	b, _ = json.Marshal(Purge("u1", ""))
	if string(b) != `{"type":"purge","installation_id":"","user_id":"u1"}` {
		t.Fatalf("purge json %s", b)
	}
}

type fakeSQS struct {
	batches [][]types.SendMessageBatchRequestEntry
	failIDs map[string]bool
	vis     []int32
}

func (f *fakeSQS) SendMessageBatch(_ context.Context, in *sqs.SendMessageBatchInput, _ ...func(*sqs.Options)) (*sqs.SendMessageBatchOutput, error) {
	f.batches = append(f.batches, in.Entries)
	out := &sqs.SendMessageBatchOutput{}
	for _, e := range in.Entries {
		if f.failIDs[aws.ToString(e.MessageDeduplicationId)] {
			out.Failed = append(out.Failed, types.BatchResultErrorEntry{Id: e.Id, Code: aws.String("InternalError")})
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeSQS) ChangeMessageVisibility(_ context.Context, in *sqs.ChangeMessageVisibilityInput, _ ...func(*sqs.Options)) (*sqs.ChangeMessageVisibilityOutput, error) {
	f.vis = append(f.vis, in.VisibilityTimeout)
	return &sqs.ChangeMessageVisibilityOutput{}, nil
}

func TestSQSSendBatchesOfTen(t *testing.T) {
	f := &fakeSQS{}
	q := &SQS{API: f, QueueURL: "https://sqs/q.fifo"}
	var msgs []Message
	for i := 0; i < 23; i++ {
		msgs = append(msgs, Day(bot, time.Unix(int64(i)*86400, 0).UTC().Format("2006-01-02")))
	}
	if err := q.Send(context.Background(), Bucketed(time.Unix(0, 0), time.Minute, msgs...)...); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(f.batches) != 3 || len(f.batches[0]) != 10 || len(f.batches[2]) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("batches %d", len(f.batches))
	}
	e := f.batches[0][0]
	if aws.ToString(e.MessageGroupId) != bot || !strings.HasPrefix(aws.ToString(e.MessageDeduplicationId), "day#"+bot+"#") ||
		!strings.Contains(aws.ToString(e.MessageBody), `"type":"day"`) {
		t.Fatalf("bad entry %+v", e)
	}
}

func TestSQSSendReportsFailedEntries(t *testing.T) {
	m := Article(bot, hash)
	o := Outgoing{Message: m, DedupID: "x"}
	q := &SQS{API: &fakeSQS{failIDs: map[string]bool{"x": true}}, QueueURL: "q"}
	if err := q.Send(context.Background(), o); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("failed entries must surface as an error")
	}
}

func TestSQSChangeVisibilityRoundsUp(t *testing.T) {
	f := &fakeSQS{}
	q := &SQS{API: f, QueueURL: "q"}
	q.ChangeVisibility(context.Background(), "rh", 5500*time.Millisecond)
	if len(f.vis) != 1 || f.vis[0] != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("visibility %v, want [6]", f.vis)
	}
}

func TestLocalQueueDedupsAndDelivers(t *testing.T) {
	var calls atomic.Int32
	done := make(chan struct{}, 4)
	l := &Local{Delay: 10 * time.Millisecond, Log: slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)),
		Handle: func(_ context.Context, ev events.SQSEvent) (events.SQSEventResponse, error) {
			calls.Add(1)
			done <- struct{}{}
			return events.SQSEventResponse{}, nil
		}}
	o := Bucketed(time.Unix(0, 0), time.Minute, Article(bot, hash))
	l.Send(context.Background(), o...)
	l.Send(context.Background(), o...)
	<-done
	time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)
	if calls.Load() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("delivered %d times, want 1", calls.Load())
	}
}

func TestRecorder(t *testing.T) {
	r := &Recorder{}
	r.Send(context.Background(), Bucketed(time.Unix(0, 0), time.Minute, Article(bot, hash), Day(bot, "2026-10-06"))...)
	if got := r.Messages(); len(got) != 2 || got[1].Type != TypeDay {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v", got)
	}
	if d := r.Drain(); len(d) != 2 || len(r.Messages()) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("Drain must empty the recorder")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the queue package**

`backend/internal/queue/message.go`:

```go
// Package queue builds notion-sync.fifo messages (spec §2.12) and sends them.
package queue

import (
	"context"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

type Type string

const (
	TypeArticle Type = "article"
	TypeDay     Type = "day"
	TypePurge   Type = "purge"
)

// Message is the JSON body of a queue message.
type Message struct {
	Type           Type   `json:"type"`
	InstallationID string `json:"installation_id"`
	URLHash        string `json:"url_hash,omitempty"`
	Date           string `json:"date,omitempty"`
	UserID         string `json:"user_id,omitempty"`
}

func Article(inst, urlHash string) Message {
	return Message{Type: TypeArticle, InstallationID: inst, URLHash: urlHash}
}
func Day(inst, date string) Message { return Message{Type: TypeDay, InstallationID: inst, Date: date} }
func Purge(userID, inst string) Message {
	return Message{Type: TypePurge, InstallationID: inst, UserID: userID}
}

// GroupID serialises all work for one installation (C4).
func (m Message) GroupID() string {
	if m.InstallationID != "" {
		return m.InstallationID
	}
	return "user#" + m.UserID
}

func (m Message) key() string {
	switch m.Type {
	case TypeArticle:
		return "article#" + m.InstallationID + "#" + m.URLHash
	case TypeDay:
		return "day#" + m.InstallationID + "#" + m.Date
	default:
		return "purge#" + m.UserID
	}
}

// DedupID collapses article/day messages per debounce bucket B; purge per second.
func (m Message) DedupID(now time.Time, bucket time.Duration) string {
	if m.Type == TypePurge {
		return m.key() + "#" + strconv.FormatInt(now.Unix(), 10)
	}
	b := max(int64(bucket/time.Second), 1)
	return m.key() + "#" + strconv.FormatInt(now.Unix()/b, 10)
}

// RequeueDedupID never collides with DedupID, so a worker re-enqueue inside the
// same bucket isn't swallowed by SQS's 5-minute dedup window.
func (m Message) RequeueDedupID(now time.Time) string {
	return m.key() + "#r" + strconv.FormatInt(now.UnixNano(), 10)
}

// Outgoing is a message plus its deduplication id.
type Outgoing struct {
	Message Message
	DedupID string
}

// Bucketed pairs each message with its bucket dedup id.
func Bucketed(now time.Time, bucket time.Duration, msgs ...Message) []Outgoing {
	out := make([]Outgoing, len(msgs))
	for i, m := range msgs {
		out[i] = Outgoing{Message: m, DedupID: m.DedupID(now, bucket)}
	}
	return out
}

// Sender enqueues messages.
type Sender interface {
	Send(ctx context.Context, msgs ...Outgoing) error
}
```

`backend/internal/queue/sqs.go`:

```go
package queue

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/sqs"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/sqs/types"
)

// SQSAPI is the subset of *sqs.Client used here.
type SQSAPI interface {
	SendMessageBatch(context.Context, *sqs.SendMessageBatchInput, ...func(*sqs.Options)) (*sqs.SendMessageBatchOutput, error)
	ChangeMessageVisibility(context.Context, *sqs.ChangeMessageVisibilityInput, ...func(*sqs.Options)) (*sqs.ChangeMessageVisibilityOutput, error)
}

// SQS sends to the notion-sync FIFO queue.
type SQS struct {
	API      SQSAPI
	QueueURL string
}

// Send uses SendMessageBatch, 10 entries per call. Any failed entry is an error.
func (q *SQS) Send(ctx context.Context, msgs ...Outgoing) error {
	for start := 0; start < len(msgs); start += 10 {
		chunk := msgs[start:min(start+10, len(msgs))]
		entries := make([]types.SendMessageBatchRequestEntry, len(chunk))
		for i, o := range chunk {
			body, err := json.Marshal(o.Message)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			entries[i] = types.SendMessageBatchRequestEntry{
				Id:                     aws.String(strconv.Itoa(i)),
				MessageBody:            aws.String(string(body)),
				MessageGroupId:         aws.String(o.Message.GroupID()),
				MessageDeduplicationId: aws.String(o.DedupID),
			}
		}
		out, err := q.API.SendMessageBatch(ctx, &sqs.SendMessageBatchInput{QueueUrl: aws.String(q.QueueURL), Entries: entries})
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("queue: send: %w", err)
		}
		if len(out.Failed) > 0 {
			return fmt.Errorf("queue: %d of %d messages failed (%s)", len(out.Failed), len(entries), aws.ToString(out.Failed[0].Code))
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// ChangeVisibility hides a message for d, rounded up to whole seconds (Notion 429, spec §2.15).
func (q *SQS) ChangeVisibility(ctx context.Context, receiptHandle string, d time.Duration) error {
	secs := int32(min(math.Ceil(d.Seconds()), 43200))
	_, err := q.API.ChangeMessageVisibility(ctx, &sqs.ChangeMessageVisibilityInput{
		QueueUrl: aws.String(q.QueueURL), ReceiptHandle: aws.String(receiptHandle), VisibilityTimeout: secs,
	})
	return err
}
```

`backend/internal/queue/recorder.go`:

```go
package queue

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
)

// Recorder is an in-memory Sender for tests. Set Err to make Send fail.
type Recorder struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	sent []Outgoing
	Err  error
}

func (r *Recorder) Send(_ context.Context, msgs ...Outgoing) error {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	if r.Err != nil {
		return r.Err
	}
	r.sent = append(r.sent, msgs...)
	return nil
}

// Messages returns the bodies sent so far.
func (r *Recorder) Messages() []Message {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	out := make([]Message, len(r.sent))
	for i, o := range r.sent {
		out[i] = o.Message
	}
	return out
}

// Drain returns everything sent so far and clears the recorder.
func (r *Recorder) Drain() []Outgoing {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	out := r.sent
	r.sent = nil
	return out
}
```

`backend/internal/queue/local.go`:

```go
package queue

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"log/slog"
	"strconv"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/events"
)

// Local emulates the FIFO queue in-process for cmd/local: a 5-minute dedup window,
// a delivery delay, one message at a time, and redelivery after failures.
type Local struct {
	Delay  time.Duration
	Handle func(context.Context, events.SQSEvent) (events.SQSEventResponse, error)
	Log    *slog.Logger

	mu   sync.Mutex
	seen map[string]time.Time
	run  sync.Mutex
	seq  atomic.Int64
}

func (l *Local) Send(_ context.Context, msgs ...Outgoing) error {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	if l.seen == nil {
		l.seen = map[string]time.Time{}
	}
	now := time.Now()
	for _, o := range msgs {
		if t, ok := l.seen[o.DedupID]; ok && now.Sub(t) < 5*time.Minute {
			continue
		}
		l.seen[o.DedupID] = now
		body, err := json.Marshal(o.Message)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		l.schedule(string(body), o.Message.GroupID(), l.Delay, 1)
	}
	return nil
}

func (l *Local) schedule(body, group string, delay time.Duration, attempt int) {
	id := strconv.FormatInt(l.seq.Add(1), 10)
	time.AfterFunc(delay, func() {
		l.run.Lock()
		defer l.run.Unlock()
		ev := events.SQSEvent{Records: []events.SQSMessage{{
			MessageId: id, ReceiptHandle: id, Body: body, Attributes: map[string]string{"MessageGroupId": group},
		}}}
		resp, err := l.Handle(context.Background(), ev)
		if err == nil && len(resp.BatchItemFailures) == 0 {
			return
		}
		if attempt >= 10 {
			l.Log.Error("local queue: dropping message after 10 attempts", "body", body)
			return
		}
		l.schedule(body, group, 30*time.Second, attempt+1)
	})
}

// ChangeVisibility is a no-op locally; failed messages are retried after 30 s.
func (l *Local) ChangeVisibility(context.Context, string, time.Duration) error { return nil }
```

`backend/internal/setup/schema.go`:

```go
package setup

// Database titles the template must contain (spec §2.11).
const (
	LibraryTitle = "Reading Library"
	DailyTitle   = "Daily Log"
)

// PropSpec is one server-owned property: logical key, column name and Notion type.
type PropSpec struct {
	Key  string
	Name string
	Type string
}

// LibraryProps are the server-owned Reading Library properties (spec §3.3).
// User-owned columns (Tags, Status, Rating, Notes) are created by setup but never
// validated or written (C1).
var LibraryProps = []PropSpec{
	{"title", "Name", "title"},
	{"url", "URL", "url"},
	{"source", "Source", "select"},
	{"author", "Author", "rich_text"},
	{"site", "Site", "select"},
	{"word_count", "Word count", "number"},
	{"first_read", "First read", "date"},
	{"last_read", "Last read", "date"},
	{"minutes", "Reading time (min)", "number"},
	{"progress", "Progress", "number"},
	{"sessions", "Sessions", "number"},
	{"completed", "Completed", "checkbox"},
}

// DailyProps are the Daily Log properties (all server-owned).
var DailyProps = []PropSpec{
	{"day_title", "Name", "title"},
	{"day_date", "Date", "date"},
	{"day_minutes", "Minutes", "number"},
	{"day_articles", "Articles", "relation"},
}

func libraryCreateSchema() map[string]any {
	empty := map[string]any{}
	number := func(format string) map[string]any { return map[string]any{"number": map[string]any{"format": format}} }
	status := func(name, group string) map[string]any { return map[string]any{"name": name, "group": group} }
	var stars []any
	for _, s := range []string{"⭐", "⭐⭐", "⭐⭐⭐", "⭐⭐⭐⭐", "⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐"} {
		stars = append(stars, map[string]any{"name": s})
	}
	return map[string]any{
		"Name":               map[string]any{"title": empty},
		"URL":                map[string]any{"url": empty},
		"Source":             map[string]any{"select": empty},
		"Author":             map[string]any{"rich_text": empty},
		"Site":               map[string]any{"select": empty},
		"Word count":         number("number"),
		"First read":         map[string]any{"date": empty},
		"Last read":          map[string]any{"date": empty},
		"Reading time (min)": number("number"),
		"Progress":           number("percent"),
		"Sessions":           number("number"),
		"Completed":          map[string]any{"checkbox": empty},
		"Tags":               map[string]any{"multi_select": empty},
		"Status": map[string]any{"status": map[string]any{"options": []any{
			status("Not started", "To-do"), status("Read later", "To-do"), status("Reading", "In progress"),
			status("Done", "Complete"), status("Abandoned", "Complete"),
		}}},
		"Rating": map[string]any{"select": map[string]any{"options": stars}},
		"Notes":  map[string]any{"rich_text": empty},
	}
}

func dailyCreateSchema(libraryDSID string) map[string]any {
	empty := map[string]any{}
	return map[string]any{
		"Name":    map[string]any{"title": empty},
		"Date":    map[string]any{"date": empty},
		"Minutes": map[string]any{"number": map[string]any{"format": "number"}},
		"Articles": map[string]any{"relation": map[string]any{
			"data_source_id": libraryDSID, "type": "single_property", "single_property": empty,
		}},
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/notion/notiontest/fake.go`:

```go
// Package notiontest is an in-memory fake of the Notion endpoints the backend uses.
// It enforces Notion-Version, keys page properties by property id, and can inject
// failures, rejected tokens, archived pages and deleted data sources.
package notiontest

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/setup"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

type Request struct {
	Method string
	Path   string
	Query  string
	Auth   string
	Body   map[string]any
}

type Fail struct {
	Status     int
	Code       string
	Message    string
	RetryAfter int // seconds; 0 = no header
	Times      int // matching calls to fail; 0 means 1
}

type Page struct {
	ID         string
	URL        string
	DataSource string
	Properties map[string]any // keyed by property id
	Children   []any
	Archived   bool
}

func (p *Page) json() map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{"object": "page", "id": p.ID, "url": p.URL, "archived": p.Archived, "in_trash": p.Archived, "properties": p.Properties}
}

// LibrarySchema and DailySchema are the template's columns (name → type) per spec §3.3.
var LibrarySchema = map[string]string{
	"Name": "title", "URL": "url", "Source": "select", "Author": "rich_text", "Site": "select",
	"Word count": "number", "First read": "date", "Last read": "date", "Reading time (min)": "number",
	"Progress": "number", "Sessions": "number", "Completed": "checkbox",
	"Tags": "multi_select", "Status": "status", "Rating": "select", "Notes": "rich_text",
}

var DailySchema = map[string]string{"Name": "title", "Date": "date", "Minutes": "number", "Articles": "relation"}

// PropID is the fake's property id for a column name.
func PropID(name string) string { return "id-" + strings.ReplaceAll(strings.ToLower(name), " ", "-") }

type Template struct{ ID, LibraryDB, LibraryDS, DailyDB, DailyDS string }

// DBConfig is what template resolution should produce for this template.
func (t Template) DBConfig() store.DBConfig {
	props := map[string]string{}
	for _, p := range setup.LibraryProps {
		props[p.Key] = PropID(p.Name)
	}
	for _, p := range setup.DailyProps {
		props[p.Key] = PropID(p.Name)
	}
	return store.DBConfig{LibraryDBID: t.LibraryDB, LibraryDSID: t.LibraryDS, LibraryURL: "https://www.notion.so/" + t.LibraryDB,
		DailyDBID: t.DailyDB, DailyDSID: t.DailyDS, Props: props}
}

type Server struct {
	*httptest.Server

	mu              sync.Mutex
	reqs            []Request
	fails           map[string]*Fail
	rejected        map[string]bool
	tokenResponse   map[string]any
	refreshResponse map[string]any
	searchResults   []map[string]any
	blocks          map[string][]map[string]any
	databases       map[string]map[string]any
	dataSources     map[string]map[string]any
	pages           map[string]*Page
	order           []string
	seq             int
}

func New(t testing.TB) *Server {
	s := &Server{
		fails: map[string]*Fail{}, rejected: map[string]bool{}, blocks: map[string][]map[string]any{},
		databases: map[string]map[string]any{}, dataSources: map[string]map[string]any{}, pages: map[string]*Page{},
	}
	s.Server = httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(s.serve))
	t.Cleanup(s.Close)
	return s
}

// ChildDatabase builds a child_database block.
func ChildDatabase(id, title string) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{"object": "block", "id": id, "type": "child_database", "child_database": map[string]any{"title": title}}
}

// AddTemplate registers a duplicated template page holding both databases.
func (s *Server) AddTemplate(id string) Template {
	tpl := Template{ID: id, LibraryDB: id + "-lib-db", LibraryDS: id + "-lib-ds", DailyDB: id + "-daily-db", DailyDS: id + "-daily-ds"}
	s.AddDatabase(tpl.LibraryDB, tpl.LibraryDS, LibrarySchema)
	s.AddDatabase(tpl.DailyDB, tpl.DailyDS, DailySchema)
	s.SetBlocks(id, map[string]any{"object": "block", "id": "intro", "type": "paragraph"},
		ChildDatabase(tpl.LibraryDB, setup.LibraryTitle), ChildDatabase(tpl.DailyDB, setup.DailyTitle))
	return tpl
}

func (s *Server) AddDatabase(dbID, dsID string, schema map[string]string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.addDatabaseLocked(dbID, dsID, schema)
}

func (s *Server) addDatabaseLocked(dbID, dsID string, schema map[string]string) map[string]any {
	props := map[string]any{}
	for name, typ := range schema {
		props[name] = map[string]any{"id": PropID(name), "name": name, "type": typ}
	}
	s.dataSources[dsID] = map[string]any{"object": "data_source", "id": dsID, "properties": props}
	db := map[string]any{"object": "database", "id": dbID, "url": "https://www.notion.so/" + dbID,
		"data_sources": []any{map[string]any{"id": dsID, "name": "Default"}}}
	s.databases[dbID] = db
	return db
}

func (s *Server) SetBlocks(parentID string, blocks ...map[string]any) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.blocks[parentID] = blocks
}

func (s *Server) RemoveProperty(dsID, name string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	delete(s.dataSources[dsID]["properties"].(map[string]any), name)
}

func (s *Server) SetPropertyType(dsID, name, typ string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.dataSources[dsID]["properties"].(map[string]any)[name] = map[string]any{"id": PropID(name), "name": name, "type": typ}
}

// DeleteDataSource simulates the user deleting a database (scenario 10).
func (s *Server) DeleteDataSource(dsID string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	delete(s.dataSources, dsID)
}

// AddPage inserts a page as if a user (or an earlier attempt) had created it.
func (s *Server) AddPage(dsID string, props map[string]any) *Page {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return s.newPageLocked(dsID, props)
}

func (s *Server) Page(id string) *Page {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return s.pages[id]
}

// Pages lists pages of a data source in creation order.
func (s *Server) Pages(dsID string) []*Page {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	var out []*Page
	for _, id := range s.order {
		if p := s.pages[id]; p.DataSource == dsID {
			out = append(out, p)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (s *Server) ArchivePage(id string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.pages[id].Archived = true
}

// SetPageProperty simulates a user editing a column in Notion.
func (s *Server) SetPageProperty(pageID, propID string, v any) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.pages[pageID].Properties[propID] = v
}

// RejectToken makes every request bearing tok fail with 401.
func (s *Server) RejectToken(tok string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.rejected[tok] = true
}

// FailNext makes the next f.Times calls to method+path return f.
func (s *Server) FailNext(method, path string, f Fail) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if f.Times == 0 {
		f.Times = 1
	}
	s.fails[method+" "+path] = &f
}

func (s *Server) SetTokenResponse(r map[string]any) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.tokenResponse = r
}

// SetRefreshResponse sets the refresh_token grant response; nil → 400 invalid_grant.
func (s *Server) SetRefreshResponse(r map[string]any) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.refreshResponse = r
}

func (s *Server) SetSearchResults(results ...map[string]any) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.searchResults = results
}

func (s *Server) Requests() []Request {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]Request(nil), s.reqs...)
}

// Calls filters Requests by method and path prefix.
func (s *Server) Calls(method, pathPrefix string) []Request {
	var out []Request
	for _, r := range s.Requests() {
		if r.Method == method && strings.HasPrefix(r.Path, pathPrefix) {
			out = append(out, r)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (s *Server) serve(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	var body map[string]any
	_ = json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&body)
	s.reqs = append(s.reqs, Request{Method: r.Method, Path: r.URL.Path, Query: r.URL.RawQuery, Auth: r.Header.Get("Authorization"), Body: body})

	if r.Header.Get("Notion-Version") != "2025-09-03" {
		writeErr(w, Fail{Status: 400, Code: "missing_version", Message: "Notion-Version must be 2025-09-03"})
		return
	}
	if f := s.takeFail(r.Method + " " + r.URL.Path); f != nil {
		writeErr(w, *f)
		return
	}
	if tok, ok := strings.CutPrefix(r.Header.Get("Authorization"), "Bearer "); ok && s.rejected[tok] {
		writeErr(w, Fail{Status: 401, Code: "unauthorized", Message: "API token is invalid."})
		return
	}
	p := strings.Split(strings.Trim(r.URL.Path, "/"), "/")
	switch {
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && r.URL.Path == "/v1/oauth/token":
		s.token(w, body)
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && r.URL.Path == "/v1/search":
		writeJSON(w, map[string]any{"object": "list", "results": s.searchResults, "has_more": false})
	case r.Method == http.MethodGet && len(p) == 4 && p[1] == "blocks" && p[3] == "children":
		blocks, ok := s.blocks[p[2]]
		if !ok {
			writeErr(w, notFound("block"))
			return
		}
		writeJSON(w, map[string]any{"object": "list", "results": blocks, "has_more": false, "next_cursor": nil})
	case r.Method == http.MethodGet && len(p) == 3 && p[1] == "databases":
		db, ok := s.databases[p[2]]
		if !ok {
			writeErr(w, notFound("database"))
			return
		}
		writeJSON(w, db)
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && r.URL.Path == "/v1/databases":
		s.createDatabase(w, body)
	case r.Method == http.MethodGet && len(p) == 3 && p[1] == "data_sources":
		ds, ok := s.dataSources[p[2]]
		if !ok {
			writeErr(w, notFound("data source"))
			return
		}
		writeJSON(w, ds)
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && len(p) == 4 && p[1] == "data_sources" && p[3] == "query":
		s.query(w, p[2], body)
	case r.Method == http.MethodPost && r.URL.Path == "/v1/pages":
		s.createPage(w, body)
	case r.Method == http.MethodPatch && len(p) == 3 && p[1] == "pages":
		s.updatePage(w, p[2], body)
	default:
		writeErr(w, Fail{Status: 400, Code: "fake_unhandled", Message: r.Method + " " + r.URL.Path})
	}
}

func (s *Server) takeFail(k string) *Fail {
	f := s.fails[k]
	if f == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if f.Times--; f.Times <= 0 {
		delete(s.fails, k)
	}
	return f
}

func (s *Server) token(w http.ResponseWriter, body map[string]any) {
	var resp map[string]any
	switch body["grant_type"] {
	case "authorization_code":
		resp = s.tokenResponse
	case "refresh_token":
		resp = s.refreshResponse
	}
	if resp == nil {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{"error": "invalid_grant", "error_description": "grant is invalid"})
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, resp)
}

func (s *Server) createDatabase(w http.ResponseWriter, body map[string]any) {
	parent, _ := body["parent"].(map[string]any)
	if _, ok := parent["page_id"].(string); !ok {
		writeErr(w, Fail{Status: 400, Code: "validation_error", Message: "parent.page_id is required"})
		return
	}
	s.seq++
	dbID, dsID := fmt.Sprintf("db-%d", s.seq), fmt.Sprintf("ds-%d", s.seq)
	ids, _ := body["initial_data_source"].(map[string]any)
	props, _ := ids["properties"].(map[string]any)
	schema := map[string]string{}
	for name, cfg := range props {
		for k := range cfg.(map[string]any) {
			if k != "type" && k != "name" {
				schema[name] = k
			}
		}
	}
	writeJSON(w, s.addDatabaseLocked(dbID, dsID, schema))
}

func (s *Server) query(w http.ResponseWriter, dsID string, body map[string]any) {
	if _, ok := s.dataSources[dsID]; !ok {
		writeErr(w, notFound("data source"))
		return
	}
	filter, _ := body["filter"].(map[string]any)
	prop, _ := filter["property"].(string)
	results := []any{}
	for _, id := range s.order {
		pg := s.pages[id]
		if pg.DataSource == dsID && !pg.Archived && matches(pg.Properties[prop], filter) {
			results = append(results, pg.json())
		}
	}
	writeJSON(w, map[string]any{"object": "list", "results": results, "has_more": false})
}

func matches(val any, filter map[string]any) bool {
	v, _ := val.(map[string]any)
	if v == nil {
		return false
	}
	if f, ok := filter["url"].(map[string]any); ok {
		return v["url"] == f["equals"]
	}
	if f, ok := filter["date"].(map[string]any); ok {
		d, _ := v["date"].(map[string]any)
		return d != nil && d["start"] == f["equals"]
	}
	return false
}

func (s *Server) createPage(w http.ResponseWriter, body map[string]any) {
	parent, _ := body["parent"].(map[string]any)
	dsID, _ := parent["data_source_id"].(string)
	if parent["type"] != "data_source_id" {
		writeErr(w, Fail{Status: 400, Code: "validation_error", Message: "parent.type must be data_source_id"})
		return
	}
	if _, ok := s.dataSources[dsID]; !ok {
		writeErr(w, notFound("data source"))
		return
	}
	props, _ := body["properties"].(map[string]any)
	pg := s.newPageLocked(dsID, props)
	pg.Children, _ = body["children"].([]any)
	writeJSON(w, pg.json())
}

func (s *Server) newPageLocked(dsID string, props map[string]any) *Page {
	s.seq++
	id := fmt.Sprintf("page-%d", s.seq)
	if props == nil {
		props = map[string]any{}
	}
	pg := &Page{ID: id, URL: "https://www.notion.so/" + id, DataSource: dsID, Properties: props}
	s.pages[id] = pg
	s.order = append(s.order, id)
	return pg
}

func (s *Server) updatePage(w http.ResponseWriter, id string, body map[string]any) {
	pg, ok := s.pages[id]
	if !ok {
		writeErr(w, notFound("page"))
		return
	}
	if pg.Archived {
		writeErr(w, Fail{Status: 400, Code: "validation_error", Message: "Can't edit block that is archived. You must unarchive the block before editing."})
		return
	}
	props, _ := body["properties"].(map[string]any)
	for k, v := range props {
		pg.Properties[k] = v
	}
	writeJSON(w, pg.json())
}

func notFound(what string) Fail {
	return Fail{Status: 404, Code: "object_not_found", Message: "Could not find " + what}
}

func writeErr(w http.ResponseWriter, f Fail) {
	if f.RetryAfter > 0 {
		w.Header().Set("Retry-After", strconv.Itoa(f.RetryAfter))
	}
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.WriteHeader(f.Status)
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{"object": "error", "status": f.Status, "code": f.Code, "message": f.Message})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, v any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(v)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the service**

`backend/internal/setup/setup.go`:

```go
// Package setup resolves or creates the user's Notion databases and resyncs
// pending data when an installation becomes active (spec §2.7, §2.11, T22).
package setup

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// Error is a setup failure whose Reason is stored in INST.setup_error.
type Error struct{ Reason string }

func (e *Error) Error() string { return "setup: " + e.Reason }

// Reason returns the setup_error value for err. Unexpected errors map to "notion_error".
func Reason(err error) string {
	var e *Error
	if errors.As(err, &e) {
		return e.Reason
	}
	return "notion_error"
}

type Service struct {
	Notion *notion.Client
	Store  *store.Store
	Queue  queue.Sender
	Bucket time.Duration
	Now    func() time.Time
}

// ResolveTemplate finds "Reading Library" and "Daily Log" inside a duplicated
// template page and resolves their data sources and property ids (spec §2.11).
func (s *Service) ResolveTemplate(ctx context.Context, token, templateID string) (store.DBConfig, error) {
	blocks, err := s.Notion.ListBlockChildren(ctx, token, templateID)
	if isNotFound(err) {
		return store.DBConfig{}, &Error{"template_not_found"}
	}
	if err != nil {
		return store.DBConfig{}, err
	}
	ids := map[string]string{}
	for _, b := range blocks {
		if b.Type == "child_database" && b.ChildDatabase != nil {
			if _, seen := ids[b.ChildDatabase.Title]; !seen {
				ids[b.ChildDatabase.Title] = b.ID
			}
		}
	}
	return s.resolve(ctx, token, ids[LibraryTitle], ids[DailyTitle])
}

// CreateDatabases creates both databases under parentPageID (spec §2.7). Library
// goes first because the Daily Log relation needs its data source id.
func (s *Service) CreateDatabases(ctx context.Context, token, parentPageID string) (store.DBConfig, error) {
	lib, err := s.Notion.CreateDatabase(ctx, token, parentPageID, LibraryTitle, libraryCreateSchema())
	if err != nil {
		return store.DBConfig{}, err
	}
	if len(lib.DataSources) == 0 {
		return store.DBConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("setup: created %s has no data source", LibraryTitle)
	}
	daily, err := s.Notion.CreateDatabase(ctx, token, parentPageID, DailyTitle, dailyCreateSchema(lib.DataSources[0].ID))
	if err != nil {
		return store.DBConfig{}, err
	}
	return s.resolve(ctx, token, lib.ID, daily.ID)
}

func (s *Service) resolve(ctx context.Context, token, libDBID, dailyDBID string) (store.DBConfig, error) {
	if libDBID == "" {
		return store.DBConfig{}, &Error{"database_missing:" + LibraryTitle}
	}
	if dailyDBID == "" {
		return store.DBConfig{}, &Error{"database_missing:" + DailyTitle}
	}
	libDS, libURL, libProps, err := s.resolveDatabase(ctx, token, libDBID, LibraryTitle, LibraryProps)
	if err != nil {
		return store.DBConfig{}, err
	}
	dailyDS, _, dailyProps, err := s.resolveDatabase(ctx, token, dailyDBID, DailyTitle, DailyProps)
	if err != nil {
		return store.DBConfig{}, err
	}
	for k, v := range dailyProps {
		libProps[k] = v
	}
	return store.DBConfig{LibraryDBID: libDBID, LibraryDSID: libDS, LibraryURL: libURL,
		DailyDBID: dailyDBID, DailyDSID: dailyDS, Props: libProps}, nil
}

func (s *Service) resolveDatabase(ctx context.Context, token, dbID, title string, specs []PropSpec) (dsID, url string, props map[string]string, err error) {
	db, err := s.Notion.GetDatabase(ctx, token, dbID)
	if isNotFound(err) || (err == nil && len(db.DataSources) == 0) {
		return "", "", nil, &Error{"database_missing:" + title}
	}
	if err != nil {
		return "", "", nil, err
	}
	ds, err := s.Notion.GetDataSource(ctx, token, db.DataSources[0].ID)
	if isNotFound(err) {
		return "", "", nil, &Error{"database_missing:" + title}
	}
	if err != nil {
		return "", "", nil, err
	}
	props = map[string]string{}
	for _, spec := range specs {
		p, ok := ds.Properties[spec.Name]
		if !ok {
			return "", "", nil, &Error{"property_missing:" + spec.Name}
		}
		if p.Type != spec.Type {
			return "", "", nil, &Error{"property_type:" + spec.Name}
		}
		props[spec.Key] = p.ID
	}
	return ds.ID, db.URL, props, nil
}

// Resync enqueues every ART/DAY whose version is ahead of Notion (T22). Call it
// whenever an installation becomes active.
func (s *Service) Resync(ctx context.Context, installationID string) error {
	hashes, dates, err := s.Store.PendingSync(ctx, installationID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	msgs := make([]queue.Message, 0, len(hashes)+len(dates))
	for _, h := range hashes {
		msgs = append(msgs, queue.Article(installationID, h))
	}
	for _, d := range dates {
		msgs = append(msgs, queue.Day(installationID, d))
	}
	if len(msgs) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	return s.Queue.Send(ctx, queue.Bucketed(s.Now(), s.Bucket, msgs...)...)
}

func isNotFound(err error) bool {
	e, ok := notion.AsAPIError(err)
	return ok && e.Status == http.StatusNotFound
}
```

```go
package httpapi_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/httpapi"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion/notiontest"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store/storetest"
)

const (
	testExtID = "abcdefghijklmnopabcdefghijklmnop"
	testBotID = "b7c1e0f2-0000-4000-8000-000000000001"
)

var testVerifier = strings.Repeat("v", 64)

type testEnv struct {
	t      *testing.T
	db     *storetest.Env
	notion *notiontest.Server
	queue  *queue.Recorder
	cfg    *config.Config
	now    time.Time
	logs   *bytes.Buffer
	e      *echo.Echo
}

func newTestEnv(t *testing.T) *testEnv {
	t.Helper()
	env := &testEnv{t: t, db: storetest.New(t), notion: notiontest.New(t), queue: &queue.Recorder{}, logs: &bytes.Buffer{},
		now: time.Date(2026, 10, 6, 3, 12, 0, 0, time.UTC)}
	vars := map[string]string{
		"APP_ENV": "test", "TABLE_NAME": env.db.Table, "QUEUE_URL": "local", "NOTION_CLIENT_ID": "cid",
		"NOTION_CLIENT_SECRET": "csecret", "NOTION_VERSION": "2025-09-03", "NOTION_API_BASE": env.notion.URL,
		"TOKEN_ENC_KEYS":          "v1:" + base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bytes.Repeat([]byte{1}, 32)),
		"DEVICE_TOKEN_PEPPER":     base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bytes.Repeat([]byte{2}, 32)),
		"ALLOWED_EXTENSION_IDS":   testExtID,
		"DEBOUNCE_BUCKET_SECONDS": "120",
	}
	cfg, err := config.Load(config.RoleAPI, func(k string) string { return vars[k] })
	must(t, err)
	env.cfg = cfg
	nc := &notion.Client{BaseURL: cfg.NotionAPIBase, Version: cfg.NotionVersion, ClientID: cfg.NotionClientID, ClientSecret: cfg.NotionClientSecret}
	env.e = httpapi.New(httpapi.Deps{Config: cfg, Store: env.db.Store, Notion: nc, Queue: env.queue,
		Now: func() time.Time { return env.now }, Log: slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(env.logs, nil))})
	return env
}

// do sends a request. body may be nil, a raw string, or any JSON-encodable value.
func (env *testEnv) do(method, target string, body any, token string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	env.t.Helper()
	var rdr io.Reader
	switch b := body.(type) {
	case nil:
	case string:
		rdr = strings.NewReader(b)
	default:
		raw, err := json.Marshal(b)
		must(env.t, err)
		rdr = bytes.NewReader(raw)
	}
	req := httptest.NewRequest(method, target, rdr)
	if rdr != nil {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	}
	if token != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
	}
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	env.e.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	return rec
}

func decode[T any](t *testing.T, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder) T {
	t.Helper()
	var v T
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decode %q: %v", rec.Body.String(), err)
	}
	return v
}

func errorCode(t *testing.T, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder) string {
	t.Helper()
	var body struct {
		Error struct {
			Code    string `json:"code"`
			Message string `json:"message"`
		} `json:"error"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("not a §2.0 error body: %q", rec.Body.String())
	}
	return body.Error.Code
}

func must(t *testing.T, err error) {
	t.Helper()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// seedUser creates a user with one device and returns its bearer token.
func (env *testEnv) seedUser() (uid, token string) {
	t, ctx := env.t, context.Background()
	uid = "01J9ZK5USER00000000000001"
	must(t, env.db.Store.CreateUser(ctx, uid, store.Profile{NotionUserID: "nuser-1", Name: "Tuan Do", CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(env.now)}))
	token, err := crypto.NewDeviceToken()
	must(t, err)
	must(t, env.db.Store.CreateDevice(ctx, uid, "dev-1", store.Device{Name: "MacBook Chrome",
		TokenHMAC: crypto.TokenHMAC(env.cfg.DeviceTokenPepper, token), CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(env.now), LastSeenAt: store.Timestamp(env.now)}))
	return uid, token
}

// seedInstallation stores an installation for uid with tokens sealed the way the callback seals them.
func (env *testEnv) seedInstallation(uid, status string) *store.Installation {
	t, ctx := env.t, context.Background()
	tpl := env.notion.AddTemplate("tpl-1")
	access, err := env.cfg.Keys.Seal([]byte("secret-access-token"), []byte(testBotID))
	must(t, err)
	refresh, err := env.cfg.Keys.Seal([]byte("secret-refresh-token"), []byte(testBotID))
	must(t, err)
	inst := &store.Installation{UserID: uid, WorkspaceID: "ws-1", WorkspaceName: "Tuan's Notion", Status: status,
		AccessTokenEnc: access, RefreshTokenEnc: refresh, TokenVersion: 1, CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(env.now), UpdatedAt: store.Timestamp(env.now)}
	if status == store.StatusActive {
		inst.ApplyDatabases(tpl.DBConfig())
	}
	must(t, env.db.Store.PutInstallation(ctx, testBotID, inst))
	must(t, env.db.Store.PutUserInstallation(ctx, uid, testBotID, env.now))
	must(t, env.db.Store.SetActiveInstallation(ctx, uid, testBotID))
	return inst
}
```

```go
package httpapi_test

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

func TestConfigEndpoint(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/config", nil, "")
	if rec.Code != 200 || rec.Header().Get("Cache-Control") != "max-age=3600" {
		t.Fatalf("%d %v", rec.Code, rec.Header())
	}
	body := decode[config.PublicConfig](t, rec)
	if body.Version != 1 || body.Tracking.IdleSeconds != 60 || len(body.StripQueryParams) == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("body %+v", body)
	}
}

// T12 AC: unauthenticated GET /v1/me → 401 with the §2.0 error shape.
func TestMeRequiresDeviceToken(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	for _, tok := range []string{"", "not-a-token", "rt_" + strings.Repeat("A", 43)} {
		rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/me", nil, tok)
		if rec.Code != 401 || errorCode(t, rec) != "unauthorized" {
			t.Fatalf("token %q: %d %s", tok, rec.Code, rec.Body)
		}
	}
}

func TestUnknownRouteIsJSON404(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/nope", nil, "")
	if rec.Code != 404 || errorCode(t, rec) != "not_found" {
		t.Fatalf("%d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
}

type meResponse struct {
	User struct {
		ID   string `json:"id"`
		Name string `json:"name"`
	} `json:"user"`
	Device struct {
		ID   string `json:"id"`
		Name string `json:"name"`
	} `json:"device"`
	Installation *struct {
		ID            string  `json:"id"`
		WorkspaceName string  `json:"workspace_name"`
		Status        string  `json:"status"`
		SetupError    string  `json:"setup_error"`
		LibraryURL    *string `json:"library_url"`
	} `json:"installation"`
}

func TestMeReturnsUserDeviceAndInstallation(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	uid, tok := env.seedUser()
	env.seedInstallation(uid, store.StatusActive)
	rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/me", nil, tok)
	if rec.Code != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("%d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	body := decode[meResponse](t, rec)
	if body.User.ID != uid || body.User.Name != "Tuan Do" || body.Device.ID != "dev-1" || body.Device.Name != "MacBook Chrome" {
		t.Fatalf("body %+v", body)
	}
	if body.Installation == nil || body.Installation.ID != testBotID || body.Installation.Status != "active" ||
		body.Installation.LibraryURL == nil || body.Installation.WorkspaceName != "Tuan's Notion" {
		t.Fatalf("installation %+v", body.Installation)
	}
}

func TestMeWithoutInstallationReturnsNull(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	_, tok := env.seedUser()
	rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/me", nil, tok)
	if rec.Code != 200 || !strings.Contains(rec.Body.String(), `"installation":null`) {
		t.Fatalf("%d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
}

func TestNeedsSetupInstallationShowsErrorAndNullLibrary(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	uid, tok := env.seedUser()
	inst := env.seedInstallation(uid, store.StatusNeedsSetup)
	inst.SetupError = "template_not_found"
	must(t, env.db.Store.PutInstallation(t.Context(), testBotID, inst))
	rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/me", nil, tok)
	if !strings.Contains(rec.Body.String(), `"setup_error":"template_not_found"`) || !strings.Contains(rec.Body.String(), `"library_url":null`) {
		t.Fatalf("%s", rec.Body)
	}
}

// C13: the request log has the path but never the query string or bearer token.
func TestRequestLogOmitsQueryAndToken(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	_, tok := env.seedUser()
	env.do("GET", "/v1/me?code=abc123secret", nil, tok)
	logs := env.logs.String()
	if !strings.Contains(logs, "/v1/me") || strings.Contains(logs, "abc123secret") || strings.Contains(logs, tok) {
		t.Fatalf("log leak: %s", logs)
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/app.go`:

```go
// Package httpapi is the Echo application behind the api Lambda's Function URL (spec §2).
package httpapi

import (
	"log/slog"
	"time"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"
	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4/middleware"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/setup"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// Deps are the handlers' collaborators.
type Deps struct {
	Config *config.Config
	Store  *store.Store
	Notion *notion.Client
	Queue  queue.Sender
	Now    func() time.Time
	Log    *slog.Logger
}

type handlers struct {
	Deps
	setup *setup.Service
}

// New builds the app: /v1 routes, §2.0 JSON errors, 256 KB body limit and
// path-only request logs. CORS is configured on the Function URL, not here.
func New(d Deps) *echo.Echo {
	e := echo.New()
	e.HideBanner, e.HidePort = true, true
	e.HTTPErrorHandler = errorHandler(d.Log)
	e.Use(middleware.Recover())
	e.Use(requestLogger(d.Log))
	e.Use(middleware.BodyLimit("256K"))

	h := &handlers{Deps: d, setup: &setup.Service{Notion: d.Notion, Store: d.Store, Queue: d.Queue, Bucket: d.Config.DebounceBucket, Now: d.Now}}
	v1 := e.Group("/v1")
	v1.GET("/config", h.getConfig)
	v1.GET("/me", h.getMe, h.requireDevice)
	return e
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/errors.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"
)

// apiError renders as {"error":{"code","message"}} (spec §2.0).
type apiError struct {
	status int
	code   string
	msg    string
}

func (e *apiError) Error() string { return e.code + ": " + e.msg }

func errInvalid(format string, a ...any) *apiError {
	return &apiError{http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_request", fmt.Sprintf(format, a...)}
}
func errInvalidGrant() *apiError {
	return &apiError{http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_grant", "authorization code is invalid, expired or already used"}
}
func errUnauthorized() *apiError {
	return &apiError{http.StatusUnauthorized, "unauthorized", "missing, unknown or revoked device token"}
}
func errNoInstallation() *apiError {
	return &apiError{http.StatusConflict, "no_installation", "connect Notion first"}
}
func errConflict(msg string) *apiError { return &apiError{http.StatusConflict, "conflict", msg} }
func errNotFound(msg string) *apiError { return &apiError{http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", msg} }
func errTooLarge(msg string) *apiError {
	return &apiError{http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, "payload_too_large", msg}
}
func errCapacity() *apiError {
	return &apiError{http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "capacity_exceeded", "storage is busy; retry with backoff"}
}

func toAPIError(err error) *apiError {
	var ae *apiError
	if errors.As(err, &ae) {
		return ae
	}
	var he *echo.HTTPError
	if errors.As(err, &he) {
		switch he.Code {
		case http.StatusNotFound:
			return errNotFound("no such route")
		case http.StatusMethodNotAllowed:
			return &apiError{he.Code, "method_not_allowed", "method not allowed"}
		case http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge:
			return errTooLarge("request body exceeds 256 KB")
		}
		if he.Code < 500 {
			return &apiError{he.Code, "invalid_request", http.StatusText(he.Code)}
		}
	}
	return &apiError{http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal", "internal error"}
}

func errorHandler(log *slog.Logger) echo.HTTPErrorHandler {
	return func(err error, c echo.Context) {
		if c.Response().Committed {
			return
		}
		ae := toAPIError(err)
		if ae.status >= 500 {
			log.ErrorContext(c.Request().Context(), "request failed", "path", c.Request().URL.Path, "err", err)
		}
		_ = c.JSON(ae.status, map[string]any{"error": map[string]string{"code": ae.code, "message": ae.msg}})
	}
}

// decodeJSON reads the request body into v; oversized bodies become 413.
func decodeJSON(c echo.Context, v any) error {
	if err := json.NewDecoder(c.Request().Body).Decode(v); err != nil {
		var he *echo.HTTPError
		if errors.As(err, &he) && he.Code == http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge {
			return errTooLarge("request body exceeds 256 KB")
		}
		return errInvalid("malformed JSON body")
	}
	return nil
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/middleware.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

const principalKey = "principal"

// Principal is the authenticated device and its user.
type Principal struct {
	UserID    string
	TokenHMAC string
	Device    *store.Device
	Profile   *store.Profile
}

// InstallationID is the user's active installation (one per user in v1), or "".
func (p *Principal) InstallationID() string { return p.Profile.ActiveInstallationID }

func principal(c echo.Context) *Principal { return c.Get(principalKey).(*Principal) }

// requireDevice authenticates "Authorization: Bearer rt_…" (spec §2.4).
func (h *handlers) requireDevice(next echo.HandlerFunc) echo.HandlerFunc {
	return func(c echo.Context) error {
		tok, ok := strings.CutPrefix(c.Request().Header.Get("Authorization"), "Bearer ")
		if !ok || !crypto.ValidDeviceTokenFormat(tok) {
			return errUnauthorized()
		}
		ctx := c.Request().Context()
		mac := crypto.TokenHMAC(h.Config.DeviceTokenPepper, tok)
		ref, err := h.Store.GetDeviceToken(ctx, mac)
		if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
			return errUnauthorized()
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		dev, err := h.Store.GetDevice(ctx, ref.UserID, ref.DeviceID)
		if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) || (err == nil && dev.RevokedAt != "") {
			return errUnauthorized()
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		prof, err := h.Store.GetProfile(ctx, ref.UserID)
		if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) || (err == nil && prof.DeletedAt != "") {
			return errUnauthorized()
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		h.touch(ctx, ref.UserID, dev)
		c.Set(principalKey, &Principal{UserID: ref.UserID, TokenHMAC: mac, Device: dev, Profile: prof})
		return next(c)
	}
}

// touch updates last_seen_at at most hourly. The in-memory check skips the write
// (and its WCU) on most requests; the conditional update closes races.
func (h *handlers) touch(ctx context.Context, uid string, dev *store.Device) {
	now := h.Now()
	if seen, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, dev.LastSeenAt); err == nil && now.Sub(seen) < time.Hour {
		return
	}
	if err := h.Store.TouchDevice(ctx, uid, dev.ID(), now); err != nil {
		h.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "auth: touch device failed", "err", err)
	}
}

// requestLogger logs method, path, status and latency. Never the query string
// (OAuth codes/state), headers or bodies (C13).
func requestLogger(log *slog.Logger) echo.MiddlewareFunc {
	return func(next echo.HandlerFunc) echo.HandlerFunc {
		return func(c echo.Context) error {
			start := time.Now()
			err := next(c)
			status := c.Response().Status
			if err != nil {
				status = toAPIError(err).status
			}
			log.InfoContext(c.Request().Context(), "request", "method", c.Request().Method,
				"path", c.Request().URL.Path, "status", status, "ms", time.Since(start).Milliseconds())
			return err
		}
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/views.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

type userView struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

type deviceView struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

// installationView is the "installation" object of spec §2.3/§2.5.
type installationView struct {
	ID            string  `json:"id"`
	WorkspaceName string  `json:"workspace_name"`
	Status        string  `json:"status"`
	SetupError    string  `json:"setup_error,omitempty"`
	LibraryURL    *string `json:"library_url"`
}

func viewInstallation(i *store.Installation) *installationView {
	if i == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := &installationView{ID: i.ID(), WorkspaceName: i.WorkspaceName, Status: i.Status, SetupError: i.SetupError}
	if i.LibraryURL != "" {
		u := i.LibraryURL
		v.LibraryURL = &u
	}
	return v
}

// activeInstallation loads the principal's active installation, or nil if none.
func (h *handlers) activeInstallation(ctx context.Context, p *Principal) (*store.Installation, error) {
	id := p.InstallationID()
	if id == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	inst, err := h.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, id)
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		return nil, nil
	}
	return inst, err
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_me.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"
)

// getConfig serves GET /v1/config (spec §2.10).
func (h *handlers) getConfig(c echo.Context) error {
	c.Response().Header().Set("Cache-Control", "max-age=3600")
	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, h.Config.Public)
}

// getMe serves GET /v1/me (spec §2.5).
func (h *handlers) getMe(c echo.Context) error {
	p := principal(c)
	inst, err := h.activeInstallation(c.Request().Context(), p)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{
		"user":         userView{ID: p.UserID, Name: p.Profile.Name},
		"device":       deviceView{ID: p.Device.ID(), Name: p.Device.Name},
		"installation": viewInstallation(inst),
	})
}
```
