`backend/internal/app/app.go`:

```go
// Package app wires production dependencies for the cmd binaries.
package app

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	awsconfig "github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/sqs"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/httpapi"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// Logger writes JSON logs to stdout (CloudWatch).
func Logger() *slog.Logger { return slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, nil)) }

func AWSConfig(ctx context.Context) (aws.Config, error) { return awsconfig.LoadDefaultConfig(ctx) }

// DynamoDB honours DYNAMODB_ENDPOINT (cmd/local → DynamoDB Local).
func DynamoDB(cfg aws.Config) *dynamodb.Client {
	return dynamodb.NewFromConfig(cfg, func(o *dynamodb.Options) {
		if ep := os.Getenv("DYNAMODB_ENDPOINT"); ep != "" {
			o.BaseEndpoint = aws.String(ep)
		}
	})
}

func SQS(cfg aws.Config) *sqs.Client { return sqs.NewFromConfig(cfg) }

func NotionClient(cfg *config.Config, pacer notion.Pacer) *notion.Client {
	return &notion.Client{BaseURL: cfg.NotionAPIBase, Version: cfg.NotionVersion, ClientID: cfg.NotionClientID,
		ClientSecret: cfg.NotionClientSecret, HTTP: &http.Client{Timeout: 15 * time.Second}, Pacer: pacer}
}

// APIDeps assembles the api Lambda's dependencies. The API never paces or refreshes (C3, C8).
func APIDeps(cfg *config.Config, st *store.Store, q queue.Sender, log *slog.Logger) httpapi.Deps {
	return httpapi.Deps{Config: cfg, Store: st, Notion: NotionClient(cfg, nil), Queue: q, Now: time.Now, Log: log}
}
```

`backend/cmd/api/main.go`:

```go
// Command api is the api Lambda: Echo behind a Function URL (payload v2).
package main

import (
	"context"
	"os"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/lambda"
	echoadapter "github.com/awslabs/aws-lambda-go-api-proxy/echo"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/app"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/httpapi"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

func main() {
	log := app.Logger()
	cfg, err := config.Load(config.RoleAPI, os.Getenv)
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("config", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	awsCfg, err := app.AWSConfig(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("aws config", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	st := store.New(app.DynamoDB(awsCfg), cfg.TableName)
	q := &queue.SQS{API: app.SQS(awsCfg), QueueURL: cfg.QueueURL}
	e := httpapi.New(app.APIDeps(cfg, st, q, log))
	lambda.Start(echoadapter.NewV2(e).ProxyWithContext)
}
```

`backend/cmd/local/main.go` (the worker is wired in Task 17; for now the local queue only logs deliveries):

```go
// Command local runs the API on :8080 against DynamoDB Local with an in-process queue.
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/events"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/app"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/httpapi"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

func main() {
	log := app.Logger()
	cfg, err := config.Load(config.RoleAPI, os.Getenv)
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("config", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	ctx := context.Background()
	awsCfg, err := app.AWSConfig(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("aws config", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	ddb := app.DynamoDB(awsCfg)
	if err := store.CreateTable(ctx, ddb, cfg.TableName); err != nil {
		log.Error("create table (is `make ddb-up` running?)", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	st := store.New(ddb, cfg.TableName)
	q := &queue.Local{Delay: localDelay(cfg), Log: log, Handle: func(_ context.Context, ev events.SQSEvent) (events.SQSEventResponse, error) {
		for _, r := range ev.Records {
			log.Info("local queue: delivered", "body", r.Body)
		}
		return events.SQSEventResponse{}, nil
	}}
	e := httpapi.New(app.APIDeps(cfg, st, q, log))
	addr := os.Getenv("LISTEN_ADDR")
	if addr == "" {
		addr = ":8080"
	}
	log.Info("listening", "addr", addr)
	if err := e.Start(addr); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
		log.Error("server", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
}

// localDelay is LOCAL_QUEUE_DELAY_SECONDS, defaulting to the debounce bucket (D = B).
func localDelay(cfg *config.Config) time.Duration {
	if secs, err := strconv.Atoi(os.Getenv("LOCAL_QUEUE_DELAY_SECONDS")); err == nil && secs >= 0 {
		return time.Duration(secs) * time.Second
	}
	return cfg.DebounceBucket
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_oauth.go`

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: routes `GET /v1/oauth/notion/start` and `GET /v1/oauth/notion/callback`; unexported `(*handlers).redirectURI(c) string`; `oauthStateTTL = 10m`, `authCodeTTL = 2m`. package httpapi_test

```go
import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// fmtAV renders an attribute value (e.g. &{secret {}}) so scans can look for plaintext.
func fmtAV(v any) string { return fmt.Sprintf("%v", v) }

func startURL(ext string) string {
	q := url.Values{"ext_redirect": {ext}, "code_challenge": {crypto.S256Challenge(testVerifier)}, "code_challenge_method": {"S256"}}
	return "/v1/oauth/notion/start?" + q.Encode()
}

func extRedirect() string { return "https://" + testExtID + ".chromiumapp.org/" }

func tokenResponse(templateID any) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{
		"access_token": "secret-access-token", "refresh_token": "secret-refresh-token", "token_type": "bearer",
		"bot_id": testBotID, "workspace_id": "ws-1", "workspace_name": "Tuan's Notion", "workspace_icon": nil,
		"owner":                  map[string]any{"type": "user", "user": map[string]any{"id": "nuser-1", "name": "Tuan Do", "person": map[string]any{"email": "tuan@example.com"}}},
		"duplicated_template_id": templateID,
	}
}

// start runs /start and returns the state Notion would echo back.
func (env *testEnv) start(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	rec := env.do("GET", startURL(extRedirect()), nil, "")
	if rec.Code != 302 {
		t.Fatalf("start: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	loc, _ := url.Parse(rec.Header().Get("Location"))
	return loc.Query().Get("state")
}

// connect runs start + callback with the given Notion token response and returns the one-time code.
func (env *testEnv) connect(t *testing.T, tokenResp map[string]any) string {
	t.Helper()
	env.notion.SetTokenResponse(tokenResp)
	state := env.start(t)
	rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/oauth/notion/callback?code=notion-code&state="+state, nil, "")
	if rec.Code != 302 {
		t.Fatalf("callback: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	loc, _ := url.Parse(rec.Header().Get("Location"))
	code := loc.Query().Get("code")
	if code == "" {
		t.Fatalf("no code in %s", loc)
	}
	return code
}

// T13 AC: unknown extension id → 400.
func TestOAuthStartRejectsUnknownExtension(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	for _, ext := range []string{
		"https://" + strings.Repeat("z", 32) + ".chromiumapp.org/",
		"http://" + testExtID + ".chromiumapp.org/",
		"https://" + testExtID + ".chromiumapp.org.evil.com/",
		"https://evil.com/?x=" + testExtID + ".chromiumapp.org",
	} {
		if rec := env.do("GET", startURL(ext), nil, ""); rec.Code != 400 || errorCode(t, rec) != "invalid_request" {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d %s", ext, rec.Code, rec.Body)
		}
	}
}

func TestOAuthStartRequiresS256Challenge(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	q := url.Values{"ext_redirect": {extRedirect()}, "code_challenge": {"short"}, "code_challenge_method": {"plain"}}
	if rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/oauth/notion/start?"+q.Encode(), nil, ""); rec.Code != 400 {
		t.Fatalf("%d", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestOAuthStartRedirectsToNotion(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	rec := env.do("GET", startURL(extRedirect()), nil, "")
	loc, _ := url.Parse(rec.Header().Get("Location"))
	q := loc.Query()
	if rec.Code != 302 || loc.Path != "/v1/oauth/authorize" || q.Get("client_id") != "cid" || q.Get("owner") != "user" ||
		q.Get("response_type") != "code" || q.Get("redirect_uri") != "http://example.com/v1/oauth/notion/callback" || len(q.Get("state")) != 43 {
		t.Fatalf("%d %s", rec.Code, loc)
	}
}

// T13 AC: happy path → 302 to the extension with a code; INST stored with encrypted tokens only.
func TestOAuthCallbackHappyPathWithTemplate(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	tpl := env.notion.AddTemplate("tpl-1")
	code := env.connect(t, tokenResponse("tpl-1"))
	if len(code) != 43 {
		t.Fatalf("code %q", code)
	}
	inst, err := env.db.Store.GetInstallation(context.Background(), testBotID)
	must(t, err)
	if inst.Status != store.StatusActive || inst.LibraryDSID != tpl.LibraryDS || inst.DuplicatedTemplateID != "tpl-1" || inst.TokenVersion != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("installation %+v", inst)
	}
	for _, item := range env.db.Scan(t) {
		for k, v := range item {
			if s := fmtAV(v); strings.Contains(s, "secret-access-token") || strings.Contains(s, "secret-refresh-token") {
				t.Fatalf("plaintext token stored in %s", k)
			}
		}
	}
	plain, err := env.cfg.Keys.Open(inst.AccessTokenEnc, []byte(testBotID))
	if err != nil || string(plain) != "secret-access-token" {
		t.Fatalf("access token does not decrypt: %v", err)
	}
	ex := env.notion.Calls("POST", "/v1/oauth/token")
	if len(ex) != 1 || ex[0].Body["redirect_uri"] != "http://example.com/v1/oauth/notion/callback" || ex[0].Body["code"] != "notion-code" {
		t.Fatalf("token exchange %+v", ex)
	}
	uid, err := env.db.Store.UserIDForNotionUser(context.Background(), "nuser-1")
	must(t, err)
	prof, _ := env.db.Store.GetProfile(context.Background(), uid)
	if prof.ActiveInstallationID != testBotID || prof.Email != "tuan@example.com" {
		t.Fatalf("profile %+v", prof)
	}
}

// T13 AC / scenario 17: a replayed state → 400.
func TestOAuthCallbackReusedStateRejected(t *testing.T) {

func TestOAuthCallbackExpiredStateRejected(t *testing.T) {

	state := env.start(t)
	env.now = env.now.Add(11 * time.Minute)
	if rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/oauth/notion/callback?code=c&state="+state, nil, ""); rec.Code != 400 {

}

func TestOAuthCallbackAccessDenied(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	state := env.start(t)
	rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/oauth/notion/callback?error=access_denied&state="+state, nil, "")

// Review focus 5 / scenario 11: reconnect after revoke, user skips template → reuse DBs, active, resync.
func TestOAuthReconnectWithoutTemplateKeepsDatabases(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	tpl := env.notion.AddTemplate("tpl-1")
	env.connect(t, tokenResponse("tpl-1"))
	ctx := context.Background()
	must(t, env.db.Store.SetInstallationStatus(ctx, testBotID, store.StatusRevoked, "", env.now))
	_, err := env.db.Store.PushSegment(ctx, store.SegmentWrite{InstallationID: testBotID, DeviceID: "d", SegmentID: "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001",
		SessionID: "s", URLHash: store.URLHash("https://a.example/x"), Date: "2026-10-06", StartedAt: env.now, EndedAt: env.now.Add(time.Minute),
		ActiveSeconds: 60, Article: store.ArticleMeta{CanonicalURL: "https://a.example/x", Source: "generic"}})
	must(t, err)
	env.queue.Drain()

	env.connect(t, tokenResponse(nil))
	inst, _ := env.db.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, testBotID)
	if inst.Status != store.StatusActive || inst.LibraryDSID != tpl.LibraryDS || inst.TokenVersion != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("installation %+v", inst)
	}
	if msgs := env.queue.Messages(); len(msgs) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("resync enqueued %+v", msgs)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the handlers**

`backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_oauth.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"
	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/setup"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

const (
	oauthStateTTL = 10 * time.Minute
	authCodeTTL   = 2 * time.Minute
)

// oauthStart serves GET /v1/oauth/notion/start (spec §2.1).
func (h *handlers) oauthStart(c echo.Context) error {
	q := c.QueryParams()
	ext := q.Get("ext_redirect")
	if !h.allowedExtRedirect(ext) {
		return errInvalid("ext_redirect is not an allowed extension redirect")
	}
	challenge := q.Get("code_challenge")
	if q.Get("code_challenge_method") != "S256" || !crypto.ValidChallenge(challenge) {
		return errInvalid("code_challenge must be an S256 challenge")
	}
	state, err := crypto.RandomB64URL(32)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	st := store.OAuthState{ExtRedirect: ext, CodeChallenge: challenge}
	if err := h.Store.PutOAuthState(c.Request().Context(), state, st, h.Now().Add(oauthStateTTL)); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return c.Redirect(http.StatusFound, h.Notion.AuthorizeURL(h.redirectURI(c), state))
}

// allowedExtRedirect accepts only https://<id>.chromiumapp.org/… for configured ids.
func (h *handlers) allowedExtRedirect(raw string) bool {
	u, err := url.Parse(raw)
	if err != nil || u.Scheme != "https" || u.User != nil || u.Port() != "" {
		return false
	}
	id, ok := strings.CutSuffix(strings.ToLower(u.Hostname()), ".chromiumapp.org")
	return ok && h.Config.AllowedExtensionIDs[id]
}

// redirectURI is PUBLIC_BASE_URL or the request's own origin, plus the callback path (C14).
func (h *handlers) redirectURI(c echo.Context) string {
	base := h.Config.PublicBaseURL
	if base == "" {
		base = c.Scheme() + "://" + c.Request().Host
	}
	return base + "/v1/oauth/notion/callback"
}

// oauthCallback serves GET /v1/oauth/notion/callback (spec §2.2).
func (h *handlers) oauthCallback(c echo.Context) error {
	ctx := c.Request().Context()
	q := c.QueryParams()
	st, err := h.Store.ConsumeOAuthState(ctx, q.Get("state"), h.Now())
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		return c.String(http.StatusBadRequest, "Link expired, try again")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if e := q.Get("error"); e != "" {
		return redirectWith(c, st.ExtRedirect, "error", e)
	}
	tok, err := h.Notion.ExchangeCode(ctx, q.Get("code"), h.redirectURI(c))
	if err != nil {
		h.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "oauth: code exchange failed", "err", err)
		return redirectWith(c, st.ExtRedirect, "error", "exchange_failed")
	}
	if tok.BotID == "" || tok.Owner.User.ID == "" {
		return redirectWith(c, st.ExtRedirect, "error", "unsupported_owner")
	}
	uid, err := h.resolveUser(ctx, tok)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	inst, err := h.upsertInstallation(ctx, uid, tok)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	code, err := crypto.RandomB64URL(32)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ac := store.AuthCode{UserID: uid, InstallationID: inst.ID(), CodeChallenge: st.CodeChallenge, ExtRedirect: st.ExtRedirect}
	if err := h.Store.PutAuthCode(ctx, code, ac, h.Now().Add(authCodeTTL)); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return redirectWith(c, st.ExtRedirect, "code", code)
}

func redirectWith(c echo.Context, base, key, val string) error {
	u, err := url.Parse(base)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	q := u.Query()
	q.Set(key, val)
	u.RawQuery = q.Encode()
	return c.Redirect(http.StatusFound, u.String())
}

// resolveUser maps the Notion owner to our user, creating one if needed (§2.2 step 4).
// A user whose deletion is pending is treated as absent.
func (h *handlers) resolveUser(ctx context.Context, tok *notion.TokenResponse) (string, error) {
	nuid := tok.Owner.User.ID
	uid, err := h.Store.UserIDForNotionUser(ctx, nuid)
	switch {
	case err == nil:
		prof, perr := h.Store.GetProfile(ctx, uid)
		if perr == nil && prof.DeletedAt == "" {
			return uid, nil
		}
		if perr != nil && !errors.Is(perr, store.ErrNotFound) {
			return "", perr
		}
	case !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound):
		return "", err
	}
	uid = ulid.Make().String()
	return uid, h.Store.CreateUser(ctx, uid, store.Profile{NotionUserID: nuid, Name: tok.Owner.User.Name,
		Email: tok.Owner.User.Person.Email, CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(h.Now())})
}

// upsertInstallation stores INST#<bot_id>/META with sealed tokens (C18) and decides
// its status (§2.2 steps 5–7).
func (h *handlers) upsertInstallation(ctx context.Context, uid string, tok *notion.TokenResponse) (*store.Installation, error) {
	now := h.Now()
	inst, err := h.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, tok.BotID)
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		inst, err = &store.Installation{CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(now)}, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	aad := []byte(tok.BotID)
	if inst.AccessTokenEnc, err = h.Config.Keys.Seal([]byte(tok.AccessToken), aad); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	inst.RefreshTokenEnc = ""
	if tok.RefreshToken != "" {
		if inst.RefreshTokenEnc, err = h.Config.Keys.Seal([]byte(tok.RefreshToken), aad); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	inst.TokenExpiresAt = ""
	if tok.ExpiresIn > 0 {
		inst.TokenExpiresAt = store.Timestamp(now.Add(time.Duration(tok.ExpiresIn) * time.Second))
	}
	inst.TokenVersion++
	inst.UserID, inst.WorkspaceID, inst.WorkspaceName, inst.WorkspaceIcon = uid, tok.WorkspaceID, tok.WorkspaceName, tok.WorkspaceIcon
	inst.UpdatedAt, inst.SetupError = store.Timestamp(now), ""

	switch {
	case tok.DuplicatedTemplateID != "":
		inst.DuplicatedTemplateID = tok.DuplicatedTemplateID
		db, err := h.setup.ResolveTemplate(ctx, tok.AccessToken, tok.DuplicatedTemplateID)
		if err != nil {
			inst.Status, inst.SetupError = store.StatusNeedsSetup, setup.Reason(err)
			h.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "oauth: template resolution failed", "reason", inst.SetupError, "err", err)
		} else {
			inst.ApplyDatabases(db)
			inst.Status = store.StatusActive
		}
	case inst.HasDatabases():
		inst.Status = store.StatusActive // reconnect to an already set-up workspace
	default:
		inst.Status = store.StatusNeedsSetup
	}

	if err := h.Store.PutInstallation(ctx, tok.BotID, inst); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := h.Store.PutUserInstallation(ctx, uid, tok.BotID, now); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := h.Store.SetActiveInstallation(ctx, uid, tok.BotID); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if inst.Status == store.StatusActive {
		if err := h.setup.Resync(ctx, tok.BotID); err != nil {
			h.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "oauth: resync enqueue failed", "err", err)
		}
	}
	return inst, nil
}
```

```go
	v1.GET("/oauth/notion/callback", h.oauthCallback)
```

```go
import (

type tokenResp struct {

		ID   string `json:"id"`

		LibraryURL *string `json:"library_url"`
	} `json:"installation"`
}

func redeem(env *testEnv, code, verifier string) *tokenResp {
	rec := env.do("POST", "/v1/auth/token", map[string]string{"code": code, "code_verifier": verifier, "device_name": "MacBook Chrome"}, "")
	if rec.Code != 200 {
		return nil
	}
	r := decode[tokenResp](env.t, rec)
	return &r
}

func TestAuthTokenHappyPath(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	env.notion.AddTemplate("tpl-1")

		r.Installation.ID != testBotID || r.Installation.Status != "active" || r.Installation.LibraryURL == nil {
		t.Fatalf("response %+v", r)

	// T14 AC: token stored only as HMAC.
	ref, err := env.db.Store.GetDeviceToken(context.Background(), crypto.TokenHMAC(env.cfg.DeviceTokenPepper, r.DeviceToken))
	must(t, err)
	if ref.DeviceID != r.DeviceID || ref.UserID != r.User.ID {
		t.Fatalf("token ref %+v", ref)
	}
	for _, item := range env.db.Scan(t) {
		for _, v := range item {
			if strings.Contains(fmtAV(v), r.DeviceToken) {
				t.Fatal("plaintext device token stored")
			}
		}
	}
	if rec := env.do("GET", "/v1/me", nil, r.DeviceToken); rec.Code != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("new token rejected: %d", rec.Code)
	}
}

// T14 AC / scenario 17: wrong verifier → invalid_grant, and the code is burned.
func TestAuthTokenWrongVerifier(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	code := env.connect(t, tokenResponse(nil))
	rec := env.do("POST", "/v1/auth/token", map[string]string{"code": code, "code_verifier": strings.Repeat("w", 64)}, "")

	}
	if redeem(env, code, testVerifier) != nil {
```

`backend/internal/textutil/textutil.go`:

```go
// Package textutil holds small string helpers.
package textutil

// Truncate cuts s to at most max runes without splitting a character.
func Truncate(s string, max int) string {
	if len(s) <= max {
		return s
	}
	n := 0
	for i := range s {
		if n == max {
			return s[:i]
		}
		n++
	}
	return s
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_auth.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"
	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/textutil"
)

type tokenRequest struct {
	Code         string `json:"code"`
	CodeVerifier string `json:"code_verifier"`
	DeviceName   string `json:"device_name"`
}

// authToken serves POST /v1/auth/token (spec §2.3). The code is consumed before the
// PKCE check, so a wrong verifier burns it.
func (h *handlers) authToken(c echo.Context) error {
	ctx := c.Request().Context()
	var req tokenRequest
	if err := decodeJSON(c, &req); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	now := h.Now()
	ac, err := h.Store.ConsumeAuthCode(ctx, req.Code, now)
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		return errInvalidGrant()
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !crypto.VerifyPKCE(req.CodeVerifier, ac.CodeChallenge) {
		return errInvalidGrant()
	}
	token, err := crypto.NewDeviceToken()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	deviceID := ulid.Make().String()
	dev := store.Device{Name: deviceName(req.DeviceName), TokenHMAC: crypto.TokenHMAC(h.Config.DeviceTokenPepper, token),
		CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(now), LastSeenAt: store.Timestamp(now)}
	if err := h.Store.CreateDevice(ctx, ac.UserID, deviceID, dev); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	prof, err := h.Store.GetProfile(ctx, ac.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	inst, err := h.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, ac.InstallationID)
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		inst, err = nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{
		"device_token": token,
		"device_id":    deviceID,
		"user":         userView{ID: ac.UserID, Name: prof.Name},
		"installation": viewInstallation(inst),
	})
}

func deviceName(s string) string {
	if s = strings.TrimSpace(s); s == "" {
		return "Unknown device"
	}
	return textutil.Truncate(s, 100)
}
```

In `backend/internal/httpapi/app.go`, add before `return e`:


`backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_account.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"
)

// logout serves POST /v1/auth/logout (spec §2.17).
func (h *handlers) logout(c echo.Context) error {
	p := principal(c)
	if err := h.Store.RevokeDevice(c.Request().Context(), p.UserID, p.Device.ID(), p.TokenHMAC, h.Now()); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return c.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_setup.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// installationToken loads the active installation and decrypts its access token.
// The API never refreshes (C8): a Notion 401 here surfaces as 409 for the client to reconnect.
func (h *handlers) installationToken(ctx context.Context, p *Principal) (*store.Installation, string, error) {
	inst, err := h.activeInstallation(ctx, p)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, "", err
	}
	if inst == nil {
		return nil, "", errNoInstallation()
	}
	if inst.Status == store.StatusRevoked {
		return nil, "", errConflict("Notion connection revoked; reconnect")
	}
	tok, err := h.Config.Keys.Open(inst.AccessTokenEnc, []byte(inst.ID()))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, "", err
	}
	return inst, string(tok), nil
}

// notionErr maps Notion failures in API calls to §2.0 errors.
func notionErr(err error) error {
	e, ok := notion.AsAPIError(err)
	if !ok {
		return err
	}
	switch {
	case e.Status == http.StatusUnauthorized:
		return errConflict("Notion authorization expired; reconnect")
	case e.Status == http.StatusNotFound:
		return errNotFound("Notion object not found or not shared with the integration")
	case e.Status == http.StatusBadRequest:
		return errInvalid("Notion rejected the request: %s", e.Message)
	}
	return err
}

// setupPages serves GET /v1/setup/pages (spec §2.6).
func (h *handlers) setupPages(c echo.Context) error {
	ctx := c.Request().Context()
	_, token, err := h.installationToken(ctx, principal(c))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	pages, err := h.Notion.SearchPages(ctx, token, c.QueryParam("q"))
	if err != nil {
		return notionErr(err)
	}
	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"pages": pages})
}

type setupRequest struct {
	ParentPageID string `json:"parent_page_id"`
}

// postSetup serves POST /v1/setup (spec §2.7).
func (h *handlers) postSetup(c echo.Context) error {
	ctx := c.Request().Context()
	var req setupRequest
	if err := decodeJSON(c, &req); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if strings.TrimSpace(req.ParentPageID) == "" {
		return errInvalid("parent_page_id is required")
	}
	inst, token, err := h.installationToken(ctx, principal(c))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if inst.Status == store.StatusActive {
		return errConflict("installation is already set up")
	}
	db, err := h.setup.CreateDatabases(ctx, token, req.ParentPageID)
	if err != nil {
		return notionErr(err)
	}
	if err := h.Store.SaveInstallationDatabases(ctx, inst.ID(), db, h.Now()); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	inst.ApplyDatabases(db)
	inst.Status, inst.SetupError = store.StatusActive, ""
	if err := h.setup.Resync(ctx, inst.ID()); err != nil {
		h.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "setup: resync enqueue failed", "err", err)
	}
	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, viewInstallation(inst))
}
```

`SearchPages` returns a non-nil (possibly empty) slice, so `"pages"` always encodes as a JSON array.

`backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_articles.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

const maxStatusURLs = 100

type statusRequest struct {
	CanonicalURLs []string `json:"canonical_urls"`
}

type articleStatus struct {
	TotalSeconds int64   `json:"total_seconds"`
	MaxProgress  float64 `json:"max_progress"`
	Sessions     int64   `json:"sessions"`
	Completed    bool    `json:"completed"`
	LastReadAt   string  `json:"last_read_at"`
	NotionURL    *string `json:"notion_url"`
}

// articlesStatus serves POST /v1/articles/status (spec §2.9). Unknown URLs are omitted.
func (h *handlers) articlesStatus(c echo.Context) error {
	var req statusRequest
	if err := decodeJSON(c, &req); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(req.CanonicalURLs) > maxStatusURLs {
		return errTooLarge("canonical_urls exceeds 100")
	}
	inst := principal(c).InstallationID()
	if inst == "" {
		return errNoInstallation()
	}
	urlByHash := map[string]string{}
	hashes := make([]string, 0, len(req.CanonicalURLs))
	for _, u := range req.CanonicalURLs {
		h := store.URLHash(u)
		if _, dup := urlByHash[h]; !dup {
			urlByHash[h] = u
			hashes = append(hashes, h)
		}
	}
	arts, err := h.Store.BatchGetArticles(c.Request().Context(), inst, hashes)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	items := make(map[string]articleStatus, len(arts))
	for hash, a := range arts {
		st := articleStatus{TotalSeconds: a.TotalSeconds, MaxProgress: a.MaxProgress, Sessions: a.Sessions,
			Completed: a.Completed, LastReadAt: a.LastReadAt}
		if a.NotionURL != "" {
			u := a.NotionURL
			st.NotionURL = &u
		}
		items[urlByHash[hash]] = st
	}
	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"items": items})
}
```

```go
package syncsvc_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/syncsvc"
)

var (
	ctx = context.Background()
	now = time.Date(2026, 10, 6, 3, 12, 0, 0, time.UTC)
)

const (
	artURL = "https://medium.com/p/8f3c2a1b9d4e"
	bot    = "bot-1"
)

// fakePusher remembers committed segment ids, like the real conditional put.
type fakePusher struct {
	seen     map[string]bool
	throttle map[string]bool
	got      []store.SegmentWrite
}

func (f *fakePusher) PushSegment(_ context.Context, w store.SegmentWrite) (store.PushOutcome, error) {
	f.got = append(f.got, w)
	if f.throttle[w.SegmentID] {
		return store.PushRetry, nil
	}
	if f.seen == nil {
		f.seen = map[string]bool{}
	}
	if f.seen[w.SegmentID] {
		return store.PushDuplicate, nil
	}
	f.seen[w.SegmentID] = true
	return store.PushAccepted, nil
}

func newService() (*syncsvc.Service, *fakePusher, *queue.Recorder) {
	p, rec := &fakePusher{throttle: map[string]bool{}}, &queue.Recorder{}
	return &syncsvc.Service{Store: p, Queue: rec, Bucket: 120 * time.Second, Now: func() time.Time { return now }}, p, rec
}

func article(u string) syncsvc.Article {
	return syncsvc.Article{CanonicalURL: u, Source: "medium", Title: "Designing Idempotent APIs", Author: "Jane Doe",
		SiteName: "Better Programming", WordCount: 1820, PublishedAt: "2026-09-30"}
}

func seg(id, u string, started time.Time, secs int) syncsvc.Segment {
	return syncsvc.Segment{ID: id, SessionID: "c3a9", Seq: 0, CanonicalURL: u, StartedAt: started.Format(time.RFC3339),
		EndedAt: started.Add(time.Duration(secs+5) * time.Second).Format(time.RFC3339), ActiveSeconds: secs, MaxProgress: 0.42}
}

func id(n int) string { return "6f1d2c9e-6a43-4b1e-9a0e-2f7c1d5b8e" + string(rune('0'+n/10)) + string(rune('0'+n%10)) }

func TestPushClassifiesOutcomes(t *testing.T) {
	svc, p, _ := newService()
	p.seen = map[string]bool{id(2): true}
	p.throttle[id(3)] = true
	bad := seg(id(4), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 60)
	bad.ActiveSeconds = 4000
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{article(artURL)}, Segments: []syncsvc.Segment{
		seg(id(1), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 58), seg(id(2), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 58), seg(id(3), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 58), bad,
	}}
	resp, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(resp.Accepted) != 1 || resp.Accepted[0] != id(1) || len(resp.Duplicate) != 1 || resp.Duplicate[0] != id(2) ||
		len(resp.Retry) != 1 || resp.Retry[0] != id(3) || len(resp.Rejected) != 1 || resp.Rejected[0].Reason != syncsvc.ReasonActiveSecondsRange {
		t.Fatalf("resp %+v", resp)
	}
}

// T16 AC: one article + one day message per distinct key.
func TestPushEnqueuesOncePerKey(t *testing.T) {
	svc, _, rec := newService()
	other := "https://example.com/post"
	start := now.Add(-time.Hour)
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{article(artURL), article(other)}, Segments: []syncsvc.Segment{
		seg(id(1), artURL, start, 60), seg(id(2), artURL, start.Add(time.Minute), 60), seg(id(3), artURL, start.Add(2*time.Minute), 60), seg(id(4), other, start, 60),
	}}
	if _, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req); err != nil {

	}

	want := []queue.Message{queue.Article(bot, store.URLHash(artURL)), queue.Article(bot, store.URLHash(other)), queue.Day(bot, "2026-10-06")}

		}

}

// T16 AC / scenario 16: date is computed in the request tz.
func TestPushDateUsesRequestTZ(t *testing.T) {
	svc, p, rec := newService()
	started := time.Date(2026, 10, 5, 18, 30, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	svc.Now = func() time.Time { return started.Add(time.Hour) }
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{article(artURL)}, Segments: []syncsvc.Segment{seg(id(1), artURL, started, 60)}}

	}

	}
	if msgs := rec.Messages(); msgs[len(msgs)-1] != queue.Day(bot, "2026-10-06") {
		t.Fatalf("day message %+v", msgs)
	}
}

// Scenario 15: each invalid segment is rejected with a reason; the request still succeeds.
func TestPushValidationReasons(t *testing.T) {
	svc, p, _ := newService()
	ok := func(n int) syncsvc.Segment { return seg(id(n), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 60) }
	cases := map[string]func(*syncsvc.Segment){
		syncsvc.ReasonInvalidID:            func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.ID = "not-a-uuid" },
		syncsvc.ReasonInvalidSession:       func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.SessionID = "" },
		syncsvc.ReasonInvalidSeq:           func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.Seq = -1 },
		syncsvc.ReasonInvalidTimestamp:     func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.StartedAt = "yesterday" },
		syncsvc.ReasonActiveSecondsRange:   func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.ActiveSeconds = 0 },
		syncsvc.ReasonEndedBeforeStarted:   func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.EndedAt = now.Add(-2 * time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339) },
		syncsvc.ReasonActiveExceedsWindow:  func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.ActiveSeconds = 600 },
		syncsvc.ReasonProgressRange:        func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.MaxProgress = 1.5 },
		syncsvc.ReasonStartedAtRange:       func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { *s = seg(s.ID, artURL, now.Add(-40*24*time.Hour), 60) },
		syncsvc.ReasonUnknownArticle:       func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.CanonicalURL = "https://not-in-articles.example/" },
		syncsvc.ReasonInvalidArticle:       func(s *syncsvc.Segment) { s.CanonicalURL = "ftp://bad.example/x" },
	}
	var segs []syncsvc.Segment
	n := 10
	for _, mutate := range cases {
		s := ok(n)
		mutate(&s)
		segs = append(segs, s)
		n++
	}
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{article(artURL), {CanonicalURL: "ftp://bad.example/x", Source: "x"}}, Segments: segs}
	resp, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got := map[string]bool{}
	for _, r := range resp.Rejected {
		got[r.Reason] = true
	}
	for reason := range cases {
		if !got[reason] {
			t.Errorf("no segment rejected with %s (got %+v)", reason, resp.Rejected)
		}
	}
	if len(p.got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("invalid segments reached the store: %d", len(p.got))
	}
}

func TestPushAllThrottled(t *testing.T) {
	svc, p, _ := newService()
	p.throttle[id(1)] = true
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{article(artURL)}, Segments: []syncsvc.Segment{seg(id(1), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 60)}}
	if _, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req); !errors.Is(err, syncsvc.ErrAllThrottled) {
		t.Fatalf("err %v", err)
	}
}

func TestPushEnvelopeErrors(t *testing.T) {
	svc, _, _ := newService()
	many := make([]syncsvc.Segment, 51)
	_, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Segments: many})
	var env *syncsvc.EnvelopeError
	if !errors.As(err, &env) || !env.TooLarge {
		t.Fatalf("51 segments: %v", err)
	}
	for _, tz := range []string{"", "Mars/Olympus", "Local"} {
		_, err = svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: tz})
		if !errors.As(err, &env) || env.TooLarge {
			t.Fatalf("tz %q: %v", tz, err)
		}
	}
}

// Interpretation 2: long titles are truncated, not rejected.
func TestPushTruncatesLongTitle(t *testing.T) {
	svc, p, _ := newService()
	a := article(artURL)
	a.Title = strings.Repeat("Đ", 600)
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{a}, Segments: []syncsvc.Segment{seg(id(1), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 60)}}
	resp, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req)
	if err != nil || len(resp.Accepted) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("resp %+v err %v", resp, err)
	}
	if n := len([]rune(p.got[0].Article.Title)); n != 500 {
		t.Fatalf("title has %d runes", n)
	}
}

// Stored times are UTC so DynamoDB string comparisons order correctly.
func TestPushNormalizesOffsetTimestamps(t *testing.T) {
	svc, p, _ := newService()
	s := seg(id(1), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 60)
	s.StartedAt = now.Add(-time.Hour).In(time.FixedZone("ICT", 7*3600)).Format(time.RFC3339)
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{article(artURL)}, Segments: []syncsvc.Segment{s}}
	if _, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := store.Timestamp(p.got[0].StartedAt); got != store.Timestamp(now.Add(-time.Hour)) {
		t.Fatalf("started %s", got)
	}
}

// Review focus 2: enqueue fails after commit; the client's retry sees duplicates and must still enqueue.
func TestPushRetryAfterEnqueueFailureStillEnqueues(t *testing.T) {
	svc, _, rec := newService()
	req := syncsvc.PushRequest{TZ: "UTC", Articles: []syncsvc.Article{article(artURL)}, Segments: []syncsvc.Segment{seg(id(1), artURL, now.Add(-time.Hour), 60)}}
	rec.Err = errors.New("sqs unavailable")
	if _, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("enqueue failure must fail the request so the client retries")
	}
	rec.Err = nil
	resp, err := svc.Push(ctx, bot, "dev-1", req)
	if err != nil || len(resp.Duplicate) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("retry resp %+v err %v", resp, err)
	}
	if len(rec.Messages()) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("retry enqueued %+v", rec.Messages())
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/syncsvc/types.go`:

```go
// Package syncsvc validates POST /v1/sync/push bodies and applies them (spec §2.8).
package syncsvc

type Article struct {
	CanonicalURL string `json:"canonical_url"`
	Source       string `json:"source"`
	Title        string `json:"title"`
	Author       string `json:"author"`
	SiteName     string `json:"site_name"`
	WordCount    int    `json:"word_count"`
	PublishedAt  string `json:"published_at"`
	OriginalURL  string `json:"original_url"`
}

type Segment struct {
	ID            string  `json:"id"`
	SessionID     string  `json:"session_id"`
	Seq           int     `json:"seq"`
	CanonicalURL  string  `json:"canonical_url"`
	StartedAt     string  `json:"started_at"`
	EndedAt       string  `json:"ended_at"`
	ActiveSeconds int     `json:"active_seconds"`
	MaxProgress   float64 `json:"max_progress"`
}

type PushRequest struct {
	ClientTime string    `json:"client_time"`
	TZ         string    `json:"tz"`
	Articles   []Article `json:"articles"`
	Segments   []Segment `json:"segments"`
}

type Rejection struct {
	ID     string `json:"id"`
	Reason string `json:"reason"`
}

// PushResponse lists segment ids by outcome. The client marks accepted ∪ duplicate
// as synced, keeps retry, and drops rejected.
type PushResponse struct {
	Accepted  []string    `json:"accepted"`
	Duplicate []string    `json:"duplicate"`
	Retry     []string    `json:"retry"`
	Rejected  []Rejection `json:"rejected"`
}

func newResponse() *PushResponse {
	return &PushResponse{Accepted: []string{}, Duplicate: []string{}, Retry: []string{}, Rejected: []Rejection{}}
}
```

`backend/internal/syncsvc/validate.go`:

```go
package syncsvc

import (
	"fmt"
	"net/url"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/textutil"
)

// Segment rejection reasons.
const (
	ReasonInvalidID           = "invalid_id"
	ReasonInvalidSession      = "invalid_session_id"
	ReasonInvalidSeq          = "invalid_seq"
	ReasonInvalidTimestamp    = "invalid_timestamp"
	ReasonActiveSecondsRange  = "active_seconds_out_of_range"
	ReasonEndedBeforeStarted  = "ended_before_started"
	ReasonActiveExceedsWindow = "active_seconds_exceed_window"
	ReasonProgressRange       = "max_progress_out_of_range"
	ReasonStartedAtRange      = "started_at_out_of_range"
	ReasonUnknownArticle      = "unknown_article"
	ReasonInvalidArticle      = "invalid_article"
)

const (
	MaxArticles      = 50
	MaxSegments      = 50
	maxURLLen        = 2048
	maxTitleRunes    = 500
	maxNameRunes     = 200
	maxWordCount     = 200000
	maxSessionIDLen  = 64
	maxActiveSeconds = 3600
	windowSlack      = 5 * time.Second
	maxSegmentAge    = 30 * 24 * time.Hour
	maxClockSkew     = 5 * time.Minute
)

var (
	uuidRE   = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$`)
	sourceRE = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-z0-9_-]{1,32}$`)
)

// EnvelopeError fails the whole request: 413 when TooLarge, else 400.
type EnvelopeError struct {
	TooLarge bool
	Msg      string
}

func (e *EnvelopeError) Error() string { return e.Msg }

func checkEnvelope(req *PushRequest) (*time.Location, error) {
	if len(req.Articles) > MaxArticles || len(req.Segments) > MaxSegments {
		return nil, &EnvelopeError{TooLarge: true, Msg: fmt.Sprintf("at most %d articles and %d segments per push", MaxArticles, MaxSegments)}
	}
	if req.TZ == "" || req.TZ == "Local" {
		return nil, &EnvelopeError{Msg: "tz must be an IANA time zone name"}
	}
	loc, err := time.LoadLocation(req.TZ)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, &EnvelopeError{Msg: "tz is not a known IANA time zone"}
	}
	return loc, nil
}

// normalizeArticle trims and bounds an article. Identity fields (url, source,
// word_count) must be valid; descriptive text is truncated (interpretation 2).
func normalizeArticle(a Article) (store.ArticleMeta, bool) {
	u := strings.TrimSpace(a.CanonicalURL)
	src := strings.TrimSpace(a.Source)
	if !validURL(u) || !sourceRE.MatchString(src) || a.WordCount < 0 || a.WordCount > maxWordCount {
		return store.ArticleMeta{}, false
	}
	return store.ArticleMeta{
		CanonicalURL: u,
		Source:       src,
		Title:        textutil.Truncate(strings.TrimSpace(a.Title), maxTitleRunes),
		Author:       textutil.Truncate(strings.TrimSpace(a.Author), maxNameRunes),
		SiteName:     textutil.Truncate(strings.TrimSpace(a.SiteName), maxNameRunes),
		WordCount:    a.WordCount,
		PublishedAt:  normalizeDate(a.PublishedAt),
	}, true
}

func validURL(s string) bool {
	if s == "" || len(s) > maxURLLen {
		return false
	}
	u, err := url.Parse(s)
	return err == nil && (u.Scheme == "https" || u.Scheme == "http") && u.Host != ""
}

// normalizeDate keeps YYYY-MM-DD, reduces RFC 3339 to its UTC date, and drops anything else.
func normalizeDate(s string) string {
	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
	if _, err := time.Parse(time.DateOnly, s); err == nil {
		return s
	}
	if t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, s); err == nil {
		return t.UTC().Format(time.DateOnly)
	}
	return ""
}

// validateSegment returns the parsed times or a rejection reason (spec §2.8 validation).
func validateSegment(s Segment, now time.Time) (start, end time.Time, reason string) {
	if !uuidRE.MatchString(s.ID) {
		return start, end, ReasonInvalidID
	}
	if s.SessionID == "" || len(s.SessionID) > maxSessionIDLen {
		return start, end, ReasonInvalidSession
	}
	if s.Seq < 0 {
		return start, end, ReasonInvalidSeq
	}
	start, err1 := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, s.StartedAt)
	end, err2 := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, s.EndedAt)
	if err1 != nil || err2 != nil {
		return start, end, ReasonInvalidTimestamp
	}
	if s.ActiveSeconds < 1 || s.ActiveSeconds > maxActiveSeconds {
		return start, end, ReasonActiveSecondsRange
	}
	if end.Before(start) {
		return start, end, ReasonEndedBeforeStarted
	}
	if time.Duration(s.ActiveSeconds)*time.Second > end.Sub(start)+windowSlack {
		return start, end, ReasonActiveExceedsWindow
	}
	if s.MaxProgress < 0 || s.MaxProgress > 1 {
		return start, end, ReasonProgressRange
	}
	if start.Before(now.Add(-maxSegmentAge)) || start.After(now.Add(maxClockSkew)) {
		return start, end, ReasonStartedAtRange
	}
	return start, end, ""
}
```

`backend/internal/syncsvc/service.go`:

```go
package syncsvc

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// ErrAllThrottled means every valid segment hit DynamoDB throttling → 503.
var ErrAllThrottled = errors.New("syncsvc: all segments throttled")

// Pusher applies one segment exactly once (*store.Store).
type Pusher interface {
	PushSegment(ctx context.Context, w store.SegmentWrite) (store.PushOutcome, error)
}

type Service struct {
	Store  Pusher
	Queue  queue.Sender
	Bucket time.Duration
	Now    func() time.Time
}

// Push validates, writes each segment in its own transaction (C25), and enqueues
// one article/day message per touched key.
func (s *Service) Push(ctx context.Context, installationID, deviceID string, req PushRequest) (*PushResponse, error) {
	loc, err := checkEnvelope(&req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	now := s.Now()

	articles := map[string]store.ArticleMeta{}
	invalid := map[string]bool{}
	for _, a := range req.Articles {
		key := strings.TrimSpace(a.CanonicalURL)
		meta, ok := normalizeArticle(a)
		if !ok {
			if _, have := articles[key]; !have {
				invalid[key] = true
			}
			continue
		}
		if _, dup := articles[key]; !dup {
			articles[key] = meta
			delete(invalid, key)
		}
	}

	resp := newResponse()
	var writes []store.SegmentWrite
	for _, seg := range req.Segments {
		start, end, reason := validateSegment(seg, now)
		key := strings.TrimSpace(seg.CanonicalURL)
		meta, known := articles[key]
		if reason == "" && !known {
			reason = ReasonUnknownArticle
			if invalid[key] {
				reason = ReasonInvalidArticle
			}
		}
		if reason != "" {
			resp.Rejected = append(resp.Rejected, Rejection{ID: seg.ID, Reason: reason})
			continue
		}
		writes = append(writes, store.SegmentWrite{
			InstallationID: installationID, DeviceID: deviceID, SegmentID: seg.ID, SessionID: seg.SessionID, Seq: seg.Seq,
			URLHash: store.URLHash(meta.CanonicalURL), Date: start.In(loc).Format(time.DateOnly),
			StartedAt: start, EndedAt: end, ActiveSeconds: seg.ActiveSeconds, MaxProgress: seg.MaxProgress, Article: meta,
		})
	}

	var touched touchedKeys
	for _, w := range writes {
		out, err := s.Store.PushSegment(ctx, w)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		switch out {
		case store.PushAccepted:
			resp.Accepted = append(resp.Accepted, w.SegmentID)
			touched.add(w)
		case store.PushDuplicate:
			// Re-enqueue: the push that committed it may have failed to enqueue (interpretation 3).
			resp.Duplicate = append(resp.Duplicate, w.SegmentID)
			touched.add(w)
		case store.PushRetry:
			resp.Retry = append(resp.Retry, w.SegmentID)
		}
	}
	if len(writes) > 0 && len(resp.Retry) == len(writes) {
		return nil, ErrAllThrottled
	}
	if msgs := touched.messages(installationID); len(msgs) > 0 {
		if err := s.Queue.Send(ctx, queue.Bucketed(now, s.Bucket, msgs...)...); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return resp, nil
}

// touchedKeys collects distinct article hashes and dates in first-seen order.
type touchedKeys struct {
	hashes, dates []string
	seen          map[string]bool
}

func (t *touchedKeys) add(w store.SegmentWrite) {
	if t.seen == nil {
		t.seen = map[string]bool{}
	}
	if k := "a#" + w.URLHash; !t.seen[k] {
		t.seen[k] = true
		t.hashes = append(t.hashes, w.URLHash)
	}
	if k := "d#" + w.Date; !t.seen[k] {
		t.seen[k] = true
		t.dates = append(t.dates, w.Date)
	}
}

// messages lists article messages before day messages, so a day page can relate
// to article pages created in the same batch.
func (t *touchedKeys) messages(inst string) []queue.Message {
	msgs := make([]queue.Message, 0, len(t.hashes)+len(t.dates))
	for _, h := range t.hashes {
		msgs = append(msgs, queue.Article(inst, h))
	}
	for _, d := range t.dates {
		msgs = append(msgs, queue.Day(inst, d))
	}
	return msgs
}
```

`backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_sync.go`:

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"errors"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/syncsvc"
)

// syncPush serves POST /v1/sync/push (spec §2.8). It is accepted whatever the
// installation status; Notion catches up via resync.
func (h *handlers) syncPush(c echo.Context) error {
	p := principal(c)
	if p.InstallationID() == "" {
		return errNoInstallation()
	}
	var req syncsvc.PushRequest
	if err := decodeJSON(c, &req); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	resp, err := h.sync.Push(c.Request().Context(), p.InstallationID(), p.Device.ID(), req)
	var env *syncsvc.EnvelopeError
	switch {
	case errors.As(err, &env):
		if env.TooLarge {
			return errTooLarge(env.Msg)
		}
		return errInvalid("%s", env.Msg)
	case errors.Is(err, syncsvc.ErrAllThrottled):
		return errCapacity()
	case err != nil:
		return err
	}
	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, resp)
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/env_test.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/events"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion/notiontest"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store/storetest"
)

var (
	ctx     = context.Background()
	testNow = time.Date(2026, 10, 6, 3, 12, 0, 0, time.UTC)
)

const (
	testBot = "b7c1e0f2-0000-4000-8000-000000000001"
	artURL  = "https://medium.com/p/8f3c2a1b9d4e"
)

type fakeVisibility struct{ calls []time.Duration }

func (f *fakeVisibility) ChangeVisibility(_ context.Context, _ string, d time.Duration) error {
	f.calls = append(f.calls, d)
	return nil
}

type wenv struct {
	t    *testing.T
	w    *Worker
	db   *storetest.Env
	ns   *notiontest.Server
	q    *queue.Recorder
	vis  *fakeVisibility
	tpl  notiontest.Template
	keys *crypto.Keyring
	now  time.Time
	logs *bytes.Buffer
	segs int
}

func b64(b byte) string { return base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bytes.Repeat([]byte{b}, 32)) }

func must(t *testing.T, err error) {
	t.Helper()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func newEnv(t *testing.T) *wenv { return newEnvWithKeys(t, "v1:"+b64(1)) }

func newEnvWithKeys(t *testing.T, keys string) *wenv {
	t.Helper()
	kr, err := crypto.ParseKeyring(keys)
	must(t, err)
	pub, err := config.LoadPublic("")
	must(t, err)
	e := &wenv{t: t, db: storetest.New(t), ns: notiontest.New(t), q: &queue.Recorder{}, vis: &fakeVisibility{},
		keys: kr, now: testNow, logs: &bytes.Buffer{}}
	e.tpl = e.ns.AddTemplate("tpl-1")
	e.w = New(Deps{
		Store:      e.db.Store,
		Notion:     &notion.Client{BaseURL: e.ns.URL, Version: "2025-09-03", ClientID: "cid", ClientSecret: "csecret"},
		Keys:       kr,
		Queue:      e.q,
		Visibility: e.vis,
		Tracking:   pub.Tracking,
		Now:        func() time.Time { return e.now },
		Jitter:     func() time.Duration { return 2 * time.Second },
		Log:        slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(e.logs, nil)),
	})
	return e
}

func (e *wenv) seedInstallation(access, refresh string) *store.Installation {
	return e.seedInstallationWith(e.keys, access, refresh)
}

// seedInstallationWith stores an active installation whose tokens are sealed by kr.
func (e *wenv) seedInstallationWith(kr *crypto.Keyring, access, refresh string) *store.Installation {
	e.t.Helper()
	a, err := kr.Seal([]byte(access), []byte(testBot))
	must(e.t, err)
	r := ""
	if refresh != "" {
		r, err = kr.Seal([]byte(refresh), []byte(testBot))
		must(e.t, err)
	}
	inst := &store.Installation{UserID: "u1", WorkspaceName: "WS", Status: store.StatusActive, AccessTokenEnc: a,
		RefreshTokenEnc: r, TokenVersion: 1, CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(e.now), UpdatedAt: store.Timestamp(e.now)}
	inst.ApplyDatabases(e.tpl.DBConfig())
	must(e.t, e.db.Store.PutInstallation(ctx, testBot, inst))
	return inst
}

func (e *wenv) read(url string, seq, secs int, progress float64) {
	e.readMeta(store.ArticleMeta{CanonicalURL: url, Source: "medium", Title: "Designing Idempotent APIs", Author: "Jane Doe",
		SiteName: "Better Programming", WordCount: 1000}, seq, secs, progress)
}

// readMeta records one new segment of secs active seconds starting at e.now, then advances e.now.
func (e *wenv) readMeta(meta store.ArticleMeta, seq, secs int, progress float64) {
	e.t.Helper()
	e.segs++
	_, err := e.db.Store.PushSegment(ctx, store.SegmentWrite{InstallationID: testBot, DeviceID: "d1",
		SegmentID: fmt.Sprintf("00000000-0000-4000-8000-%012d", e.segs), SessionID: "s1", Seq: seq,
		URLHash: store.URLHash(meta.CanonicalURL), Date: "2026-10-06", StartedAt: e.now,
		EndedAt: e.now.Add(time.Duration(secs) * time.Second), ActiveSeconds: secs, MaxProgress: progress, Article: meta})
	must(e.t, err)
	e.now = e.now.Add(time.Duration(secs) * time.Second)
}

// handle runs the messages as one SQS batch and returns the reported failures.
func (e *wenv) handle(msgs ...queue.Message) []events.SQSBatchItemFailure {
	e.t.Helper()
	var ev events.SQSEvent
	for i, m := range msgs {
		body, err := json.Marshal(m)
		must(e.t, err)
		ev.Records = append(ev.Records, events.SQSMessage{MessageId: fmt.Sprintf("m%d", i), ReceiptHandle: fmt.Sprintf("rh%d", i), Body: string(body)})
	}
	resp, err := e.w.HandleSQS(ctx, ev)
	must(e.t, err)
	return resp.BatchItemFailures
}

func pid(name string) string { return notiontest.PropID(name) }

// num reads a number property of a fake page by column name.
func num(pg *notiontest.Page, name string) float64 {
	v, _ := pg.Properties[pid(name)].(map[string]any)
	f, _ := v["number"].(float64)
	return f
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/worker_test.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/events"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// C21: after the first failure, it and every later message are reported failed.
func TestFirstFailureFailsRestOfBatch(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	var ran []string
	e.w.handlers[queue.TypeArticle] = func(_ context.Context, m queue.Message) error {
		ran = append(ran, m.URLHash)
		if m.URLHash == "bad" {
			return errors.New("boom")
		}
		return nil
	}
	fails := e.handle(queue.Article(testBot, "ok"), queue.Article(testBot, "bad"), queue.Article(testBot, "later"))
	if len(fails) != 2 || fails[0].ItemIdentifier != "m1" || fails[1].ItemIdentifier != "m2" {
		t.Fatalf("failures %+v", fails)
	}
	if len(ran) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("ran %v; messages after a failure must not run", ran)
	}
}

// T21 AC / scenario 12: 429 → visibility ≥ Retry-After (+ jitter), message failed.
func TestRateLimitChangesVisibility(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.w.handlers[queue.TypeArticle] = func(context.Context, queue.Message) error {
		return &notion.APIError{Status: 429, Code: "rate_limited", RetryAfter: 5 * time.Second}
	}
	fails := e.handle(queue.Article(testBot, "h"))
	if len(fails) != 1 || len(e.vis.calls) != 1 || e.vis.calls[0] != 7*time.Second {
		t.Fatalf("fails %+v visibility %v", fails, e.vis.calls)
	}
}

func TestOverloadWithoutRetryAfterUsesDefault(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.w.handlers[queue.TypeArticle] = func(context.Context, queue.Message) error {
		return &notion.APIError{Status: 529, Code: "service_overload"}
	}
	e.handle(queue.Article(testBot, "h"))
	if len(e.vis.calls) != 1 || e.vis.calls[0] != defaultBackoff+2*time.Second {
		t.Fatalf("visibility %v", e.vis.calls)
	}
}

func TestServerErrorIsRetriedWithoutVisibilityChange(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.w.handlers[queue.TypeArticle] = func(context.Context, queue.Message) error { return &notion.APIError{Status: 502} }
	if fails := e.handle(queue.Article(testBot, "h")); len(fails) != 1 || len(e.vis.calls) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("fails %+v vis %v", fails, e.vis.calls)
	}
}

// §2.15: 400 validation_error is a poison message: log and ack.
func TestValidationErrorIsAcked(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.w.handlers[queue.TypeArticle] = func(context.Context, queue.Message) error {
		return &notion.APIError{Status: 400, Code: "validation_error", Message: "body failed validation"}
	}
	if fails := e.handle(queue.Article(testBot, "h")); len(fails) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("fails %+v", fails)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(e.logs.String(), "validation_error") {
		t.Fatal("poison message not logged")
	}
}

func TestUndecodableAndUnknownMessagesAreAcked(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	resp, err := e.w.HandleSQS(ctx, events.SQSEvent{Records: []events.SQSMessage{
		{MessageId: "m0", Body: "not json"}, {MessageId: "m1", Body: `{"type":"mystery"}`},
	}})
	if err != nil || len(resp.BatchItemFailures) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("resp %+v err %v", resp, err)
	}
}

func searchCall(e *wenv) func(string) error {
	return func(tok string) error {
		_, err := e.w.Notion.SearchPages(ctx, tok, "")
		return err
	}
}

// T21 AC / C7: refresh stores both new tokens and bumps token_version.
func TestSessionRefreshPersistsBothTokens(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	inst := e.seedInstallation("old-access", "old-refresh")
	e.ns.RejectToken("old-access")
	e.ns.SetRefreshResponse(map[string]any{"access_token": "new-access", "refresh_token": "new-refresh", "bot_id": testBot})

	s, err := e.w.openSession(inst)
	must(t, err)
	must(t, s.call(ctx, searchCall(e)))

	got, _ := e.db.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, testBot)
	access, _ := e.keys.Open(got.AccessTokenEnc, []byte(testBot))
	refresh, _ := e.keys.Open(got.RefreshTokenEnc, []byte(testBot))
	if got.TokenVersion != 2 || string(access) != "new-access" || string(refresh) != "new-refresh" {
		t.Fatalf("installation %+v", got)
	}
	refreshes := e.ns.Calls("POST", "/v1/oauth/token")
	if len(refreshes) != 1 || refreshes[0].Body["refresh_token"] != "old-refresh" {
		t.Fatalf("refresh calls %+v", refreshes)
	}
}

// T21 AC / scenario 11: refresh invalid_grant → revoked, message acked.
func TestSessionRefreshInvalidGrantRevokes(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	inst := e.seedInstallation("old-access", "old-refresh")
	e.ns.RejectToken("old-access") // no refresh response configured → 400 invalid_grant
	s, _ := e.w.openSession(inst)
	if err := s.call(ctx, searchCall(e)); !errors.Is(err, errAck) {
		t.Fatalf("err %v", err)
	}
	if got, _ := e.db.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, testBot); got.Status != store.StatusRevoked {
		t.Fatalf("status %s", got.Status)
	}
}

// Integration removed from the workspace: even a fresh token is rejected.
func TestSessionStillUnauthorizedAfterRefreshRevokes(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	inst := e.seedInstallation("old-access", "old-refresh")
	e.ns.RejectToken("old-access")
	e.ns.RejectToken("new-access")
	e.ns.SetRefreshResponse(map[string]any{"access_token": "new-access", "refresh_token": "new-refresh"})
	s, _ := e.w.openSession(inst)
	if err := s.call(ctx, searchCall(e)); !errors.Is(err, errAck) {
		t.Fatalf("err %v", err)
	}
	if got, _ := e.db.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, testBot); got.Status != store.StatusRevoked {
		t.Fatalf("status %s", got.Status)
	}
}

// Scenario 19: tokens sealed with v1 still open after v2 is prepended, and a refresh re-seals with v2.
func TestRefreshReencryptsWithCurrentKey(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnvWithKeys(t, "v2:"+b64(2)+",v1:"+b64(1))
	old, err := crypto.ParseKeyring("v1:" + b64(1))
	must(t, err)
	inst := e.seedInstallationWith(old, "old-access", "old-refresh")
	e.ns.RejectToken("old-access")
	e.ns.SetRefreshResponse(map[string]any{"access_token": "new-access", "refresh_token": "new-refresh"})
	s, err := e.w.openSession(inst)
	must(t, err)
	must(t, s.call(ctx, searchCall(e)))
	got, _ := e.db.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, testBot)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(got.AccessTokenEnc, "v2:") || !strings.HasPrefix(got.RefreshTokenEnc, "v2:") {
		t.Fatalf("not re-sealed with v2: %s / %s", got.AccessTokenEnc[:3], got.RefreshTokenEnc[:3])
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/worker.go`:

```go
// Package worker is the notion-writer Lambda: the only component that writes
// pages to Notion and refreshes Notion tokens (C3, C8).
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"math/rand/v2"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/events"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// VisibilityChanger hides a message for a back-off period.
type VisibilityChanger interface {
	ChangeVisibility(ctx context.Context, receiptHandle string, d time.Duration) error
}

type Deps struct {
	Store      *store.Store
	Notion     *notion.Client
	Keys       *crypto.Keyring
	Queue      queue.Sender
	Visibility VisibilityChanger
	Tracking   config.Tracking
	Now        func() time.Time
	Jitter     func() time.Duration // added to Retry-After; default rand 0..3 s
	Log        *slog.Logger
}

type handlerFunc func(ctx context.Context, m queue.Message) error

type Worker struct {
	Deps
	handlers map[queue.Type]handlerFunc
}

// errAck stops processing a message without failing it (revoked, needs_setup, …).
var errAck = errors.New("worker: ack")

const defaultBackoff = 30 * time.Second

type retryAfterError struct {
	delay time.Duration
	err   error
}

func (e *retryAfterError) Error() string { return fmt.Sprintf("retry after %s: %v", e.delay, e.err) }
func (e *retryAfterError) Unwrap() error { return e.err }

func New(d Deps) *Worker {
	if d.Jitter == nil {
		d.Jitter = func() time.Duration { return time.Duration(rand.IntN(4)) * time.Second }
	}
	w := &Worker{Deps: d}
	w.handlers = map[queue.Type]handlerFunc{}
	return w
}

// HandleSQS processes a FIFO batch in order. On the first failure it reports that
// message and every later one as failed (C21), so group order is preserved.
func (w *Worker) HandleSQS(ctx context.Context, ev events.SQSEvent) (events.SQSEventResponse, error) {
	var resp events.SQSEventResponse
	for i, rec := range ev.Records {
		err := w.handleRecord(ctx, rec)
		if err == nil {
			continue
		}
		w.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "worker: message failed", "message_id", rec.MessageId, "err", err)
		var ra *retryAfterError
		if errors.As(err, &ra) {
			if verr := w.Visibility.ChangeVisibility(ctx, rec.ReceiptHandle, ra.delay); verr != nil {
				w.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "worker: change visibility failed", "err", verr)
			}
		}
		for _, r := range ev.Records[i:] {
			resp.BatchItemFailures = append(resp.BatchItemFailures, events.SQSBatchItemFailure{ItemIdentifier: r.MessageId})
		}
		break
	}
	return resp, nil
}

func (w *Worker) handleRecord(ctx context.Context, rec events.SQSMessage) error {
	var m queue.Message
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(rec.Body), &m); err != nil {
		w.Log.ErrorContext(ctx, "worker: undecodable message dropped", "message_id", rec.MessageId)
		return nil
	}
	h, ok := w.handlers[m.Type]
	if !ok {
		w.Log.ErrorContext(ctx, "worker: unknown message type dropped", "type", m.Type)
		return nil
	}
	return w.classify(ctx, h(ctx, m))
}

// classify maps a handler error to ack (nil), a back-off, or a plain failure (spec §2.15).
func (w *Worker) classify(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	if err == nil || errors.Is(err, errAck) {
		return nil
	}
	e, ok := notion.AsAPIError(err)
	if !ok {
		return err // network or DynamoDB: normal visibility timeout
	}
	switch {
	case e.Status == http.StatusTooManyRequests || e.Status == 529:
		d := e.RetryAfter
		if d <= 0 {
			d = defaultBackoff
		}
		return &retryAfterError{delay: d + w.Jitter(), err: err}
	case e.Status >= 500:
		return err
	default:
		w.Log.ErrorContext(ctx, "worker: notion rejected request; dropping message",
			"status", e.Status, "code", e.Code, "message", e.Message)
		return nil
	}
}

// activeInstallation returns nil (→ ack) when the installation is gone or not active.
// Its data stays in DynamoDB and is resynced on reactivation (spec §2.13 step 1).
func (w *Worker) activeInstallation(ctx context.Context, id string) (*store.Installation, error) {
	inst, err := w.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, id)
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		return nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if inst.Status != store.StatusActive {
		return nil, nil
	}
	return inst, nil
}

func isStatus(err error, status int) bool {
	e, ok := notion.AsAPIError(err)
	return ok && e.Status == status
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/session.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// session runs Notion calls for one installation, refreshing the token once on 401.
// FIFO grouping guarantees no concurrent refresh for the same installation (C8).
type session struct {
	w         *Worker
	inst      *store.Installation
	token     string
	refreshed bool
}

func (w *Worker) openSession(inst *store.Installation) (*session, error) {
	tok, err := w.Keys.Open(inst.AccessTokenEnc, []byte(inst.ID()))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("worker: decrypt access token: %w", err)
	}
	return &session{w: w, inst: inst, token: string(tok)}, nil
}

// call runs fn with the current token. On 401 it refreshes once and retries. A 401
// with a freshly refreshed token means the integration was removed → revoked.
func (s *session) call(ctx context.Context, fn func(token string) error) error {
	err := fn(s.token)
	if !isStatus(err, http.StatusUnauthorized) {
		return err
	}
	if s.refreshed {
		return s.revoke(ctx)
	}
	if err := s.refresh(ctx); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err = fn(s.token); isStatus(err, http.StatusUnauthorized) {
		return s.revoke(ctx)
	}
	return err
}

// refresh rotates tokens and persists both under the token_version guard (spec §2.16, C7).
func (s *session) refresh(ctx context.Context) error {
	s.refreshed = true
	if s.inst.RefreshTokenEnc == "" {
		return s.revoke(ctx)
	}
	aad := []byte(s.inst.ID())
	rt, err := s.w.Keys.Open(s.inst.RefreshTokenEnc, aad)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("worker: decrypt refresh token: %w", err)
	}
	res, err := s.w.Notion.RefreshToken(ctx, string(rt))
	if e, ok := notion.AsAPIError(err); ok && e.Code == "invalid_grant" {
		return s.revoke(ctx)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	access, err := s.w.Keys.Seal([]byte(res.AccessToken), aad)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	refresh := s.inst.RefreshTokenEnc
	if res.RefreshToken != "" {
		if refresh, err = s.w.Keys.Seal([]byte(res.RefreshToken), aad); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	err = s.w.Store.UpdateInstallationTokens(ctx, s.inst.ID(), access, refresh, s.inst.TokenVersion, s.w.Now())
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrConditionFailed) {
		// Someone rotated first; use what they stored.
		fresh, gerr := s.w.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, s.inst.ID())
		if gerr != nil {
			return gerr
		}
		tok, oerr := s.w.Keys.Open(fresh.AccessTokenEnc, aad)
		if oerr != nil {
			return oerr
		}
		s.inst, s.token = fresh, string(tok)
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.inst.AccessTokenEnc, s.inst.RefreshTokenEnc = access, refresh
	s.inst.TokenVersion++
	s.token = res.AccessToken
	return nil
}

func (s *session) revoke(ctx context.Context) error {
	if err := s.w.Store.SetInstallationStatus(ctx, s.inst.ID(), store.StatusRevoked, "", s.w.Now()); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.w.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "worker: installation revoked", "installation_id", s.inst.ID())
	return errAck
}
```

```go
func TestCompletedRule(t *testing.T) {
	tr := config.Tracking{CompleteProgress: 0.9, CompleteTimeRatio: 0.3, WordsPerMinute: 230}
	cases := []struct {
		a    store.Article
		want bool
	}{
		{store.Article{MaxProgress: 0.95, WordCount: 1000, TotalSeconds: 100}, true}, // 100 ≥ 0.3 × 260.9
		{store.Article{MaxProgress: 0.95, WordCount: 1000, TotalSeconds: 50}, false}, // skimmed
		{store.Article{MaxProgress: 0.95, WordCount: 0, TotalSeconds: 5}, true},      // unknown length
		{store.Article{MaxProgress: 0.5, WordCount: 0, TotalSeconds: 999}, false},
		{store.Article{MaxProgress: 0.1, Completed: true}, true}, // sticky
	}
	for i, c := range cases {
		if got := isCompleted(&c.a, tr); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("case %d: got %v", i, got)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`backend/internal/worker/properties.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"math"
	"strings"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/textutil"
)

// Notion request limits (appendix §8 + request-limits reference).
const (
	notionMaxURL    = 2000
	notionMaxText   = 2000
	notionMaxSelect = 100
	maxRelations    = 100
)

// isCompleted implements spec §2.13 step 2; once true it stays true.
func isCompleted(a *store.Article, t config.Tracking) bool {
	if a.Completed {
		return true
	}
	if a.MaxProgress < t.CompleteProgress {
		return false
	}
	if a.WordCount == 0 {
		return true
	}
	est := float64(a.WordCount) / float64(t.WordsPerMinute) * 60
	return float64(a.TotalSeconds) >= t.CompleteTimeRatio*est
}

// articleMetrics are the server-owned properties written on every update (§3.3).
func articleMetrics(p map[string]string, a *store.Article, completed bool) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{
		p["last_read"]: dateValue(a.LastReadAt),
		p["minutes"]:   map[string]any{"number": roundMinutes(a.TotalSeconds)},
		p["progress"]:  map[string]any{"number": a.MaxProgress},
		p["sessions"]:  map[string]any{"number": a.Sessions},
		p["completed"]: map[string]any{"checkbox": completed},
	}
}

// articleCreateBody adds the write-once descriptive properties to the metrics.
// It never includes Tags, Status, Rating or Notes (C1).
func articleCreateBody(dsID string, p map[string]string, a *store.Article, completed bool) map[string]any {
	props := articleMetrics(p, a, completed)
	title := a.Title
	if title == "" {
		title = a.CanonicalURL
	}
	props[p["title"]] = map[string]any{"title": richText(title)}
	props[p["first_read"]] = dateValue(a.FirstReadAt)
	if a.WordCount > 0 {
		props[p["word_count"]] = map[string]any{"number": a.WordCount}
	}
	if a.Author != "" {
		props[p["author"]] = map[string]any{"rich_text": richText(a.Author)}
	}
	if s := selectName(a.Source); s != "" {
		props[p["source"]] = map[string]any{"select": map[string]any{"name": s}}
	}
	if s := selectName(a.SiteName); s != "" {
		props[p["site"]] = map[string]any{"select": map[string]any{"name": s}}
	}
	body := map[string]any{
		"parent":     map[string]any{"type": "data_source_id", "data_source_id": dsID},
		"properties": props,
	}
	if len(a.CanonicalURL) <= notionMaxURL {
		props[p["url"]] = map[string]any{"url": a.CanonicalURL}
		body["children"] = []any{map[string]any{"object": "block", "type": "bookmark", "bookmark": map[string]any{"url": a.CanonicalURL}}}
	}
	return body
}

func richText(s string) []any {
	return []any{map[string]any{"type": "text", "text": map[string]any{"content": textutil.Truncate(s, notionMaxText)}}}
}

func dateValue(ts string) map[string]any {
	if ts == "" {
		return map[string]any{"date": nil}
	}
	return map[string]any{"date": map[string]any{"start": ts}}
}

func roundMinutes(secs int64) int64 { return int64(math.Round(float64(secs) / 60)) }

// selectName makes s a valid select option: Notion forbids commas and caps length.
func selectName(s string) string {
	return textutil.Truncate(strings.Join(strings.Fields(strings.ReplaceAll(s, ",", " ")), " "), notionMaxSelect)
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/pages.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

var errPageGone = errors.New("worker: notion page archived")

// updatePage PATCHes properties; a page that comes back archived counts as gone.
func (w *Worker) updatePage(ctx context.Context, token, pageID string, props map[string]any) error {
	p, err := w.Notion.UpdatePage(ctx, token, pageID, props)
	if err == nil && (p.Archived || p.InTrash) {
		return errPageGone
	}
	return err
}

// isPageGone: 404, or Notion's 400 "Can't edit block that is archived" (spec §2.15).
func isPageGone(err error) bool {
	if errors.Is(err, errPageGone) {
		return true
	}
	e, ok := notion.AsAPIError(err)
	return ok && (e.Status == http.StatusNotFound ||
		(e.Status == http.StatusBadRequest && e.Code == "validation_error" && strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(e.Message), "archived")))
}

// requeue re-enqueues m with an id that can't collide with its bucket dedup id.
func (w *Worker) requeue(ctx context.Context, m queue.Message) error {
	return w.Queue.Send(ctx, queue.Outgoing{Message: m, DedupID: m.RequeueDedupID(w.Now())})
}

// dataSourceError: a 404 on a data source means the database was deleted, so the
// installation needs setup again and the message is acked (spec §2.15).
func (w *Worker) dataSourceError(ctx context.Context, inst *store.Installation, title string, err error) error {
	if !isStatus(err, http.StatusNotFound) {
		return err
	}
	if serr := w.Store.SetInstallationStatus(ctx, inst.ID(), store.StatusNeedsSetup, "database_missing:"+title, w.Now()); serr != nil {
		return serr
	}
	w.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "worker: database missing; installation needs setup", "installation_id", inst.ID(), "database", title)
	return errAck
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/article.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/setup"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// handleArticle implements spec §2.13. It always writes absolute values read from DynamoDB (C6).
func (w *Worker) handleArticle(ctx context.Context, m queue.Message) error {
	inst, err := w.activeInstallation(ctx, m.InstallationID)
	if inst == nil || err != nil {
		return err
	}
	art, err := w.Store.GetArticle(ctx, inst.ID(), m.URLHash)
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	completed := isCompleted(art, w.Tracking)
	sess, err := w.openSession(inst)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if art.NotionPageID == "" {
		if art.TotalSeconds < int64(w.Tracking.MinCreateSeconds) {
			return nil
		}
		if err := w.createArticlePage(ctx, sess, m.URLHash, art, completed); err != nil {
			return w.dataSourceError(ctx, inst, setup.LibraryTitle, err)
		}
	} else {
		err := sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error {
			return w.updatePage(ctx, tok, art.NotionPageID, articleMetrics(inst.Props, art, completed))
		})
		if isPageGone(err) {
			if err := w.Store.ClearArticlePage(ctx, inst.ID(), m.URLHash); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return w.requeue(ctx, m)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return w.Store.MarkArticleSynced(ctx, inst.ID(), m.URLHash, art.Version, completed, w.Now())
}

// createArticlePage adopts a page with the same URL if one exists, else creates one.
func (w *Worker) createArticlePage(ctx context.Context, sess *session, hash string, art *store.Article, completed bool) error {
	inst := sess.inst
	var page *notion.Page
	if len(art.CanonicalURL) <= notionMaxURL {
		err := sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error {
			res, err := w.Notion.QueryDataSource(ctx, tok, inst.LibraryDSID, map[string]any{
				"filter":    map[string]any{"property": inst.Props["url"], "url": map[string]any{"equals": art.CanonicalURL}},
				"page_size": 1,
			})
			if err == nil && len(res.Results) > 0 {
				page = &res.Results[0]
			}
			return err
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if page != nil {
		err := sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error {
			return w.updatePage(ctx, tok, page.ID, articleMetrics(inst.Props, art, completed))
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	} else {
		err := sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error {
			p, err := w.Notion.CreatePage(ctx, tok, articleCreateBody(inst.LibraryDSID, inst.Props, art, completed))
			page = p
			return err
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return w.Store.SetArticlePage(ctx, inst.ID(), hash, page.ID, page.URL)
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/day.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"slices"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/setup"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// handleDay implements spec §2.14.
func (w *Worker) handleDay(ctx context.Context, m queue.Message) error {
	inst, err := w.activeInstallation(ctx, m.InstallationID)
	if inst == nil || err != nil {
		return err
	}
	day, err := w.Store.GetDay(ctx, inst.ID(), m.Date)
	if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	pageIDs, err := w.dayArticlePages(ctx, inst.ID(), day.ArticleHashes)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	sess, err := w.openSession(inst)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	props := dayMetrics(inst.Props, day, pageIDs)

	if day.NotionPageID == "" {
		if err := w.createDayPage(ctx, sess, day, props); err != nil {
			return w.dataSourceError(ctx, inst, setup.DailyTitle, err)
		}
	} else {
		err := sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error { return w.updatePage(ctx, tok, day.NotionPageID, props) })
		if isPageGone(err) {
			if err := w.Store.ClearDayPage(ctx, inst.ID(), m.Date); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return w.requeue(ctx, m)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return w.Store.MarkDaySynced(ctx, inst.ID(), m.Date, day.Version)
}

// dayArticlePages returns the Notion page ids of the day's articles that have one (≤ 100).
func (w *Worker) dayArticlePages(ctx context.Context, inst string, hashes []string) ([]string, error) {
	sorted := slices.Sorted(slices.Values(hashes))
	arts, err := w.Store.BatchGetArticles(ctx, inst, sorted)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var ids []string
	for _, h := range sorted {
		if a := arts[h]; a != nil && a.NotionPageID != "" {
			ids = append(ids, a.NotionPageID)
			if len(ids) == maxRelations {
				break
			}
		}
	}
	return ids, nil
}

func dayMetrics(p map[string]string, d *store.Day, pageIDs []string) map[string]any {
	rel := make([]any, len(pageIDs))
	for i, id := range pageIDs {
		rel[i] = map[string]any{"id": id}
	}
	return map[string]any{
		p["day_minutes"]:  map[string]any{"number": roundMinutes(d.Seconds)},
		p["day_articles"]: map[string]any{"relation": rel},
	}
}

// createDayPage adopts the row for this date if one exists, else creates it.
func (w *Worker) createDayPage(ctx context.Context, sess *session, day *store.Day, metrics map[string]any) error {
	inst := sess.inst
	var page *notion.Page
	err := sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error {
		res, err := w.Notion.QueryDataSource(ctx, tok, inst.DailyDSID, map[string]any{
			"filter":    map[string]any{"property": inst.Props["day_date"], "date": map[string]any{"equals": day.Date}},
			"page_size": 1,
		})
		if err == nil && len(res.Results) > 0 {
			page = &res.Results[0]
		}
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if page != nil {
		err = sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error { return w.updatePage(ctx, tok, page.ID, metrics) })
	} else {
		props := map[string]any{
			inst.Props["day_title"]: map[string]any{"title": richText(day.Date)},
			inst.Props["day_date"]:  dateValue(day.Date),
		}
		for k, v := range metrics {
			props[k] = v
		}
		err = sess.call(ctx, func(tok string) error {
			p, err := w.Notion.CreatePage(ctx, tok, map[string]any{
				"parent":     map[string]any{"type": "data_source_id", "data_source_id": inst.DailyDSID},
				"properties": props,
			})
			page = p
			return err
		})
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.Store.SetDayPage(ctx, inst.ID(), day.Date, page.ID)
}
```

```go
package worker

import (
	"testing"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
```
