// seedUser creates user u1 (Notion user n1) with device d1 and the test installation.
func (e *wenv) seedUser(uid string) {
	e.t.Helper()
	s := e.db.Store
	must(e.t, s.CreateUser(ctx, uid, store.Profile{NotionUserID: "n-" + uid, Name: "T", CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(e.now)}))
	must(e.t, s.CreateDevice(ctx, uid, "d1", store.Device{Name: "Mac", TokenHMAC: "h-" + uid, CreatedAt: store.Timestamp(e.now), LastSeenAt: store.Timestamp(e.now)}))
	must(e.t, s.PutUserInstallation(ctx, uid, testBot, e.now))
}

func (e *wenv) remaining(pk string) int {
	keys, err := e.db.Store.PartitionKeys(ctx, pk)
	must(e.t, err)
	return len(keys)
}

// T23 AC / scenario 22: every item of the user is gone; Notion untouched.
func TestPurgeRemovesAllUserData(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.seedInstallation("tok", "rt") // META.user_id = u1
	e.seedUser("u1")
	e.read(artURL, 0, 60, 0.5)
	must(t, e.db.Store.MarkProfileDeleted(ctx, "u1", e.now))

	if f := e.handle(queue.Purge("u1", testBot)); len(f) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("failures %+v", f)
	}
	for _, pk := range []string{store.UserPK("u1"), store.InstPK(testBot), store.NUserPK("n-u1"), store.DevTokenPK("h-u1")} {
		if n := e.remaining(pk); n != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s still has %d items", pk, n)
		}
	}
	if n := len(e.ns.Requests()); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("purge called Notion %d times", n)
	}
}

func TestPurgeIgnoresLiveUser(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.seedInstallation("tok", "rt")
	e.seedUser("u1")
	e.handle(queue.Purge("u1", testBot))
	if e.remaining(store.UserPK("u1")) == 0 || e.remaining(store.InstPK(testBot)) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("purged a user that is not marked deleted")
	}
}

// Interpretation 5: an installation now owned by another user is not purged.
func TestPurgeSkipsInstallationOwnedByAnotherUser(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.seedInstallation("tok", "rt") // owned by u1
	e.seedUser("u0")
	must(t, e.db.Store.MarkProfileDeleted(ctx, "u0", e.now))
	e.handle(queue.Purge("u0", testBot))
	if e.remaining(store.InstPK(testBot)) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("deleted another user's installation")
	}
	if e.remaining(store.UserPK("u0")) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("u0 not purged")
	}
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `backend/internal/httpapi/handlers_account.go`, and add the import `"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"`:

```go
// deleteMe serves DELETE /v1/me (spec §2.17): revoke every device, mark the
// profile deleted, and let the worker purge the data. Notion content is never deleted.
func (h *handlers) deleteMe(c echo.Context) error {
	ctx := c.Request().Context()
	p := principal(c)
	now := h.Now()
	devices, err := h.Store.ListDevices(ctx, p.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, d := range devices {
		if d.RevokedAt != "" {
			continue
		}
		if err := h.Store.RevokeDevice(ctx, p.UserID, d.ID(), d.TokenHMAC, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if err := h.Store.MarkProfileDeleted(ctx, p.UserID, now); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	msg := queue.Purge(p.UserID, p.InstallationID())
	if err := h.Queue.Send(ctx, queue.Outgoing{Message: msg, DedupID: msg.DedupID(now, h.Config.DebounceBucket)}); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return c.NoContent(http.StatusAccepted)
}
```

`backend/internal/worker/purge.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"slices"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

// handlePurge deletes every DynamoDB item of a deleted user (spec §2.17). It never
// touches Notion, skips users not marked deleted, and skips installations that now
// belong to someone else (interpretation 5).
func (w *Worker) handlePurge(ctx context.Context, m queue.Message) error {
	uid := m.UserID
	if uid == "" {
		return nil
	}
	prof, err := w.Store.GetProfile(ctx, uid)
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound):
		prof = nil
	case err != nil:
		return err
	case prof.DeletedAt == "":
		w.Log.WarnContext(ctx, "worker: purge for a live user ignored", "user_id", uid)
		return nil
	}

	insts, err := w.Store.ListUserInstallationIDs(ctx, uid)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if m.InstallationID != "" && !slices.Contains(insts, m.InstallationID) {
		insts = append(insts, m.InstallationID)
	}
	for _, id := range insts {
		inst, err := w.Store.GetInstallation(ctx, id)
		if err == nil && inst.UserID != uid {
			continue
		}
		if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
			return err
		}
		if err := w.purgePartition(ctx, store.InstPK(id)); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	devices, err := w.Store.ListDevices(ctx, uid)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, d := range devices {
		if err := w.Store.DeleteDeviceToken(ctx, d.TokenHMAC); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if prof != nil {
		if err := w.Store.DeleteNotionUserLink(ctx, prof.NotionUserID, uid); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return w.purgePartition(ctx, store.UserPK(uid))
}

func (w *Worker) purgePartition(ctx context.Context, pk string) error {
	keys, err := w.Store.PartitionKeys(ctx, pk)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.Store.BatchDelete(ctx, keys)
}
```

In `backend/internal/worker/worker.go`'s `New`, add to the handlers map:

```go

```

`backend/internal/e2e/e2e_test.go`:

```go
// Package e2e_test drives API → queue → worker → fake Notion, the local stand-in for T32.
package e2e_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/url"
	"reflect"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/events"
	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/crypto"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/httpapi"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/notion/notiontest"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store/storetest"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/worker"
)

const extID = "abcdefghijklmnopabcdefghijklmnop"

type noVisibility struct{}

func (noVisibility) ChangeVisibility(context.Context, string, time.Duration) error { return nil }

func call(t *testing.T, e *echo.Echo, method, target string, body any, token string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	t.Helper()
	var rdr io.Reader
	if body != nil {
		raw, _ := json.Marshal(body)
		rdr = bytes.NewReader(raw)
	}
	req := httptest.NewRequest(method, target, rdr)
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	if token != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
	}
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	e.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	return rec
}

// deliver feeds every queued message to the worker until the queue is empty.
func deliver(t *testing.T, wk *worker.Worker, q *queue.Recorder) {
	t.Helper()
	for round := 0; round < 5; round++ {
		sent := q.Drain()
		if len(sent) == 0 {
			return
		}
		for i, o := range sent {
			body, _ := json.Marshal(o.Message)
			resp, err := wk.HandleSQS(context.Background(), events.SQSEvent{Records: []events.SQSMessage{{MessageId: strconv.Itoa(i), Body: string(body)}}})
			if err != nil || len(resp.BatchItemFailures) > 0 {
				t.Fatalf("worker failed on %s: %v %+v", body, err, resp)
			}
		}
	}
	t.Fatal("queue did not drain")
}

func segment(id string, start time.Time, secs int, progress float64, seq int) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{"id": id, "session_id": "sess-" + strconv.Itoa(seq/10), "seq": seq % 10,
		"canonical_url": "https://medium.com/p/8f3c2a1b9d4e", "started_at": start.Format(time.RFC3339),
		"ended_at": start.Add(time.Duration(secs) * time.Second).Format(time.RFC3339), "active_seconds": secs, "max_progress": progress}
}

func TestReadArticleEndToEnd(t *testing.T) {
	db := storetest.New(t)
	ns := notiontest.New(t)
	tpl := ns.AddTemplate("tpl-1")
	q := &queue.Recorder{}
	now := time.Date(2026, 10, 6, 3, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	clock := func() time.Time { return now }
	vars := map[string]string{
		"APP_ENV": "test", "TABLE_NAME": db.Table, "QUEUE_URL": "local", "NOTION_CLIENT_ID": "cid", "NOTION_CLIENT_SECRET": "cs",
		"NOTION_VERSION": "2025-09-03", "NOTION_API_BASE": ns.URL, "ALLOWED_EXTENSION_IDS": extID, "DEBOUNCE_BUCKET_SECONDS": "120",
		"TOKEN_ENC_KEYS":      "v1:" + base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bytes.Repeat([]byte{1}, 32)),
		"DEVICE_TOKEN_PEPPER": base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bytes.Repeat([]byte{2}, 32)),
	}
	cfg, err := config.Load(config.RoleAPI, func(k string) string { return vars[k] })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	log := slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	nc := &notion.Client{BaseURL: ns.URL, Version: cfg.NotionVersion, ClientID: cfg.NotionClientID, ClientSecret: cfg.NotionClientSecret}
	api := httpapi.New(httpapi.Deps{Config: cfg, Store: db.Store, Notion: nc, Queue: q, Now: clock, Log: log})
	wk := worker.New(worker.Deps{Store: db.Store, Notion: nc, Keys: cfg.Keys, Queue: q, Visibility: noVisibility{},
		Tracking: cfg.Public.Tracking, Now: clock, Log: log})

	// 1. Connect Notion, accepting the template (scenario 1).
	ns.SetTokenResponse(map[string]any{"access_token": "at", "refresh_token": "rt", "bot_id": "bot-e2e", "workspace_id": "ws",
		"workspace_name": "WS", "duplicated_template_id": "tpl-1", "owner": map[string]any{"type": "user", "user": map[string]any{"id": "nu", "name": "Tuan"}}})
	verifier := strings.Repeat("v", 64)
	ext := "https://" + extID + ".chromiumapp.org/"
	start := call(t, api, "GET", "/v1/oauth/notion/start?"+url.Values{"ext_redirect": {ext}, "code_challenge": {crypto.S256Challenge(verifier)}, "code_challenge_method": {"S256"}}.Encode(), nil, "")
	loc, _ := url.Parse(start.Header().Get("Location"))
	cb := call(t, api, "GET", "/v1/oauth/notion/callback?code=c&state="+loc.Query().Get("state"), nil, "")
	loc, _ = url.Parse(cb.Header().Get("Location"))
	tokRec := call(t, api, "POST", "/v1/auth/token", map[string]string{"code": loc.Query().Get("code"), "code_verifier": verifier, "device_name": "e2e"}, "")
	var tok struct {
		DeviceToken string `json:"device_token"`
	}
	json.Unmarshal(tokRec.Body.Bytes(), &tok)
	if tok.DeviceToken == "" {
		t.Fatalf("no device token: %d %s", tokRec.Code, tokRec.Body)
	}

	// 2. Read for 3 minutes (three 60 s segments).
	article := map[string]any{"canonical_url": "https://medium.com/p/8f3c2a1b9d4e", "source": "medium", "title": "Designing Idempotent APIs",
		"author": "Jane Doe", "site_name": "Better Programming", "word_count": 1820}
	t0 := now.Add(-5 * time.Minute)
	push := map[string]any{"tz": "Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh", "articles": []any{article}, "segments": []any{
		segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", t0, 60, 0.2, 0),
		segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002", t0.Add(time.Minute), 60, 0.4, 1),
		segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003", t0.Add(2*time.Minute), 60, 0.6, 2),
	}}
	if rec := call(t, api, "POST", "/v1/sync/push", push, tok.DeviceToken); rec.Code != 200 || !strings.Contains(rec.Body.String(), `"retry":[]`) {
		t.Fatalf("push %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	deliver(t, wk, q)

	lib := ns.Pages(tpl.LibraryDS)
	daily := ns.Pages(tpl.DailyDS)
	minutes := func(p *notiontest.Page, col string) any {
		return p.Properties[notiontest.PropID(col)].(map[string]any)["number"]
	}
	if len(lib) != 1 || minutes(lib[0], "Reading time (min)") != 3.0 || minutes(lib[0], "Progress") != 0.6 {
		t.Fatalf("library pages %+v", lib)
	}
	if len(daily) != 1 || minutes(daily[0], "Minutes") != 3.0 {
		t.Fatalf("daily pages %+v", daily)
	}
	rel := daily[0].Properties[notiontest.PropID("Articles")].(map[string]any)["relation"].([]any)
	if len(rel) != 1 || rel[0].(map[string]any)["id"] != lib[0].ID {
		t.Fatalf("relation %v", rel)
	}

	// 3. The user curates in Notion, then reads again: curation kept, metrics updated (T32 AC).
	curated := map[string]any{"status": map[string]any{"name": "Reading"}}
	ns.SetPageProperty(lib[0].ID, notiontest.PropID("Status"), curated)
	now = now.Add(time.Hour)
	push["segments"] = []any{segment("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004", now.Add(-2*time.Minute), 60, 0.7, 10)}
	call(t, api, "POST", "/v1/sync/push", push, tok.DeviceToken)
	deliver(t, wk, q)
	if minutes(lib[0], "Reading time (min)") != 4.0 || minutes(lib[0], "Sessions") != 2.0 {
		t.Fatalf("metrics not updated: %+v", lib[0].Properties)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(lib[0].Properties[notiontest.PropID("Status")], curated) {
		t.Fatal("user-owned Status changed")
	}
	if n := len(ns.Pages(tpl.LibraryDS)); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d library pages after second read", n)
	}

	// 4. The status endpoint exposes the Notion URL.
	st := call(t, api, "POST", "/v1/articles/status", map[string]any{"canonical_urls": []string{"https://medium.com/p/8f3c2a1b9d4e"}}, tok.DeviceToken)
	if !strings.Contains(st.Body.String(), lib[0].URL) {
		t.Fatalf("status %s", st.Body)
	}
}
```

Add to `backend/internal/app/app.go`, with imports `"reading-tracker/backend/internal/worker"`:

```go
// Worker assembles the notion-writer: paced Notion client (C22) and the shared keyring.
func Worker(cfg *config.Config, st *store.Store, q queue.Sender, vis worker.VisibilityChanger, log *slog.Logger) *worker.Worker {
	return worker.New(worker.Deps{
		Store: st, Notion: NotionClient(cfg, notion.NewMinInterval(350*time.Millisecond)), Keys: cfg.Keys,
		Queue: q, Visibility: vis, Tracking: cfg.Public.Tracking, Now: time.Now, Log: log,
	})
}
```

`backend/cmd/worker/main.go`:

```go
// Command worker is the notion-writer Lambda (SQS FIFO event source, ReportBatchItemFailures).
package main

import (
	"context"
	"os"

	"github.com/aws/aws-lambda-go/lambda"

	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/app"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/config"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/queue"
	"reading-tracker/backend/internal/store"
)

func main() {
	log := app.Logger()
	cfg, err := config.Load(config.RoleWorker, os.Getenv)
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("config", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	awsCfg, err := app.AWSConfig(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("aws config", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	q := &queue.SQS{API: app.SQS(awsCfg), QueueURL: cfg.QueueURL}
	w := app.Worker(cfg, store.New(app.DynamoDB(awsCfg), cfg.TableName), q, q, log)
	lambda.Start(w.HandleSQS)
}
```
